# WappFlow — Product Maturity Audit

> Generated 2026-07-01 from a multi-agent audit of the live codebase (backend Express + better-sqlite3, web Next.js, desktop Electron). **Audit only — no code was changed.** Implementation is gated on your approval.

**Scope:** 22/22 survey agents (14 modules + 8 cross-cutting dimensions) -> consolidated into 14 themes -> 3 findings adversarially pruned as false-positives -> **221 confirmed findings** (7 critical, 87 high, 95 medium, 32 low).

## 1. Executive summary & maturity verdict

WappFlow has won the feature race and lost the cohesion race. The audit surfaced ~230 live findings across 14 themes, and the pattern is unambiguous: nearly every module exists and mostly works in isolation, but the product does not behave like one system. The same lead renders with different status colors on six screens; the same destructive action is recoverable in one module and permanent in another; the same artifact (a reel, a signed contract, a won deal) reaches a dead end instead of propagating to the next object. The engineering was additive and fast, which is why there are three reel engines, two album editors, two schedulers, two trash bins, two paths to "paid," and four app shells — each shipped as the quickest way to land a feature, none reconciled afterward.

The core thesis of this phase is a deliberate inversion: stop adding surface area, start adding connective tissue. The highest-leverage work is not new features — it is a small set of shared foundations (a design-system primitive layer, one app shell with in-app navigation, a contact-centric data graph with handoffs, platform safety primitives of recycle-bin/undo/audit, and a unified notification/SSE/search bus). The root-cause analysis is decisive here: eight structural roots account for the overwhelming majority of findings, and each foundation dissolves an entire class of symptoms rather than patching them one at a time. Building the Badge/Modal/EmptyState/Toast primitives once closes ~22 design-system findings and silently fixes the per-module styling drift everywhere those primitives get adopted.

There is also a sharp integrity spine that cannot wait for the "polish" framing: unauthenticated WhatsApp endpoints returning global state, an unverified Stripe webhook, user- vs workspace-scoping leaks, a Mark-as-Paid path that bypasses the money rail with no record, and missing idempotency. These are security and money-correctness defects, not UX papercuts, and they are sequenced early alongside the foundations because they are cheap relative to their blast radius and because later integration work (store->payment, booking->deposit) will ride on these rails.

Finally, a meaningful slice of the backlog is phantom: dead tiers (free/starter/growth) contradicting the live entitlements engine, PKR amounts rendered with a $, PlanBanner/PlanChip/PlanWelcomeModal built for removed tiers and never mounted, an Empty-Trash button that throws, Gallery Expiry with a schema column nobody reads, a /settings?tab=billing link to a tab that does not exist. Deleting and reconciling dead code is the cheapest maturity we can buy and is front-loaded accordingly. The producer's named features (Universal Search, Global Notification Center, Universal Timeline, Recycle Bin, Studio/Creator/Video Intelligence, Health Dashboard) are real and desirable — but they are slotted strictly after the foundations they depend on, so each lands as a thin layer over shared infra rather than yet another bespoke subsystem.

### Maturity verdict
WappFlow is roughly one focused quarter of foundational work away from feeling like "one operating system" — the components exist; the connective tissue does not. The product today reads as a strong suite of separate apps sharing a login, not a single OS. Three things are most responsible for the gap. First, four divergent app shells with new-tab module switching instead of a single layout.js with in-app cross-module deep links — this single architectural choice is the direct cause of the "not one product" feeling, the broken handoffs, and the orphaned navigation. Second, the absence of a contact-centric data graph: winning a lead, signing a contract, finishing a reel, or taking a booking never propagates to the client/invoice/project/portfolio/timeline, and permanent deletes orphan related rows — so the contact, which should be the spine of the whole system, is instead a dead-end record in every module. Third, no shared primitive layer (design-system components plus the safety triad of recycle-bin/undo/audit and a unified notification/SSE bus) — which is why the same object looks and behaves differently on every screen and why "is this action safe?" has a different answer in each module. Fix those three and the product crosses the line from "a suite" to "an OS"; nearly everything else is then incremental polish that reuses the foundation.

### Quick wins (first week — small effort, high impact)
- Verify Stripe webhook signatures and add checkout payment-id idempotency (invoices-store-13/14) — closes a money-integrity and double-charge hole in under a day.
- Auth + workspace-scope the WhatsApp status/disconnect endpoints (platform-1) — stops a cross-tenant global-state leak.
- Delete dead plan UI and fix the throwing Empty Trash button and the /settings?tab=billing blank page (platform-2/3/4, settings-team-1) — removes phantom UI that actively misleads.
- Reconcile dead tiers and PKR-as-$ across CC/Settings/plan widgets (command-center-3/4, settings-team-2) — stops the live engine being contradicted on screen.
- Mount the impersonation banner with an exit path (command-center-1) — critical safety affordance for cross-tenant ops.
- Honor /chat?channel=X deep-links and rename the 'Inbox' nav entry (comms-1, comms-4) — calls/mentions stop opening the wrong channel.
- Stop the NavBar refetching all leads every 60s and rebuild PlanProvider's memo (frontend-perf-1/platform-8, frontend-perf-3) — app-wide perf with tiny diffs.
- Single lead-status color source + a Badge primitive's first adopters (design-system-1) — instantly de-divergences the most-seen object.
- Surface the unified portal link on lead/client detail and auto-share at conversion (integration-3, client-portal-8) — exposes the best cohesion feature for near-zero cost.
- Workspace-scope invoice queries (crm-clients-9) — team-created invoices stop disappearing.

### Structural bets (foundational investments)
- Design-system primitive layer (Badge/Modal/Toast/Input/EmptyState/Loading + tokenized status/radii/type): build once, adopt everywhere — dissolves ~22 design-system findings and the entire per-module styling/confirm/empty/loading tail, and makes every later phase cheaper because it reuses the components.
- Single app shell (layout.js) with in-app cross-module deep links + error/not-found/loading boundaries: the canvas for navigation, badges, and search; directly cures the 'not one product' feeling and the orphaned-navigation findings.
- Contact-centric data graph + Universal Timeline/Relationship Graph: makes the contact the spine so won leads, signed contracts, finished reels, and bookings propagate to client/invoice/project/portfolio/timeline — the heart of the critical handoff theme and the basis for Creator Brain and real analytics.
- Platform safety net (one Recycle Bin + Undo + Audit, with cascade-or-guard deletes): collapses every destructive-action finding into one adopted primitive and unlocks safe bulk actions and the named Draft/Undo features.
- Unified real-time + notification bus (one SSE connection, full cross-module event coverage, one bell): turns SSE/notification inconsistency into solved infra and is the home for the Global Notification Center, comms badges, and due-time follow-ups.
- Shared list infrastructure (pagination/virtualization/saved views/bulk/search/caching) over an indexed, pragma-tuned DB: the common cure for god-pages and backend data-layer perf, and the delivery vehicle for the named Saved Views and Bulk Actions features.
- Subsystem consolidation to one canonical implementation each (reel engine, album editor, scheduler, invoice template, AI FAB): removes a whole class of divergence and is the precondition for wiring Video/Studio Intelligence and clean handoffs into a single surface.
- Money + auth integrity spine (verified webhooks, idempotency, workspace-scoping, one reconciled ledger): the rail every payment/store/booking handoff rides on — cheap now, prohibitively expensive to retrofit after integration is built on top of it.

## 2. Structural root causes & themes

**Root causes** — fix these and many findings dissolve:
- No shared design-system / component layer: status colors, badges, modals, inputs, toasts, empty/loading states, radii and typography are hand-rolled per module — fixing this dissolves nearly all design-system-* and the per-page styling/confirm findings.
- Four divergent app shells with new-tab module switching instead of a single layout.js + cross-module deep links — the root cause of the navigation, broken-handoff, and 'not one product' findings.
- No contact-centric data graph / handoff layer: winning a lead, signing a contract, finishing a reel, taking a booking never propagate to client/invoice/project/portfolio/timeline, and deletes orphan rows.
- Missing platform-wide safety primitives (unified recycle bin + undo + audit log) and a unified notification/SSE bus — many destructive-action and real-time/notification findings are the same two absent foundations.
- Duplicated parallel subsystems shipped instead of one canonical implementation (3 reel engines, 2 album editors, 2 schedulers, 2 invoice templates, multiple AI FABs) — consolidation removes a whole class of divergence findings.
- Stale plan/pricing/currency model not reconciled with the live entitlements engine — dead tiers and PKR-as-$ propagate dead UI across CC, settings, and plan widgets.
- Lists/pages built without shared list infrastructure (pagination, virtualization, saved views, bulk actions, caching) and DB without hot-path indexes/pragmas — the common root of both frontend god-page and backend data-layer performance findings.
- Money rail and auth-scoping not enforced end-to-end (unverified webhooks, unauthenticated WhatsApp endpoints, user- vs workspace-scoping, Mark-as-Paid bypass, no idempotency) — the security/integrity spine.

### No shared design system / component library
Status colors, badges, modals, inputs, toasts, empty states, loading states, radii, and typography are hand-rolled per page/module with no shared primitives, so the same object looks different on every screen and 17+ modals/native dialogs reimplement the same logic.

### Fragmented navigation & four disconnected shells
Modules open in new browser tabs with divergent chrome instead of one in-app shell with layout.js; back/active-nav states are wrong or missing, breadcrumbs absent, Settings/Help/Trash are scattered, and nav items land on the wrong screen.

### Broken module handoffs around a contact
Winning a lead doesn't create a client, leads/clients have no New Contract/Shoot/Portal actions, bookings/contracts/reels/galleries don't flow into invoices/projects/portfolio/timeline, and permanent deletes orphan related rows — the modules don't connect around a contact.

### Unbranded, fragmented public client journey
Public client-facing surfaces (gallery, portal, pay, shop, booking) carry zero studio branding, preview as generic WappFlow, aren't truly unified, leave success states as dead ends, and the portal link is buried and never auto-shared.

### Missing recycle-bin / undo / audit safety net
Destructive actions across contracts, invoices, galleries, bookings, settings, and reels are permanent hard-deletes with no recycle bin or undo; the two existing trash bins are inconsistent; audit logging and restore are missing.

### Saved views, bulk actions, list parity & discoverability gaps
Lists lack shared filter/sort/search/saved-views/bulk-action parity (Clients vs Leads, galleries, settings, team, library), saved views are per-browser localStorage, and powerful features (Print Store, Reels, Bookings, Portal, command palette, plan visibility) are undiscoverable or absent from global search.

### Duplicated / divergent subsystems within a module
Multiple overlapping implementations of the same capability inside one module: three reel-generation systems & two editors, two album editors, two scheduling systems, two AI surfaces, two invoice PDF templates, two status maps, split workspace admin, three FABs/copilots.

### Phantom / dead / broken features
Features that are wired up but non-functional or unmounted: gallery expiry never set, email-invoice has no backend, overdue never computed, suspend not enforced, impersonation banner never mounts, dead plan modals/banners, broken billing tab, missing forgot-password, mentions inbox with no UI, Story-Engine editor with no export, active-call with no join.

### Notifications, SSE & real-time inconsistency
Notification feed misses cross-module events and badges, deep-links are ignored, mentions never reach the bell, render/view/decline events produce no persistent notification, and many surfaces poll instead of consuming already-emitted SSE (doubling connections).

### God-pages, unbounded fetches & client-side data handling
Single thousand-line pages with dozens of tabs, lists fetched whole with no pagination/virtualization/LIMIT, full-table refetches and fan-out per-row calls, no caching, context rebuilt every render — hurting perf and maintainability.

### Backend data-layer performance (indexes, queries, pragmas)
Auth runs 4 DB queries per request, N+1/correlated subqueries over large tables, missing indexes on hot columns, no busy_timeout/synchronous pragmas, per-event member queries, and revenue summed in JS.

### Security, auth-scoping & money-rail integrity
Unauthenticated/global WhatsApp endpoints, unverified Stripe webhook, user_id- not workspace_id-scoped queries, manual Mark-as-Paid bypassing the payments rail, uncollected store orders, no checkout idempotency, free-text permission keys, anyone-can-delete channels.

### Stale plan/pricing vocabulary & currency
Dead tiers (free/starter/growth) referenced across CC, settings overlays, plan modals/banners, and tier aliases that contradict the live creator/studio/studio_plus pricing engine; PKR amounts rendered with a $; inconsistent upgrade destinations.

### Weak guidance, terminology & first-run affordances
Next-best-action only lives on the list not the detail, comms module is mislabeled 'Inbox' with inconsistent naming, voice notes render as downloads, mentions resolved by fragile regex, command center health/risk not connected to actions, profile/security thin, currency tabs lose edits.

> Consolidation merged 20 duplicate findings across 13 groups.

## 3. Deliverable 1 — Complete product audit (module by module)

### CRM — Leads & Pipeline
The Leads module is the core CRM surface: a single list page (leads-list/page.js) renders every lead in a table with status tabs, search, sort, assignment/date/tag filters, localStorage saved views, a heuristic "Next best actions" queue, bulk actions (assign, round-robin, move-to-stage, WhatsApp group, export, move-to-clients, trash), CSV export, and reactive duplicate merging. The 2,969-line lead detail page (leads/[id]/page.js) combines an inline-editable profile, a clickable pipeline stepper, a full multi-platform chat composer (presets/AI tools/emoji/voice), and 11 tabs (Timeline, Notes, Reminders, History, Invoices, Emails, Email Flow, Related, Team Room, AI Assistant, Industry AI). The Express/better-sqlite3 backend exposes lead/tag/note/reminder/timeline/duplicate/merge routes that are reasonably optimized (attachTags is single-query de-N+1'd) and emit SSE + notifications on create. The workflow from capture to conversion is feature-rich but suffers from cohesion gaps: a Tailwind-themed add modal in an otherwise CSS-variable app, three overlapping activity views, manual-refresh feeds, sequential bulk loops, and follow-up/duplicate features that exist but are not surfaced where users would discover or act on them.

**Workflow:** End-to-end capture → qualify → pipeline → follow-up → convert is broadly complete but has friction at every handoff. CAPTURE: manual add (AddLeadModal) only collects name/phone/status/value/first-message — it cannot set email, lead_source, address, assignee, or tags at creation even though the detail page and the export support all of them, forcing an immediate second trip into the detail page to finish the record. The backend returns existing_id on duplicate-phone create (server.js:1842) but the modal discards it and shows a dead-end error string instead of "View existing lead." QUALIFY: status is changeable inline (list bulk dropdown, detail stepper) and AI scoring/sentiment/urgency render nicely, but AI analysis only runs on a manual click in the AI tab — there is no automatic qualification prompt. PIPELINE: there is NO board/kanban view at all; the "pipeline" is a clickable horizontal stepper on the detail page plus status pills on the list — dragging a lead between stages is impossible, and the prompt's "pipeline board/list UX" is list-only. FOLLOW-UP: reminders exist (per-lead tab + SidePanel agenda) and a heuristic next-action queue exists on the list, but there are no sequences, no due-reminder notifications (the /api/reminders/upcoming query has no time window despite its "due within 48h" comment, server.js:2097-2108), and the AI "Recommended Next Action" is buried in the AI tab rather than pinned to the lead header. CONVERT: Won/Lost modals and Move-to-Clients are solid and reversible. The biggest workflow drag is the bulk "Move to stage" and "Move to Clients" actions firing one HTTP request per lead in a sequential await loop with no progress UI (leads-list/page.js:970-979, 996-1002) while a batched bulk-trash endpoint already proves the pattern.

**"Where am I / what can I do / what next" test:** LEADS LIST (leads-list/page.js): PASS on "Where am I?" (header title + count badge + status tabs with counts) and "What can I do?" (visible toolbar, bulk bar, filters). Strong on "What should I do next?" thanks to the "Next best actions" queue (lines 926-958) — this is the module's best UX feature. LEAD DETAIL (leads/[id]/page.js): PASS on "Where am I?" (sticky header with name + status pill, pipeline stepper) and "What can I do?" (quick-action buttons + 11 tabs). FAILS "What should I do next?": the page opens on the Notes tab with no surfaced next step; the heuristic next-action and the AI next_action are not shown in the header, so a rep landing here has no guidance on whether this lead is overdue or what the recommended move is. The 11-tab strip is also cognitively heavy and three of those tabs (Timeline, History, plus the Activity stats card) show overlapping data. TIMELINE TAB: FAILS "Where am I?"/"What should I do next?" because it loads empty with the message "Click Refresh Timeline to load all activity" (lines 2656-2666) — a feed that requires a manual click to populate reads as broken to a first-time user even though the backend assembles it server-side in one call (server.js:4791-4818).

**Standards present:** Bulk Actions (assign, round-robin, move-to-stage, WhatsApp group, export, move-to-clients, trash), Saved Views (localStorage, leads-list/page.js:42-44, 794-812), Recycle Bin / Soft delete (is_deleted + 90-day trash, /api/leads/trash, bulk-trash), Exports (CSV of all or selected leads), Imports (POST /api/leads/bulk-upload with dedupe + plan cap), Activity Timeline (per-lead Timeline + History tabs; server.js:4791), Plan Enforcement (lead limit gates on create/import + at-limit Upgrade button, leads-list/page.js:901-919), Permissions (role='user' restricted to assigned leads, server.js:1504), Global Notifications (notify() + SSE + push on lead create; NavBar feed), Duplicate Detection (reactive merge modal + create-time exact-phone block), Responsive Design (r-* utility classes, r-tw/r-wrap/r-scroll-x), Tags/Badges, Loading/Empty/Error/Success states, Toasts
**Standards missing:** Pipeline Kanban/Board view (no drag-and-drop board exists anywhere — list-only), Universal/Global Search (NavBar has no search box; search is per-page only), Command Palette / Keyboard Shortcuts (none in the module), Drafts (AddLeadModal + composer lose all input on close/navigation), Undo (bulk trash/move-to-stage/move-to-clients have no inline undo — only the Trash page), Follow-up Sequences / cadences (only one-off manual reminders; no automated next-action scheduling), Due-reminder notifications (reminders are a passive list; nothing alerts when one is due), Saved Views server-side sharing (views are per-browser localStorage, not shared across team or devices), Feature Discovery for Merge-duplicates / Round-robin / WhatsApp-group (no tooltips, onboarding, or empty-state hints surface these), Audit Logging consistency (bulk-assign and bulk-trash do not call logAudit, unlike create/merge/bulk-upload), Analytics entry point from the list (no per-status conversion stats beyond raw counts)

**Receives from:** WhatsApp/Instagram/Facebook/Website inbound → auto-creates leads with platform_source + platform_account_id (account chip rendered in list/detail), CSV import → /api/leads/bulk-upload, Manual entry → AddLeadModal, Tags created in Settings flow into the TagPicker and tag-filter pills, Team/workspace members feed assignment dropdowns + round-robin, SSE new_message / lead_updated streams keep the detail thread and dashboards live, Email receiving (leadEmailsAPI.pollNow) folds inbound email into the lead
**Should hand off to:** Won lead → Clients (is_client toggle, reversible) — handoff present and good, Lead → Invoices (InvoiceModal) and → Media Studio shoots (LeadStudioSection) — present, Lead → Email Workflows, scheduled Meetings, WhatsApp Groups — present, MISSING: Lead → Contracts Studio (no 'Create contract' action on a Won lead despite Contracts being a core module), MISSING: Lead → Booking (no 'Create booking' handoff from the lead), MISSING: Pipeline → Reports/Analytics deep-link (status tabs don't link to conversion analytics), WEAK: AI next_action and heuristic next-action do not flow OUT to the SidePanel Tasks or to reminders — the user must retype them

_16 findings — see appendix._

### CRM — Clients & Contacts
"Clients" is not a separate entity — it is a boolean flag (is_client) on the leads table. A won lead becomes a client via a manual "Move to Clients" button, which sets is_client=1 and hides it from the Leads list while keeping it in chat/analytics (server.js:1892-1909). The Clients page (clients/page.js, 141 lines) is a thin read-only card grid showing name/phone/deal-value/client-since with search + "move back to leads". There is no dedicated client detail route — clicking "Open" reuses the lead detail page (/leads/[id]), which acts as the de-facto customer-360 but aggregates only invoices, emails, notes, reminders, history and (client-side) studio shoots — NOT contracts or bookings, even though both link to lead_id.

**Workflow:** The core lead→client lifecycle works and is reversible, but the workflow is incomplete and inconsistent. (1) The conversion is a two-step manual chore: marking a lead "Closed - Won" (WonModal, leads/[id]/page.js:220) does NOT offer to move it to Clients — the user must separately click the "Move to Clients" button (leads/[id]/page.js:1458). Status="Closed - Won" and is_client are fully decoupled in the backend (status route at server.js:1911 never touches is_client), so a studio can have dozens of won deals that never appear in Clients, silently. (2) The Clients page is a dead end for action: from a client card you can only Open, open chat (which is the SAME route as Open — both buttons at clients/page.js:129-130 go to /leads/${c.id}), or move back to Leads. You cannot create an invoice, send a contract, book a session, or start a new shoot from a client — every one of those forces a jump into the lead detail page or another module. (3) There is no per-client rollup of contracts or bookings anywhere — the two modules that DO carry lead_id (contracts/page.js, bookings/page.js:129) are invisible from the client's profile. The promised "single place to see everything about a client" does not fully exist: it's everything-except-contracts-and-bookings, and contracts/invoices/shoots are reached through three different UI affordances (a tab, a custom right-rail section, nothing).

**"Where am I / what can I do / what next" test:** Clients list (clients/page.js) — (1) Where am I? OK: clear header + "Clients" eyebrow. (2) What can I do? WEAK: only search + open + move-back; no create-invoice/contract/booking, no filters, no bulk, no sort. (3) What next? FAIL: no segmentation (active vs dormant), no next-best-action, no "X clients haven't booked in 90 days" — nothing tells the studio which client to engage. Client detail (reuses /leads/[id]) — (1) Where am I? PARTIAL: the page is branded "Lead", with a pipeline stepper and Won/Lost buttons still shown even after the person is a client (leads/[id]/page.js:1450-1456) — it never re-frames itself as a client. (2) What can I do? OK-ish but scattered across 11 tabs. (3) What next? FAIL for client context: contracts and bookings are absent, so you can't see "contract signed, deposit unpaid, shoot next week" in one glance. Invoices list (invoices/page.js) — (1) Where am I? OK. (2) What can I do? FAIL on create: there is NO "New invoice" button anywhere on the page; the empty state literally says "Create invoices from any lead profile" (invoices/page.js:491), forcing a module jump to make the module's primary object. (3) What next? PARTIAL: stat cards exist but "Overdue" is computed from a status that nothing sets automatically.

**Standards present:** Plan Enforcement (lead create/bulk gated via pricing.canCreate/checkLimit — server.js:1846, 1554), Audit Logging (client_toggle + status_change logged — server.js:1906, 1931), Activity Timeline (contact_history written on client move, status change, assignment — server.js:1899, 1928; surfaced in History tab), Undo / reversibility (Move to Clients is fully reversible from both Clients page and lead detail — clients/page.js:45, leads/[id]/page.js:1467), Empty states (Clients + Invoices both have designed empty states), Toasts / success feedback (clients/page.js:139, invoices/page.js:416), Responsive layout (auto-fill card grid on Clients; r-scroll-x / r-modal classes on Invoices), Workspace Branding (company logo/name flow into invoice document — invoices/page.js:39, 161), Confirm dialog for destructive invoice actions (useConfirm — invoices/page.js:362)
**Standards missing:** Bulk Actions on Clients (Leads list has bulk select/assign/trash/round-robin/merge — leads-list/page.js:705,837; Clients page has zero), Saved Views / Segments on Clients (Leads has wf_lead_views — leads-list/page.js:41-42; Clients has only free-text search), Exports on Clients (Leads has Export CSV — leads-list/page.js:881; Clients has none), Universal Search across clients (no global search in NavBar; AICommandCenter Ctrl+K is AI-query only and has no 'clients' command — AICommandCenter.js:22-29), Recycle Bin for clients (no trash concept distinct from leads), Sort / filter on Clients (no sort by revenue, recency, name; no active/dormant filter), 'New invoice' creation on the Invoices page (must be created from a lead — invoices/page.js:491), Per-client financial rollup (Clients sums lead.actual_sale as 'Lifetime revenue' — clients/page.js:52 — not actual paid invoices), Keyboard shortcuts on Clients/Invoices lists, Feature discovery for the lead→client conversion (nothing prompts it on Won)

**Receives from:** Leads pipeline → Clients: a won lead is manually promoted via is_client flag (server.js:1892), Settings/company → Clients & Invoices: currency symbol + company branding (settingsAPI.getCompany — clients/page.js:36, invoices/page.js:343), Media Studio → lead/client detail: shoots fetched by lead_id and shown in LeadStudioSection (leads/[id]/page.js:548-552), Invoices → lead/client detail: invoices fetched by lead_id (server.js:1816) and shown in the Invoices tab, Bookings → lead: booking carries lead_id with an 'Open lead' link (bookings/page.js:129), Contracts → lead: documents carry lead_id and client_name (contracts/page.js)
**Should hand off to:** Clients → Contracts: MISSING. No way to see or start a contract from a client; client profile has no contracts surface., Clients → Bookings: MISSING. No 'Book a session' action and no list of a client's past/upcoming bookings on the profile., Clients → Invoices: PARTIAL. Invoices show inside lead detail tab, but the Clients card has no 'create invoice' / 'amount outstanding' affordance, and the standalone Invoices page can't filter to one client., Won deal → Client: WEAK handoff. Marking Won does not offer/auto-suggest promotion to Client (leads/[id]/page.js:919-926), so the two states drift apart., Clients → unified customer 360: the lead detail page is the de-facto 360 but omits contracts + bookings (server.js getById at :1794 returns notes/reminders/history/invoices/emailWorkflows only)., Clients → bulk outreach: the Contracts module already has a bulk-send-to-clients flow (contracts/page.js:272-284) that the Clients page itself does not expose.

### Media Studio — Library, Cull & AI
A self-contained creative module (own StudioShell, own ms-* design system, own theme switcher) covering the photographer workflow: create shoot, upload media, cull (full-screen viewer with keyboard/swipe culling, non-destructive edits, AI hero/quality/dup advisory scores), AI selections/albums/reels, build gallery, publish and deliver over WhatsApp. Backend is an additive Express + better-sqlite3 monolith (media-studio.js ~2590 lines) plus a worker that auto-derives CV + composite AI scores on ingest, studio-ai.js generation, and brains.js learning (Creator Brain / house-style). Server-side storage quota gating + threshold warnings exist. The cull screen is genuinely strong; the surrounding library, AI surfacing, and cross-module integration are where the cohesion gaps live.

**Workflow:** The core create->upload->cull->deliver path is complete and mostly fast, and the cull screen is the standout: rich keyboard (P/X/M, 1-5, arrows, Z zoom, C compare, E/F, Shift+C/Shift+V copy-paste edits), touch swipe culling, windowed filmstrip for big shoots, before/after, crop, apply-to-keepers, and a "Gallery from keepers" exit. Composite AI scores are auto-computed on ingest (media-worker.js:342) so they are usually present without manual analyze. Friction concentrates in three areas. (1) The AI recommendation is a dead end: cull "Top picks" (cull/page.js:511-514) spotlights the AI-recommended top N% but offers NO one-click action to mark them all Keep or reject the rest — the photographer still clicks every photo, and the well-built /cull/bulk endpoint + mediaAPI.bulkCull are never called anywhere in the web UI. This undercuts the "faster first pass" promise in the recommendation text itself. (2) Studio AI generation is buried behind a small "Studio AI" ghost button and a modal whose results need re-clicking; the album it opens runs on a duplicate, schema-incompatible ms_albums table (see findings). (3) Cross-module integration is one-way: media events flow TO the CRM lead timeline (emitToLead) but the project page shows the linked client as plain text with no link back to the lead, no booking/contract/invoice handoff, and no storage indicator until a 413 mid-upload. Decisions also save one PUT per photo (cull/page.js:189) with no batching. Net: the module works end-to-end but does not yet feel like one OS — the AI advises but cannot be acted on in bulk, and the module is an island.

**"Where am I / what can I do / what next" test:** CULL SCREEN (cull/page.js) — PASSES all three. Where am I: HUD shows shoot title, filter counts, position N/M. What can I do: dock (Keep/Maybe/Reject), tool rail, Top picks, Gallery CTA. What should I do next: clear (decide, then Gallery when keepers>0). Strong. LIBRARY / PROJECT DETAIL ([id]/page.js) — PARTIAL. Where am I: clear (hero with title/client/counts). What can I do: visible (Upload/Cull/Albums/Reels/Studio AI). What should I do next: WEAK — galleries empty-state says "select photos then create a gallery" but the primary path is Cull; a new user lands here and the strongest next action (Cull) is a secondary ghost button while two AI features (Studio AI, Auto-organize) compete for attention with no ordering. STUDIO HOME (studio/page.js) — PARTIAL. Where am I and What can I do are clear (cinematic stage + wall + search). What should I do next FAILS for a returning user with in-flight shoots: there is no surfacing of "3 shoots awaiting cull" or "gallery has 12 new client favourites" — the home is a portfolio wall, not a work queue, so the next action must be rediscovered each visit.

**Standards present:** Plan Enforcement (storage-enforce.js gate/warn wired into upload at media-studio.js:568), Recycle Bin / soft-delete (30-day Trash: deleted_at, /trash, restore, purgeExpiredTrash), Audit Logging (logAudit on edit, batch, auto_edit, publish, create_from_cull), Bulk Actions (library: watermark/auto-edit/delete on multi-select; backend /cull/bulk and /edits/batch exist), Keyboard Shortcuts (cull screen is comprehensive), Undo (cull copy/paste edits, reset-to-original, decide(null)), Exports (gallery ZIP, album PDF, video export, worker-backed), Activity Timeline integration OUT (emitToLead mirrors media events onto lead timeline), Responsive Design (touch swipe culling, r-* utilities, isTouch handling), Permissions (canManage gate on destructive routes), Workspace search within module (shoots search on home; cull filters)
**Standards missing:** Universal Search (no global search across shoots/assets/galleries/clients; home search is client-side title filter only), Global Notifications (storage warnings notify() exist but no in-Studio notification surface; client favourites/comments/proofing submissions only appear via 20s poll, no badge/feed), Saved Views (no saved cull filters or library views), Drafts (gallery/album are immediate inserts; no draft autosave for the New Shoot / New Gallery modals), Command Palette (none in Studio), Feature Discovery (no tour/empty-state nudges pointing at Cull or Studio AI; powerful features are unlabeled icon buttons), Imports (no CSV/bulk import of shoots or external gallery import), Analytics (no per-shoot or studio-level dashboard: cull throughput, keep-rate, delivery turnaround), Storage usage indicator in Studio UI (endpoint exists, never surfaced; only a 413 mid-upload), Gallery Expiry (column exists, completely unbuilt — see findings)

**Receives from:** CRM leads -> New Shoot modal links a lead_id (studio/page.js:24, NewProjectModal), Uploaded files -> worker ingest pipeline (variants/EXIF/CV scores/composites), style_profiles (brains.js) -> auto-edit house style (media-studio.js:1122), Track-0 ms_asset_scores -> cull intelligence() + Studio AI selections, Print products (ms_print_products) -> client portal store toggle
**Should hand off to:** Media events -> CRM lead activity_timeline (emitToLead) — present but UI has no link BACK to the lead, Published gallery -> WhatsApp delivery via sendClientMessage (present, good), Published gallery -> creator Portfolio (autoIncludeGalleryIntoPortfolio), MISSING: shoot -> Booking/Contract/Invoice handoff (no link from a shoot to its contract, invoice, or booking despite those modules existing), MISSING: cull keepers / Studio AI selection -> direct deliver without the intermediate manual gallery-build step, MISSING: gallery delivery -> task/reminder or NBA in CRM (delivery turnaround is invisible to the rest of the app), MISSING: storage state -> Studio shell (warnings fire to notifications but Studio never reads them)

_11 findings — see appendix._

### Media Studio — Video & Reel
A per-shoot video studio that turns a photo/video project's assets into social reels. The core is a real timeline editor (studio/[id]/video/[timelineId]) over a single JSON EDL (video-engine.js) with clips, Ken Burns/motion + opacity keyframes, transitions, LUTs, per-clip colour, effects, text overlays and one music track, plus a server-side ffmpeg export to platform presets. On top of it sit multiple AI on-ramps: "Creative Packs" templates and score-driven "AI drafts" (both produce editable timelines), a one-click "Auto-reel" render (reel-engine.js), and a separate "Story Engine" reel planner (video-ai.js) surfaced in the Studio AI modal. It reads Track-0 CV scores + cull decisions for ranking and can auto-apply a learned house-style grade. Rendering is the single shared ffmpeg path; AI is advisory and never auto-delivers.

**Workflow:** The hand-built editor flow (project -> Reels list -> New/Template/AI draft -> timeline -> Export) is genuinely complete and polished: debounced autosave, keyboard shortcuts, background render with live progress, inline preview + download. The serious problem is THREE overlapping reel-generation systems that do not converge. (A) media-studio.js Creative Packs + AI drafts + reel-engine.js Auto-reel all build a real ms_timelines EDL that opens in the powerful editor and can export. (B) video-ai.js Story Engine (POST /api/video-ai/projects/:id/reel) writes a SEPARATE ms_reel_plans row, surfaced only in the Studio AI modal on the project page, and opens a DIFFERENT, far weaker editor at /studio/[id]/reel/[reelId] that has reorder/retime/transition dropdowns but NO render/export button and no bridge into the real engine — a dead end despite its own copy claiming "The render uses your existing video engine" (reel/[reelId]/page.js:53). A user who clicks "Generate reel plan" in Studio AI lands in an editor they can never ship from. The Reels list (video/page.js) ALSO exposes Auto-reel + AI drafts + Templates, so the same shoot has two unrelated "AI reel" entry points that produce incompatible artifacts. Beyond that, the editor's render is fire-and-forget: completion has no persistent notification, so closing the export modal loses the only handle to the finished MP4 (no exports library/history surfaced in the UI). Discoverability of the whole module is weak — entry is a single "Reels" ghost button on the project page that only appears once assets exist. Video Intelligence (video-ai.js cull/story) is a thin score-weighting stub disconnected from the editor's clip-level trimming.

**"Where am I / what can I do / what next" test:** Reels list (video/page.js): PASS on "where am I" (clear "Video Studio / Reels · {project}" header) and "what can I do" (Auto-reel, AI drafts, Templates, New, plus a strong empty-state hero). Weak on "what next" — four creation paths (Auto-reel vs AI drafts vs Templates vs Blank) with no guidance on which to pick, and Auto-reel + AI-drafts overlap confusingly. Timeline editor (video/[timelineId]/page.js): PASS on all three — name+save state answer "where am I", the rail/inspector/transport answer "what can I do", and the empty inspector explicitly says "Select a clip to edit / Click media to add" + shows shortcuts. Best screen in the module. Story-Engine reel editor (reel/[reelId]/page.js): FAILS "what should I do next" — it lets you reorder/retime but the only action is "Save reel"; there is no render, no export, no "open in full editor", so the user is stranded with a saved plan and no way to produce a video.

**Standards present:** Drafts (AI drafts + Story Engine plans are non-destructive editable drafts; ms_timelines.source = manual|template|ai_draft), Autosave (debounced 800ms save with saved/saving/dirty states, video/[timelineId]/page.js:113-122), Keyboard Shortcuts (Space/S/Del/arrows in the editor, page.js:295-306), Plan Enforcement at module level (MODULE_GATES gates /api/media, /api/video-ai under media_studio key, server.js:5434-5442), Audit Logging (logAudit on export, apply_template, ai_draft, create, reel_render), Background jobs with progress (ms_jobs + ms_video_exports, worker emits progress), Exports (the entire module IS an export pipeline to MP4 platform presets), Activity Timeline infra exists in media-studio (emitToLead) though not wired to video
**Standards missing:** Global Notifications on render completion (worker only SSE-broadcasts ms_video_export; no persistent notifications row like galleries/bookings get via notify()), Undo/Redo in the timeline editor (destructive split/delete/clear-keyframes with only a per-delete confirm; no history stack), Recycle Bin for timelines (DELETE is hard-delete of ms_timelines + ms_video_exports, media-studio.js:2288-2295; confirm() only), Bulk Actions on the Reels list (delete is one-at-a-time, no multi-select), Saved Views / filters on the Reels list (no filter by source/aspect/status), Command Palette / Universal Search indexing of reels (no global search exists; reels not findable outside the project), Feature Discovery (no tooltips/coachmarks for Auto-reel vs AI drafts vs Templates distinction), Responsive Design for the editor (3-pane rail/stage/inspector + timeline is desktop-only; no mobile block, unlike the rest of the app per the mobile-responsive memo), Per-render Plan Enforcement/metering (export has no quota check or AI-render metering, only the on/off module gate), Exports history surface (no UI list of past renders; only the live modal)

**Receives from:** Media assets (photos/videos) from the shoot via mediaAPI.listAssets — the building blocks (video/[timelineId]/page.js:91), Track-0 CV scores (ms_asset_scores: quality, sharpness, faces, smile, hook, emotion, motion) feed AI drafts + Auto-reel + Story Engine ranking, Cull decisions (ms_cull_decisions keep/reject/rating) bias AI ranking (rankMedia, video-ai-drafts.js:39-55), Studio highlights/best_of selections feed the Story Engine (bestPhotoSelection, video-ai.js:113-119), Learned house-style profile (style_profiles) auto-grades Auto-reel clips (reel-engine.js:157-172), Audio assets uploaded per-project (ms_assets type=audio) for the music track, project_type drives template/style recommendations (REC map, niche templates)
**Should hand off to:** Portfolio — a finished reel SHOULD be addable to the public portfolio (ms_portfolio_items supports kind=video) but there is NO handoff from a render to the portfolio, Galleries/Delivery — rendered MP4 lives in ms_video_exports with no path to attach to a client gallery or delivery (galleries deliver photos only); the reel is a download-only dead end, Lead activity timeline — emitToLead exists for galleries but a reel render emits no lead activity, so 'we made your highlight reel' never appears on the client record, Global notifications — render-done does not notify the user, Contracts/Booking — no cross-module link (e.g. deliver reel as part of a package), Social scheduling / direct publish to IG/TikTok — presets target these platforms but output is manual download only (acceptable for now, but the obvious next handoff)

### Galleries, Albums & Portfolio (client delivery)
This module covers the full client-delivery surface: published delivery galleries (g/[token]) with favoriting, comments, story sections, slideshow, ZIP download, proofing/selection rounds, and a print-store handoff; two separate album-design tools (a print-PDF page/slot designer at studio/[id]/albums and an AI spread/grid editor at studio/[id]/album); and a public vanity portfolio (folio/[handle]) with 10 themes, drag-reorder, auto-feed from published galleries, and WhatsApp/link sharing. Photographers create and publish galleries from the per-shoot page (studio/[id]); the public pages are token/handle-capability based with no auth. The data model is additive SQLite (ms_galleries, ms_albums, ms_portfolios, ms_proofing_sets) with a worker-backed ZIP/PDF export pipeline and watermark burning on the web variant.

**Workflow:** The happy path (upload → create gallery → add photos → publish & send → client favorites/selects → photographer approves) is genuinely complete and well-integrated: publish auto-delivers over WhatsApp, feeds the portfolio, writes audit + activity + notifications, and proofing has real revision rounds. Friction concentrates in three places. (1) Gallery settings are create-once: the CreateGalleryModal sets visibility/password/download_policy, but there is NO edit affordance afterward — the backend PUT /galleries/:id accepts settings but no UI calls it, so changing a password, opening downloads, or fixing a typo'd title is impossible without recreating. (2) The named planned feature Gallery Expiry is entirely absent: the ms_galleries.expires_at column exists (media-studio.js:1262) but is never written, never read, and never enforced in loadPublishedGallery — there is no UI, no API field, and no client-facing "link expired" state (contrast d/[token], the contracts share page, which DOES handle an 'expired' state). (3) Albums are split into two incompatible tools with different data models, routes, and entry points, so "design the album" means two different things depending on where you click. Speed-wise the core flows are fine; the main waste is duplicated polling logic and an N+1 in album load.

**"Where am I / what can I do / what next" test:** Client gallery g/[token]: PASSES. "Where am I?" — branded header with title + photo count; "What can I do?" — favorite/comment/download/slideshow/select are all visible; "What next?" — the sticky proofing banner and "Save selection" CTA guide the client. Strong screen. Photographer shoot page studio/[id] gallery cards: PARTIAL. "Where am I?" and "What can I do?" are clear (Publish, Copy link, Export ZIP, Request selections), but "What next?" leaks implementation — a published card with no share_url tells the user to run "pm2 restart wappflow-api" (page.js:416), and there is no way to edit gallery settings or set an expiry, so the screen silently lacks expected delivery controls. Albums landing studio/[id]/albums: FAILS question 2/3. "What can I do?" is ambiguous because this print-PDF designer is a different tool from the AI spread editor reached via Studio AI ("Open editor →", page.js:721); a user who designed an album in one place cannot find it in the other, and neither album surfaces in the StudioShell nav (only Shoots/Portfolio exist), so albums are essentially undiscoverable except via a per-shoot button.

**Standards present:** Plan Enforcement (storage/upload gating exists in media module; download_policy gates client downloads), Audit Logging (logAudit on publish/export/portfolio_update — media-studio.js:1658,1724,2446), Global Notifications (notify() on client collection/comment — media-studio.js:2670,2693), Activity Timeline (emitToLead + addContactHistory on publish/collection/proofing — media-studio.js:1656,2669,2766), Workspace Branding (portfolio only: accent, avatar, studio name, 10 themes), Responsive Design (r-modal/r-stack/r-full utilities used across album/portfolio/gallery), Bulk Actions (library multi-select → add to gallery; portfolio multi-select picker), Analytics (portfolio view_count; gallery favorite/comment counts surfaced on cards)
**Standards missing:** Gallery Expiry (named planned feature — column exists, zero implementation), Edit/Settings for an existing gallery (no UI to change visibility/password/downloads/title post-create), Saved Views / Filters on the galleries list (no filter, sort, or search across a project's galleries), Recycle Bin / Undo for galleries & portfolio items (gallery delete and removeItem are immediate, no soft-delete; assets have trash but galleries don't), Drafts indicator nuance (gallery 'draft' status exists but no autosave/draft UX on album-from-favorites), Keyboard Shortcuts / Command Palette (none in any delivery screen; portfolio lightbox has arrows but editor has none), Universal Search (galleries/albums/portfolio items are not searchable from global search), Feature Discovery (albums + portfolio + proofing have no first-run hints/tooltips beyond inline copy), Workspace Branding on client gallery (g/[token] is hardcoded gold #c2a878 + 'WappFlow Media Studio' footer — no studio logo/name/accent), Exports of selection data (proofing picks/favorites collections have no CSV/PDF export for the photographer)

**Receives from:** Media Library (studio/[id]) → galleries: selected assets added via addGalleryAssets; cull keepers → galleries/from-cull and album autofill (autofillAlbum 'keep'), Leads/CRM → publish & proofing delivery: project.lead_id resolves customer_phone for WhatsApp send (media-studio.js:1645,1745), Watermark module → gallery export: settings.watermark burned into web variant on ZIP/portal export (startGalleryExport), Print Store → client gallery: store_enabled flag surfaces 'Order prints' button in g/[token] (page.js:202), Folders → gallery story sections: ms_folders drive named chapters in the public gallery (media-studio.js:2614), Published galleries → portfolio candidates + auto_include feed (media-studio.js:2398,2451)
**Should hand off to:** Client favorites/collections → photographer: surfaced as counts on gallery cards + notification, but NO deep link from a collection to 'build album from these' (album-from-favorites endpoint exists at media-studio.js:744 but is not wired to the client collection UI), Proofing submitted selections → album/export: approved selections do not flow into either album editor or a filtered export; photographer must re-pick manually, Portfolio share → CRM: sharePortfolio sends over WhatsApp but does not log a lead activity/contact-history entry the way gallery publish does, Gallery delivery → project milestones: studio-experience milestones (P12) exist but publishing a gallery does not auto-advance a 'Delivered' milestone, Album PDF / gallery ZIP → invoices/store: no handoff to sell the finished album or prints after design

### Contracts Studio
Contracts Studio is a self-contained document module (its own shell, opened from the app-switcher in a NEW browser tab) for turning proposals/contracts/NDAs/SOWs into interactive, block-built documents that clients sign on a tokenized public page. The full lifecycle works end-to-end: create from pack/template/blank/upload, edit in a block builder with 3 themes + AI assist + clause library + version history, send over WhatsApp/email/link with expiry, e-sign (typed name + drawn signature + ESIGN consent + IP/UA/SHA-256 hash), then fire on-sign automations (move pipeline stage, create invoice, spin up a Media Studio project), backed by a Client Vault, Analytics funnel, and Settings. The backend (contracts-studio.js, 1039 lines) is mature and well-factored; the gaps are almost entirely in cross-module integration and a handful of product-standard omissions, not in the core flow.

**Workflow:** The template -> draft -> send -> e-sign -> vault spine is complete and genuinely strong. Send (SendModal, [id]/page.js:537) is a clean two-phase compose/sent flow with channel toggles, expiry, graceful per-channel delivery reporting (sent/no_phone/failed) and a copyable link; plan enforcement gates only NEW sends (backend:873). Signing is legally rigorous (backend:945-981): typed name + drawn signature + consent gate + IP/UA capture + SHA-256 hash + signed-PDF generation. Version snapshots on every send (backend:889-895) with a Compare/Restore UI are a nice touch. Main frictions: (1) The module opens in a SEPARATE browser TAB with its own shell (AppSwitcher.js:58 target:'_blank'; ContractsStudioShell.js) so a user mid-deal context-switches OS-level away from the CRM and back, breaking the one-OS feel. (2) You cannot send from the Overview list — every send forces a jump into the builder, even for an upload-and-sign doc that needs no editing. (3) Overview rows expose only open + delete (page.js:107-117); no duplicate, no per-row send/remind/copy-link, no multi-select bulk actions. (4) Delete is a hard delete with a browser confirm() and no undo/recycle bin (page.js:116; backend:521-524 hard-deletes doc+signers+events+approvals). (5) The Send modal disables WhatsApp/email entirely when no lead is linked (SendModal:555) with no inline way to enter an ad-hoc email/phone, so an un-linked doc can only be link-shared. Overall the workflow is feature-rich but list-level operations and cross-module hand-offs are the weak points.

**"Where am I / what can I do / what next" test:** OVERVIEW (page.js) — PASSES. Where am I: clear (\"Contracts Studio / Overview\" eyebrow+H1, line 67-68). What can I do: stats + filter tabs + New document + Bulk send are obvious. What next: empty state has a single clear CTA (line 99-104). Good screen. BUILDER ([id]/page.js) — PARTIAL. Where am I: title input + Back, fine. What can I do: a 10-icon toolbar (AI, People, Room, Versions, Clauses(¶), Save-as-template, Settings, Preview, Send) — several are bare icons with only title-tooltips (line 86-91), notably the literal \"¶\" button for clauses; first-timers won't know what these do. What next: \"Send\" is clearly the terminal CTA, good. The screen over-indexes on discoverability-by-hover. VAULT (vault/page.js) — PARTIAL. Where am I + What can I do are clear (search + expandable client cards). What should I do next FAILS: it's a read-only filing cabinet — no \"New document for this client\", no bulk export, no way to act; it's a dead-end browse view when it should be a launchpad.

**Standards present:** Analytics (dedicated funnel page: sent->viewed->signed, acceptance rate, revenue, time-to-sign — analytics/page.js, backend:826-850), Plan Enforcement (send gate via pricing.canCreate on contract_sends — backend:873-878), Activity Timeline (per-document events panel in PeopleModal — [id]/page.js:337-348; backend cs_events), Audit Logging (logAudit on send — backend:918), Drafts (documents start as draft; debounced autosave — [id]/page.js:48-55), Version History / snapshots with restore (backend:889-895, 656-668; VersionsModal), Templates + starter Packs + reusable Clause library (backend:617-688), Bulk Send (BulkSendModal — page.js:263; backend cs/bulk-send), Feature Discovery / Help (rich accordion help page — help/page.js), Global Notifications on sign (notify() — backend:979), Workspace Branding (letterhead + sender name + default theme — settings/page.js), Responsive Design (r-modal/r-stack utilities, clamp() sizing, @media in contracts.css)
**Standards missing:** Recycle Bin / Undo (delete is permanent hard-delete — backend:521-524), Bulk Actions on the document list (only bulk-SEND exists; no multi-select delete/status/export on Overview), Universal Search (no workspace-level search includes contracts; the only search is the Command-Center control plane, ControlShell.js:92), Saved Views (filter tabs are fixed: All/Drafts/Awaiting/Signed; no custom saved filters), Exports (no CSV/PDF export of the document list or analytics; signed-PDF is per-doc only), Imports (no bulk import of documents/clauses/templates), Keyboard Shortcuts / Command Palette (none in builder or list), Global Notification on client VIEW and on DECLINE (notify() only fires on sign — backend:979; decline at 985-994 and view at 931-934 emit no notification), Permissions/roles within the module (approval exists but no per-role gating of who can send/delete), Saved Views / pagination on unbounded document + lead lists

**Receives from:** Leads/CRM: a lead can be linked at create time (NewDocModal pulls leadsAPI.getAll), seeding a client signer (backend:474-479), Bulk Send pulls leads to fan-out a pack/template to many clients (page.js:263), Workspace settings: letterhead, sender name, default theme, default expiry flow into each doc (settings/page.js; backend cs_settings), Clause library + Templates + Packs flow INTO the builder, AI engine (ai.callLLM) powers draft/improve/summarize/risks and the client Q&A (backend:998-1017), Sync delta feed includes cs_documents (sync.js:45) for the desktop/offline layer
**Should hand off to:** On sign: updates lead pipeline stage + actual_sale (backend:264-268), creates an invoice from the client's selection (backend:287-297), spins up a Media Studio project (backend:299), and writes a lead contact-history row (backend:916,966) — this is the strongest part of the module, On sign/decline: SSE broadcast cs_signed/cs_updated to the workspace (backend:978,992), Client Vault can copy a Client Portal link (vault/page.js:22-24 via clientPortalAPI), MISSING: the Lead detail page (leads/[id]/page.js, 2969 lines) imports NO csAPI and shows NO contract list/panel — contracts only appear as plain text rows in the contact-history timeline; there is no "Documents" tab on the lead and no "Create contract for this client" CTA, MISSING: the main Dashboard (dashboard/page.js) does not surface contracts at all (no csAPI import) despite signed contracts representing booked revenue, MISSING: no notification/handoff when a client VIEWS or DECLINES a document
