### Booking & Scheduling
A public self-scheduling module: the studio configures services, weekly availability, buffer/blackout rules, and intake questions on /bookings, gets a shareable /book/[slug] link, and clients pick a service + day + time. Each booking finds-or-creates a lead, writes a reminder, logs contact history, fires a workspace notification, and sends a WhatsApp confirmation with a self-serve /booking/manage/[token] link for reschedule/cancel. Backend is a clean ~212-line additive module (backend/booking.js) owning booking_settings + bookings tables; it does not touch projects, invoices, or clients. Note: a completely separate ad-hoc scheduling path exists (components/ScheduleMeetingModal.js: Google Meet + Calendly inside the lead detail) that shares no data or config with this module.

**Workflow:** The client-facing happy path is genuinely good and low-friction: one page, four cards (Service → Day → Time → Details), no login, optimistic slot validation with a 409 race guard, and a token-based manage page that needs no auth. That end-to-end loop (book → confirm → reschedule/cancel → studio notified) is complete and well thought through.

The breakdowns are on the studio/owner side and at the module seams: (1) The admin /bookings page is config-first; the actual booking list ("Upcoming") is an afterthought at the bottom with no status badge, no filtering, no cancel/reschedule action, and a query that is ORDER BY start_at DESC with no date floor — so past bookings show first under a heading that says "Upcoming." (2) The owner literally cannot cancel or reschedule a booking from the admin UI — bookingAPI.cancel exists in the API layer but is never wired into the page; the only action per row is "Open lead →". (3) The list never live-updates: the backend broadcasts booking_created over SSE (booking.js:167) but no frontend code subscribes, so a studio with the page open never sees new bookings without a manual reload. (4) Email-only clients get a silently broken experience: confirmation/reschedule/cancel messages only fire when lead.customer_phone is set (booking.js:166/194/207, server.js:5529), yet the public success screen unconditionally claims "A confirmation has been sent" (book/[slug]/page.js:45). (5) Per-service duration is dropped when computing public slots, so a 90-min service is offered on the same 30-min grid as a consultation and can be double-booked into the following slot.

**"Where am I / what can I do / what next" test:** Public /book/[slug] (client): PASSES all three. Where am I? "Book with {brand}" header + brand monogram. What can I do? Pick service/day/time, fill details. What next? A single dark "Confirm {time}" button that updates with the chosen time. This is the strongest screen in the module.

Admin /bookings (owner): PARTIAL FAIL on "What should I do next?" and "What can I do?". Where am I? Clear ("Bookings" + calendar icon). But the screen is 90% settings config and the booking list is a passive read-only strip at the bottom with no badges, no cancel, no reschedule, no live refresh — the owner's only verb is "Open lead →." When a client asks to move a time, the owner has no in-app path and must fall back to editing the underlying reminder or messaging manually. It fails as an operational console.

Manage /booking/manage/[token] (client): PASSES. Where am I? "Manage your booking" + the booking card with a status pill. What can I do? Reschedule / Cancel buttons, clearly shown. What next? Obvious. Minor gap: after cancel the two action buttons disappear with no "Rebook" affordance even though the WhatsApp message invites rebooking.

**Standards present:** Plan Enforcement (booking is a resolved entitlement — booking:true across plans in entitlements.js:31; correctly ungated as a core module), Responsive Design (admin page uses r-wrap utility classes; public/manage pages use clamp() + flex/grid and read well on mobile), Global Notifications (notify() fires a 'New booking' feed item on create, booking.js:168), Activity Timeline (writes contact_history entries on create/reschedule/cancel via addContactHistory), Workspace Branding (public + manage pages show brand name + monogram from company_settings), Drafts-of-a-kind / Undo-of-a-kind (cancellation is a soft status flip to 'cancelled', not a hard delete, so it is reversible at the data layer)
**Standards missing:** Bulk Actions (no multi-select on the admin booking list), Saved Views / Filters (no way to filter bookings by service, date, or status), Recycle Bin / Undo (cancelled bookings vanish from the admin list — booking/list excludes status!='cancelled' — with no UI to view or restore them), Audit Logging (create/reschedule/cancel do NOT call logAudit, unlike contracts-studio which is passed logAudit; owner actions are untracked), Universal Search (bookings are not indexed in global search; you cannot search a client's name and find their booking), Command Palette (no booking actions registered), Exports (no CSV/ICS export of bookings; clients also get no 'add to calendar' .ics), Empty/Error states on admin list (only a single 'No bookings yet' line; no skeleton, no error state if list() rejects — the .catch silently swallows it), Keyboard Shortcuts, Feature Discovery (no onboarding/coachmark pointing studios at the booking link; buried below settings), Analytics (no booking conversion / no-show / popular-service metrics anywhere)

**Receives from:** Leads — a public booking finds-or-creates a lead by phone/email and stamps first_message 'Booked: {service}' (booking.js:150-156). This is the one strong inbound link., Reminders — create writes a reminder row with both due_date and reminder_date so the reminder cron actually fires (booking.js:164)., Contact history — create/reschedule/cancel append timeline entries (booking.js:165/192/205)., Company settings — brand name + owner resolution drive the public page branding (booking.js:55-56)., WhatsApp service — bookingSend() wires confirmations through whatsappService (server.js:5528-5533)., MISSING IN: nothing flows from Clients, Projects, or the ScheduleMeetingModal/Google-Calendar path INTO this module — the two scheduling systems are fully disjoint.
**Should hand off to:** Lead (created/updated) — present., Reminder + contact history + notification — present., MISSING OUT: no project is created or linked when a booking is made — a 'Wedding consultation' booking does not become or attach to a project, so the studio re-enters everything., MISSING OUT: no invoice/deposit handoff — services carry a price (services[].price, shown as '$X' on the public page) but booking never creates an invoice or collects a deposit, despite an Invoices module existing., MISSING OUT: booking events do not render distinctly on the lead Timeline/History — 'booking' is absent from HISTORY_ICONS/HISTORY_COLORS (leads/[id]/page.js:1228-1229) so every booking shows the generic grey Activity icon, indistinguishable from a status change., MISSING OUT: no .ics / Google-Calendar export for the client or owner, even though the Google Calendar integration already exists for the other scheduling path., MISSING OUT: clients converted to 'Won'/client status carry no booking history into the Client record.

_12 findings — see appendix._

### Invoices, Payments & Store
Three loosely-coupled money surfaces. (1) Invoices: a list/stats/filter page (invoices/page.js) with a view modal and an email modal; invoices are created only from a lead profile (leads/[id] InvoiceModal), backed by /api/invoices CRUD in server.js (2180-2250). (2) Payments: a provider-agnostic rail (backend/payments.js) that mints a public pay link (/pay/[token]) using Stripe Checkout when STRIPE_SECRET_KEY is set, else a manual "studio marks paid" record; on paid it settles the linked invoice/print_order. (3) Print Store: an admin catalog + orders page (studio/store/page.js) and a public gallery-bound shop (/shop/[token]) backed by backend/print-store.js. The pieces are only thinly integrated and several seams are broken.

**Workflow:** The end-to-end workflows are broken or incomplete at several seams. (1) INVOICE SEND IS BROKEN: invoices/page.js:274 calls invoicesAPI.sendEmail(...), but invoicesAPI in lib/api.js:178-184 defines no sendEmail method, and there is NO backend route for emailing an invoice (grep of server.js + payments.js finds none). The primary "Send via Email" action in the view modal AND the per-row send icon both throw "invoicesAPI.sendEmail is not a function", so invoices can never be delivered to a customer. handleEmailSent even optimistically flips draft->pending for a send that never happened. (2) OVERDUE IS DEAD: nothing in the backend ever sets status='overdue' (grep finds zero writes); parseInvoice (server.js:2174) only rehydrates items. So the Overdue stat card and Overdue filter chip are permanently empty regardless of due_date. (3) MANUAL MARK-PAID BYPASSES THE RAIL: handleMarkPaid calls invoicesAPI.update(id,{status:'paid'}) (PUT /api/invoices:2229) which has no logAudit, no addContactHistory, and creates no payments row — so an invoice marked paid in-app leaves no activity-timeline entry and no money record, unlike a pay-link payment which does both via markPaid/settle in payments.js:63-74. (4) NO PAY LINK IN THE SEND PATH: even if email worked, the pay link is a separate manual button that copies a URL to clipboard; it is never embedded in the customer-facing invoice. (5) PRINT ORDERS HAVE NO PAYMENT: print-store.js POST /api/store/public/:token (96-131) inserts an ms_print_orders row at status 'new' but never creates a payments record or pay link, so the customer is told "we'll be in touch" and pays out-of-band; the store is order-capture only, not commerce. (6) NO INVOICE EDIT: once created from a lead, an invoice can only be viewed/marked-paid/deleted — the list page never opens an edit form even though PUT supports it, so fixing a typo means delete + recreate from the lead.

**"Where am I / what can I do / what next" test:** invoices/page.js (list): "Where am I?" PASS (clear title + stats). "What can I do?" PARTIAL — filters/search/send/delete are visible but there is NO "New invoice" button anywhere; the only hint is the empty-state caption "Create invoices from any lead profile." A first-timer with zero invoices cannot create one from this screen. "What next?" FAIL for new users. InvoiceViewModal: "Where am I?" PASS. "What can I do?" Buttons are clear but two are broken/misleading (Send via Email throws; Mark as Paid bypasses the money rail). "What next?" weak — no edit, no per-invoice payment status detail. studio/store/page.js: "Where am I?" PASS (Print Store header + explainer). "What can I do?" PARTIAL — you can edit products/orders, but the actual shareable shop URL is only described in prose (/shop/<gallery-token>); there is no list of galleries with copyable shop links, so the studio cannot find the link to send. "What next?" FAIL — after adding a product the user has no path to "share the shop".

**Standards present:** Plan Enforcement seam (workspace-scoped queries via req.workspaceOwnerId / req.workspaceId), Audit Logging on invoice create (logAudit at server.js:2222) and contact history on create (addContactHistory 2220), Activity Timeline write on pay-link payment (addContactHistory in payments.js:67) and print order (print-store.js:126), Global Notifications/realtime (broadcastToWorkspace on payment_paid and print_order_created), Empty states and loading spinners on invoices list, pay page, shop page, store page, Status badges with a shared STATUS_COLORS map on the invoices list/modal, Search + status filter on invoices list, Responsive helpers used (r-modal, r-stack, r-wrap, r-scroll-x classes), Server-side price recomputation for shop orders (print-store.js:104-112) — does not trust client prices
**Standards missing:** Drafts of a usable kind — invoices save as 'draft' but cannot be sent (send is broken) or edited, Undo / Recycle Bin — invoice delete is hard DELETE (server.js:2247), no soft-delete despite the app having trash elsewhere, Bulk Actions — no multi-select on invoices list or store orders (no bulk send/mark-paid/delete), Saved Views — none on invoices despite status filtering, Audit Logging on mark-paid, update, delete invoice, and on store order status changes (all missing), Universal Search integration — invoices/orders not searchable from global search, Exports — no CSV/PDF export of the invoice list or payments (single-invoice print only), A Payments dashboard — paymentsAPI.list and paymentsAPI.markPaid exist in api.js:552-553 but no page consumes them; GET /api/payments has no UI, Feature Discovery for Print Store — not in NavBar (only reachable via a 'Print store' ghost button in studio/page.js:184), Per-gallery store opt-in — store_enabled is auto-derived from 'any active product exists' (media-studio.js:2613), so the shop appears on EVERY gallery at once with no per-gallery toggle, Keyboard Shortcuts / Command Palette entries for new invoice / mark paid, Overdue automation (cron or computed status) — completely absent

**Receives from:** Leads/CRM -> Invoices: invoice creation is initiated only from leads/[id] InvoiceModal (handleSave at ~319), passing customer name/email/phone, Company Settings -> Invoices: currency, tax_rate/tax_name, invoice_prefix/counter, company logo/address pulled from company_settings (server.js:2201, settingsAPI.getCompany on invoices/page.js:343), Galleries (Media Studio) -> Shop: /shop/[token] is keyed off ms_galleries.share_token; store_enabled flag injected into the public gallery payload (media-studio.js:2630) and rendered as 'Order prints' on g/[token]:202, Projects -> Print orders: order attaches to the project's existing lead_id when the gallery has a project (print-store.js:115-116), Stripe -> Payments: checkout.session.completed webhook settles the payment (payments.js:117-128)
**Should hand off to:** Payments -> Invoices/Print orders: settle() flips invoice/ms_print_orders to 'paid' on payment (payments.js:65-66) — good, but only triggered by the pay rail, not by manual in-app mark-paid, Print orders -> CRM: creates/links a lead, writes contact history, sends a WhatsApp confirmation (print-store.js:117-127) — solid, MISSING Invoices -> Contracts: a signed contract/deposit does not generate or link an invoice; the two money flows are unconnected (payments.js kind supports 'contract_deposit' but no contract code mints one), MISSING Invoices -> Email/Comms: no route exists to actually send an invoice email (the called sendEmail has no implementation), MISSING Print order -> Invoice/Payment: a placed shop order does not become an invoice or a pay link, so revenue is captured but never collected in-product, MISSING Invoices/Store -> Analytics/Reports: paid revenue is computed client-side per page (invoices/page.js:391) and store sales are not aggregated anywhere central, MISSING Store page -> Gallery: studio/store/page.js gives no way to pick a gallery and copy its /shop/<token> link

_15 findings — see appendix._

### Client Portal & the public client journey (client/[token], g/[token], d/[token], shop/[token], pay/[token], book/[slug], booking/manage/[token], folio/[handle])
WappFlow exposes the client-facing world as eight independent, login-less, token/slug-addressed pages: a unified hub (client/[token]) that aggregates a lead's galleries, documents, invoices, albums, orders, milestones and projects; a photo gallery with favourites/proofing/comments/download/slideshow (g/[token]); a contract/proposal sign surface with an AI "ask" widget (d/[token]); a print shop tied to a gallery token (shop/[token]); a pay link (pay/[token]); a booking page + manage/reschedule page (book/[slug], booking/manage/[token]); and a public portfolio (folio/[handle]). Each is a standalone Next.js client component that fetches one public backend endpoint with no auth. They work individually, but they were clearly built module-by-module: there is no shared shell, no shared branding source, no navigation between them, and the "unified" hub omits half of them.

**Workflow:** The per-surface workflows are individually complete and fairly polished (gallery proofing, contract sign-sheet, booking slot picker, cart checkout all work end to end with sensible empty/loading states). The failure is at the JOURNEY level — these read as eight disconnected microsites, not one product. Three structural gaps: (1) BRANDING IS FRAGMENTED INTO 3 IDENTITIES. The portal/shop/book/pay/booking-manage pages all render a first-letter avatar on a hardcoded blue→indigo gradient (#0ea5e9→#6366f1) + company_name; the gallery (g/[token]) is a totally different black/gold (#c2a878) theme that never shows the studio name at all and footers "Delivered with WappFlow Media Studio"; the portfolio (folio) is a third system with its own avatar, accent color and 10 themes. The studio's real uploaded logo (company_settings.company_logo, server.js:302) is displayed on NONE of them. (2) THE HUB DOESN'T HUB. client/[token] (backend server.js:5498-5525) aggregates galleries, documents, invoices, albums, orders, milestones, projects — but never surfaces booking, pay links, or the shop, and its rows only link out to /g and /d. There is no way to get from the gallery/shop/pay/book pages back to the portal, or between each other, except the one /shop link inside the gallery (g/[token]:203). (3) DEAD-END TERMINAL STATES. After a client signs (d), pays (pay), orders (shop), or books (book), the success screen is a terminal card with no next step — no "see everything for your project" link back to the portal, no "book your session" after signing, no "pay your deposit" after signing. Each conversion drops the client off a cliff instead of into the next step of the relationship.

**"Where am I / what can I do / what next" test:** GALLERY (g/[token]) — Where am I? FAILS: header says only "Your Gallery" + gallery title, never the studio's name/logo; a client who received the link by forward has no idea whose gallery it is, and the footer credits "WappFlow Media Studio" not the photographer. What can I do? PASSES: favourite, comment, download, slideshow, proofing and "order prints" are all discoverable. What next? PARTIAL: "Order prints" links to /shop, but there's no path to the rest of the client's project (invoices, contracts, portal). PORTAL (client/[token]) — Where am I? PASSES: "Welcome, {name}" + brand name. What can I do? PARTIAL: it's a read-only list; rows open galleries/docs but invoices/albums/orders/milestones are non-interactive text with no pay/download action. What next? FAILS: an unpaid invoice shows an "Overdue" pill but is NOT clickable to a pay page (no link wired, portal.js:86), and booking/shop are absent entirely — the one screen meant to orient the client can't route them to their most important action. PAY (pay/[token]) — Where am I? PARTIAL: shows amount + description but no studio brand/logo at all (pay.js renders no header/brand). What can I do? PASSES: pay button or "pay with studio directly" fallback. What next? FAILS: success state is a dead end with no return to portal/receipt.

**Standards present:** Plan Enforcement & feature flags (store_enabled gating in gallery g/[token]:202; download_policy 'none'/'web'/'high-res' enforced backend media-studio.js:2466,2705), Responsive Design (all surfaces use clamp() headings, mobile-first single-column layouts, sticky action bars; gallery lightbox has touch-reachable controls g/[token]:286-297), Analytics (contract d/[token] tracks time-on-page + scroll depth via sendBeacon, d/[token]:52-69), Activity/Notifications handoff INTO staff app (gallery favourite/collection/comment fire broadcastToWorkspace + notify + addContactHistory, media-studio.js:2649,2669-2670,2693), Password protection for private galleries (portalAllowed, media-studio.js:1453), Workspace Branding partially present (company_name surfaced on most surfaces), Empty states (every surface has a tailored empty/loading/not-found state)
**Standards missing:** Workspace Branding — logo image (company_settings.company_logo exists but is shown on ZERO public surfaces) and brand/accent color (hardcoded #6366f1/#c2a878 everywhere; folio has accent but the other 7 don't), Link-preview / SEO metadata — no generateMetadata on any public route, so every shared gallery/portal/contract link previews as generic 'WappFlow — AI-powered customer operations' in WhatsApp/iMessage/social instead of the studio, Cross-surface Navigation — no shared header/footer linking portal↔gallery↔shop↔pay↔book, Universal Search / Command Palette (N/A and correctly absent on client surfaces), Drafts / Undo / Recycle Bin (N/A for clients), Expiry handling consistency — only d/[token] handles 'expired'; gallery/shop/pay/book collapse all failures to one generic 'not available', Consistent identity capture — gallery remembers the visitor's name in localStorage (g/[token]:41,63) but shop/book/pay re-ask name+contact every time with no prefill from the portal, Accessibility — favourite/comment/download actions in the gallery grid are revealed only on mouse hover (g/[token]:247-248); keyboard/screen-reader users rely on the lightbox path

**Receives from:** Leads/CRM → portal (client_portals table keyed by lead_id; portal aggregates that lead's galleries/docs/invoices/albums/orders/milestones/projects, server.js:5504-5517), Media Studio → gallery + shop (published ms_galleries.share_token drives both /g and /shop, print-store.js:87), Contracts Studio → /d sign surface (cs_documents.token), Invoices/Payments → /pay (payment token), Booking config → /book/[slug] and /booking/manage/[token], Studio Portfolio → /folio/[handle], company_settings → brand name + currency symbol for shop/book/pay/portal (print-store.js:40, booking.js:56)
**Should hand off to:** Gallery client actions → staff notifications + lead contact history (favourite/collection/comment, media-studio.js:2649-2670,2693) — GOOD, Booking/order/contract completion → CRM lead create/update + WhatsApp confirmation, MISSING: portal does not surface or link to booking, pay, or shop (server.js:5518-5524) despite the backend comment calling it 'one branded link per client across ALL modules', MISSING: unpaid invoices in the portal don't link to their /pay page (portal.js:86 renders status pill, no href), MISSING: post-conversion handoffs — signed contract → pay deposit / book session; placed order → pay; booked session → portal — all terminate in a dead-end success card, MISSING: portal link is not exposed on the lead/client detail screen or anywhere in the main CRM; it lives only in Contracts Vault (vault/page.js:69)

_10 findings — see appendix._

### Communications (Team Chat + Project Rooms + Huddles/Calls + Floating WhatsApp Chat)
A Slack-style internal team-collaboration module: channels + DMs with real-time messaging over SSE, threads, @mentions, pins, presence (online/away/dnd), read receipts, typing, message edit, voice notes, server-side search, and self-hosted LiveKit voice/video/screenshare huddles with a full call lifecycle (invite/missed/timeline). It also ships Project Rooms (RoomPanel) — a private channel bound to a lead/project/gallery/contract/booking whose messages mirror to the lead's activity timeline — and a separate FloatingChat widget for WhatsApp conversations with customers. Backend is comms.js (additive on chat_* base tables in server.js); frontend is chat/page.js plus RoomPanel/HuddleModal/FloatingChat components. The backend is genuinely deep and well-built; the gaps are almost entirely in surfacing, cohesion, and consistency rather than missing capability.

**Workflow:** The core send/receive/react/reply/thread/pin/search/call workflows are complete and real-time end-to-end (afterMessage fans out over SSE, replacing the old 3s poll). But the module is two disconnected experiences wearing one name: (1) the full Communications 2.0 surface lives only at /chat, and (2) RoomPanel embeds a deliberately stripped-down version inside entity pages. The biggest workflow break is that none of the rich features are discoverable or reachable from outside /chat: when comms.js notifies a user of a call or mention it deep-links to /chat?channel=<id> (comms.js:445,489), but chat/page.js never reads a channel query param — it always opens channels[0] (loadChannels, line 487). So every call/mention deep-link drops the user on the wrong channel. Mentions and thread-replies are notified only via ephemeral broadcastToUser + push (afterMessage lines 168-169, 178-179); they are never written to the persistent notifications table that the global bell reads (only calls call notify(), lines 445/489). An offline @mention therefore vanishes from the global feed and survives only in the /comms/mentions inbox, which has an API (commsAPI.mentions) but no UI anywhere. RoomPanel still polls every 6s (RoomPanel.js:46) even though the SSE infrastructure that killed polling in the main chat already exists. Voice notes record and upload but render as a generic file-download link, not an audio player (MessageBubble lines 243-252). An active call exists in the backend (GET /channels/:id/active-call) but the chat header only ever shows "start" buttons — there's no "Join call in progress" banner, so a teammate who arrives after a call starts cannot join from the UI.

**"Where am I / what can I do / what next" test:** /chat (main): (1) Where am I? PASS — channel name, lock icon, description, message count in header. (2) What can I do? MOSTLY PASS — rich toolbar, but pins/search/huddle/video are icon-only with no labels and the mentions inbox + active-call join are absent. (3) What next? FAIL — no unread/mention signal pulls you to the right channel; deep-links land on channels[0]; there's no "you were mentioned" surface. RoomPanel (entity pages): (1) Where am I? WEAK — header reads "Team Room · <title>" with no indication it's the same system as /chat or that messages mirror to the timeline. (2) What can I do? PARTIAL — type + call only; no threads/pins/react/edit/mentions, and no link to open the full channel in /chat. (3) What next? FAIL — a dead end; you cannot escalate the room conversation into the main comms surface. NavBar entry: (1) Where am I? FAIL — the link is labeled "Inbox" with an Inbox icon (NavBar.js:171) but routes to team chat, while "Inbox" in a CRM means the customer/WhatsApp conversation inbox; the page itself is titled "Team Chat" (page.js:793) and the module is "Communications" — three different names for one thing, and no unread/mention badge on the item.

**Standards present:** Real-time updates (SSE, no polling) in main chat, Search (server-side message search across visible channels), Presence (online/away/dnd) + typing indicators, Read receipts (derived, no extra writes), Pins, Threads, Message edit, @mention autocomplete + mention persistence, Activity Timeline integration (room + call messages mirror to lead timeline), Push notifications (with DND suppression), Confirm-before-delete dialog (useConfirm), Mute toggle (per-user sound mute), HTML sanitization allowlist (XSS-safe rich text), Responsive (r-* utilities + HuddleModal mobile media query)
**Standards missing:** Global Notifications for @mentions/thread-replies (calls only), Mentions inbox UI (API exists, no screen), Command Palette / Universal Search integration (chat not searchable from global search), Keyboard Shortcuts (no channel switching, no jump-to; only Enter/Esc), Saved Views / channel sections beyond Channels+DMs (no favorites/starred), Bulk Actions (no multi-message select/delete), Drafts (typed-but-unsent text is lost on channel switch), Undo (delete is permanent after confirm), Recycle Bin (no message/channel restore), Audit Logging (no log of channel create/delete, pins, edits), Exports (no conversation export), Plan Enforcement (comms has no feature gate despite team_collaboration gating Team/Knowledge), Permissions (no channel admin/member management UI; anyone can delete any channel via chatAPI.deleteChannel), Feature Discovery (no tooltips/coachmarks for huddles/threads/rooms), Workspace Branding (hard-coded #1e1e2e sidebar, ignores theme), Analytics (call_sessions/duration captured but never surfaced)

**Receives from:** Leads detail page → RoomPanel(type=lead) (leads/[id]/page.js:2460), Studio/project detail → RoomPanel(type=project) (studio/[id]/page.js:558), Contract detail → RoomPanel(type=contract) (contracts/[id]/page.js:171), Workspace members API → DM picker, @mention list, presence roster (chat/page.js:473), SSE /api/events → real-time message/edit/delete/reaction/typing/presence/pin frames, LiveKit env (URL/KEY/SECRET) → huddle token minting (comms.js:25)
**Should hand off to:** Room + call messages → lead activity_timeline (afterMessage:152, logCallTimeline:410) — GOOD, Calls → global notifications feed + push (comms.js:445,489) — GOOD, MISSING: @mentions/thread-replies → global notifications feed (only ephemeral SSE+push), MISSING: deep-link from notification → correct channel (chat ignores ?channel=), MISSING: gallery/booking RoomPanel (backend supports both ROOM_ENTITIES, but no page mounts them), MISSING: 'discuss this' entry point from contract/invoice/booking into a DM or channel, MISSING: comms unread/mention count → NavBar badge (badge only counts notifications+leads+reminders), MISSING: call analytics (duration_s) → reports/dashboard

_15 findings — see appendix._

### Command Center (internal platform control plane — /control + /api/cc/*)
A founder/ops-only control plane with its own identity tier (cc_admins, separate JWT audience, IP allowlist, full cc_audit spine) spanning 18 pages: Executive Overview, Founder Inbox, Customers list + Workspace 360, Customer Health scoring, Adoption, Plans editor, Feature Flags, Desktop Fleet, Storage, AI Center, Support tickets, Reports, Event Stream (SSE), Audit Center, Database/SQL console, Time Machine. Backend is command-center.js (910 lines) plus 6 cc-* sub-modules. It is genuinely wired: server.js:5594 calls require('./command-center')(app, db, {...}) and the sub-modules mount inside it — the old MEMORY note "Command Center is dead code / unmounted" is STALE (fixed in P1 per DESKTOP-FINAL-VISION.md lines 45/72). The frontend /control is intentionally unlinked from the main app, reached only via direct URL or the founder-gated desktop nav entry. The plumbing is strong (audit on every write, step-up for SQL, impersonation token mint); the real gaps are UX cohesion, a stale plan/currency vocabulary, an unmounted impersonation banner, and the unbuilt System Health dashboard.

**Workflow:** The core ops loop works: Overview/Inbox surface signals -> click into Workspace 360 -> suspend/restore/grant grace/change plan/impersonate/add override, all audited. But the per-workspace action UX is crude and risky for the stakes involved. Destructive cross-tenant actions are driven by browser window.prompt()/alert(): suspend reason, grace days, and a 3-prompt sequence for overrides (customers/[id]:112-118), plus plan change via an instant <select> with zero confirmation (customers/[id]:36). None of these require step-up or a confirm dialog — even though step-up tokens exist server-side and are already wired into the SQL console (database/page.js:45-53). The single biggest break is impersonation: customers/[id]:46 opens /impersonate, which swaps the admin session into the target workspace and redirects to /dashboard, but ImpersonationBanner.js is imported nowhere in the entire app (grep of all of src finds only its own definition), so the admin lands in the customer's account with NO banner, NO "Exit impersonation" button, and their own session buried in localStorage — the only escape is manually clearing storage. Secondary friction: the Audit Center and Event Stream render flat capped lists with zero filters even though the backend supports admin_id/action/target_type/workspace_id (command-center.js:670-683) and type/workspace_id (647-662) filtering. Customers list filters offer plan values (free/starter/growth) that no longer exist in the live pricing engine, so those filters silently return nothing. Role permissions are returned by /cc/me but never read by any page, so a limited-role admin sees every button and only discovers they lack a permission via a 403 alert().

**"Where am I / what can I do / what next" test:** Workspace 360 (customers/[id]) — Where am I? Clear (workspace name + status pill). What can I do? Visible (suspend/grace/impersonate/plan/override/module buttons), but no role-gating so some buttons fail on click. What next? Weak — no guidance from health scores to action; the risk_factors that drive the Inbox aren't shown here. FAILS partially on safety: irreversible cross-tenant actions fire from prompt()/select with no confirm/step-up. Impersonation result screen (/dashboard while impersonating) — Where am I? FAILS HARD: no banner, indistinguishable from a normal session; admin cannot tell they are impersonating nor how to exit. Audit Center — Where am I? Clear. What can I do? FAILS: no filters/search despite a backend that supports them, so finding a specific admin's actions in a long log is impossible without exporting CSV and grepping. Overview passes all three but mislabels PKR amounts as $ (Implied MRR).

**Standards present:** Audit Logging — comprehensive cc_audit on every write with before/after diffs (command-center.js:210-222), surfaced in Audit Center with expandable diff, Universal Search — global topbar search across workspaces/users/leads/contracts/projects (ControlShell.js:90-106 + /cc/search), Exports — reusable ExportButton (CSV/JSON) respecting current filters, audited server-side (ExportButton.js + command-center.js:828-869), Permissions (backend) — role-based requirePerm gates on every destructive route (command-center.js:202-207), Activity Timeline — per-workspace platform_events feed in Workspace 360 + merged events/audit Time Machine, Plan Enforcement — plan/override/module changes invalidate entitlements and broadcast immediately (command-center.js:421-435, 626-645), Step-up auth — re-password elevation for founder-level SQL (database/page.js:45-53, backend step-up at :268-276), Drafts/Scheduling — saved Reports with on-demand + scheduled email runs (reports/page.js), Live updates — admin SSE event stream consumed correctly via onmessage+switch (events/page.js:19-24), matching the documented unnamed-frame SSE pattern
**Standards missing:** Saved Views — no saved filter sets on Customers/Health/Audit despite multi-filter lists, Bulk Actions — no multi-select on Customers (cannot bulk-suspend, bulk-change-plan, bulk-flag-assign), Keyboard Shortcuts / Command Palette — none; the topbar search is the only fast nav, no Cmd-K, no row-action hotkeys, Undo / Recycle Bin — destructive actions (suspend, override revoke, plan change) have no undo and no confirmation safety net, Global Notifications — no in-app alerting for new high-severity inbox items; founder must open the Inbox page to see them, Responsive Design — ControlShell uses a fixed 232px sidebar with no mobile/collapse handling (ControlShell.js:64); tables overflow on narrow screens, Feature Discovery — /control is reachable only by URL or desktop founder nav; no in-app affordance, tooltips, or onboarding for the ~20 surfaces, System Health dashboard — planned Section 20 (system/page.js, CPU/RAM/disk via pm2/os) is NOT built (no system/ page, no backend endpoint; COMMAND-CENTER-SPEC.md:227/268 marks it partial)

**Receives from:** server.js:5594 mounts the module with auth/broadcastToUser/broadcastToWorkspace/logAudit/JWT_SECRET/sendEmail seams, entitlements.js resolver feeds Workspace 360 entitlements + plan catalog (Plans page reads /cc/plans), cc-metering.js nightly rollup + scoring feeds Customer Health, Adoption, and Founder Inbox signals, Core audit_logs surfaced as legacy events in the Event Stream (command-center.js:656-659), ms_assets / storage tracking feeds the Storage dashboard and per-workspace storage_bytes, ai_usage metering ledger feeds AI Center + Overview AI tile, Desktop fleet device check-ins feed Desktop Fleet (cc-desktop.js), Workspace JWT injection from the desktop webview (harmless; /control uses CC's own auth)
**Should hand off to:** Impersonation -> normal app via /impersonate token swap (works) BUT the return handoff is broken because ImpersonationBanner is never mounted (no exit path back to /control), Plan/override/module changes -> broadcastToWorkspace('plan_updated') so the customer app re-resolves entitlements live (command-center.js:432), Suspend -> NO login enforcement yet; the endpoint itself admits this (command-center.js:398 returns a note that suspended-login enforcement is a follow-up), so suspending a workspace does not actually block its users, Reports -> workspace-owner SMTP seam for scheduled emails (server.js:5596-5604), Support tickets are CC-internal only — no flow OUT to the customer (no customer-facing ticket view or notification), and no flow IN from a customer help widget, Founder Inbox items deep-link only to Workspace 360; no handoff to create a Support ticket or a Report from an at-risk signal, Feature Flags note app-wide enforcement is still pending resolver migration (flags/page.js:34) — flags are authored here but not yet universally enforced

_11 findings — see appendix._

### Settings, Team, Onboarding & Knowledge
This cluster covers account/workspace administration: a ~3000-line "god" Settings page with 18 sidebar tabs (connections, plan, appearance, company, currency, presets, email templates/sending/receiving, auto-reply, tags, lost-reasons, notifications, integrations, workspace, data & privacy, AI command, password); a dedicated Team page (members, roles & per-role/per-member permissions, activity logs); a Profile page; a Knowledge Base (documents, website crawler, AI memories); a workspace-scoped Storage page; and the onboarding funnel (signup, login, accept-invite). Workspace branding (name, logo, currency, email signature) is split across the Settings Company/Workspace/Appearance tabs. The screens are visually polished but are independent, duplicative components with no shared design system, several broken cross-links, and stale plan terminology.

**Workflow:** End-to-end the admin flows function but are fragmented and lean on module-jumping. (1) settings/page.js is one ~3000-line file rendering 18 mutually-exclusive tabs; there is no search/filter over tabs, no grouping (Connections sits beside Change Password), and each tab independently fetches its own data on mount (PresetsTab, TagsTab, AutoReplyTab, NotificationsTab, etc. each useEffect+fetch), so switching tabs re-issues network calls with no caching. (2) Team management is split: Settings > Workspace tab shows your role, workspace-name editor, and a read-only top-5 members list with a "Manage Team" button to /team — so basic team admin spans two screens, and workspace name lives in Settings while members live in /team. (3) Save UX is inconsistent: Company and Currency tabs share the workspace-level save button (handleSaveCompany) holding shared company state, but other child tabs save per-item; there is no unsaved-changes guard, so editing Company/Currency then switching tabs (which calls window.scrollTo and re-renders) risks silently losing edits, and there is no Drafts/Undo anywhere. (4) The signup/login/invite funnel is clean, but password recovery is entirely missing — login.js line 187 has a literal forgot-password placeholder comment and no /forgot-password route exists, so a locked-out user has no self-service path. (5) Knowledge Base has good auto-refresh polling but the two halves (Documents vs Memories) and the website crawler all live on one screen with no link back to where the AI actually uses the knowledge (Inbox/AI replies).

**"Where am I / what can I do / what next" test:** Settings (god page): "Where am I?" PASS (sidebar highlights active tab). "What can I do?" WEAK — 18 ungrouped, unsearchable tabs; the default landing tab is Connections, not an overview, so first-time users face a wall of options. "What next?" FAIL — no overview/onboarding state, and arriving via /settings?tab=billing lands on a BLANK page (no such tab id). Team page: "Where am I?" PASS (workspace name + member count header). "What can I do?" PASS (Invite, tabs for Members/Permissions/Logs). "What next?" PASS for admins (empty state has Invite CTA). Knowledge Base: "Where am I?" PASS (clear header + stats). "What can I do?" PASS (upload, crawl, learn-from-chats, add memory). "What next?" WEAK — after extracting memories there is no handoff/CTA to the Inbox where the AI consumes them, so the value loop is invisible.

**Standards present:** Plan Enforcement (TAB_GATING in settings, LockedOverlay on team/knowledge, per-platform/per-seat limits in Connections & Team invite), Permissions (4 roles + 9-permission matrix + per-member overrides + custom keys in team/page.js), Audit Logging (Activity Logs tab in Team + Audit log card in Settings Data & Privacy, both via auditAPI.getLogs), Exports (Data & Privacy tab full-workspace JSON export), Workspace Branding (logo, company name, email signature, currency in Company/Currency tabs), Responsive Design (r-* utility classes, @media breakpoints in auth pages, mobile NavBar), Search within a screen (Team members search, Knowledge memories search+type filter), Notification settings (push subscribe/unsubscribe/test in NotificationsTab), Theme/Appearance (dark/light toggle)
**Standards missing:** Universal Search / Command Palette (no global search; settings 18 tabs are not searchable), Saved Views (none in Team/Knowledge lists), Bulk Actions (Team members and Knowledge memories have no multi-select; remove/delete are one-by-one), Undo / Recycle Bin (member removal, memory delete, document delete are all permanent - confirm() only), Drafts / unsaved-changes guard (Settings form tabs discard edits on tab switch), Keyboard Shortcuts (none across Settings/Team/Knowledge), Activity Timeline scoped to an entity (logs are flat workspace lists, not per-member or per-document), Imports (Knowledge supports upload/crawl but no bulk memory import/CSV; no settings import), Feature Discovery (no tooltips/coachmarks; 2FA/SSO absent), Password Recovery (no forgot-password flow), Analytics (no usage analytics on Knowledge memory hit-rate or team activity)

**Receives from:** NavBar account menu -> /profile, /settings, /team, /knowledge; plan badge -> /settings?tab=billing (BROKEN id), accept-invite (email link with token) -> inviteAPI.accept -> sets token/user/workspace -> /dashboard, signup/login -> authAPI -> /dashboard with welcome-modal flag, Storage 80/90/100% backend warnings -> Notification center (per file comment), Team invite uses plan.limits.team_members for seat enforcement, Settings Connections feeds the Inbox channels (platformAccountsAPI)
**Should hand off to:** Knowledge memories SHOULD link to the Inbox/AI-reply surface that consumes them (currently no outbound link or 'where this is used' affordance), Company branding (logo, signature, currency) flows OUT to Invoices/Contracts/Email but there is no preview/link from Settings to those modules confirming it applied, Team roles/permissions SHOULD surface in Leads/Inbox assignment, but there is no link from Team to assignment views, Storage page links OUT to /studio/{id} for largest projects (good) but has no inbound link from the Settings sidebar (only reachable via deep link), Audit log SHOULD allow drilling into the referenced entity (entity_id is truncated text, not a link), Data export produces JSON but there is no scheduled-export or re-import path

_16 findings — see appendix._

### Platform Plumbing (entitlements, pricing, plan UX, notifications, confirm, AI copilots, trash, WhatsApp, API client)
This is the cross-cutting layer every module leans on: a data-driven entitlements resolver (backend/entitlements.js) plus live usage/enforcement (backend/pricing.js) feeding a single PlanProvider context (lib/plan.js); plan-lock UI primitives (PlanLock.js); a global UsageWarnings banner; a global Confirm/Alert provider (lib/confirm.js); two AI assistants (AICommandCenter for CRM, StudioCopilot for Studio) plus a WhatsApp FloatingChat; a NavBar notification bell; per-viewer lead-unread tracking (lib/unread.js); two separate Recycle Bins (CRM leads + Studio media); a single axios client (lib/api.js); and WhatsApp connection management. The backend half is genuinely strong. The frontend plan-UX half has drifted out of sync with the 2026-06-21 plan rename (Creator/Studio/Studio+/Enterprise): several plan components still hardcode the dead free/starter/growth tiers and silently never render. WhatsApp UI still drives legacy single-account, unauthenticated endpoints while a full per-account API sits unused.

**Workflow:** The plan/entitlements BACKEND workflow is complete and cohesive: edit a plan in the tables, the resolver picks it up, pricing enforces it live with zero code changes (entitlements.js getEntitlements + pricing.js checkLimit/buildUsage). But the user-facing plan-maturity workflow is broken at the seams. (1) The onboarding "what you unlocked" moment (PlanWelcomeModal) is dead twice over: it only has PLAN_CONFIG for free/starter/growth (none are real tiers), and it is not mounted anywhere, so no user ever sees a welcome/feature tour. (2) The persistent upgrade nudges PlanBanner and PlanChip are written for free/starter/growth and are never imported, so paying users get no in-nav plan visibility beyond the transient UsageWarnings banner. (3) The "Empty Trash" action on the CRM trash page calls leadsAPI.emptyTrash() which does not exist in api.js, so the button throws. The two Recycle Bins diverge in retention (90 vs 30 days), confirm mechanism (global confirm vs window.confirm), and undo affordances. The WhatsApp setup workflow only manages one legacy connection though the plan sells 2-5 accounts. AI assistance is split across two differently-branded copilots with no shared identity. Notifications reach is partial: contracts/bookings/gallery push into the feed, but Studio, Comms unread, and Media jobs do not.

**"Where am I / what can I do / what next" test:** WhatsApp Connection page (app/whatsapp/page.js): "Where am I?" OK (clear status card). "What can I do?" WEAK - only connect/disconnect ONE account; no way to add the 2nd-5th account the plan grants, no per-account list. "What next?" OK when connected (Go to Dashboard). FAILS the multi-account promise. CRM Trash page (app/trash/page.js): "Where am I?" / "What can I do?" clear, but the headline Empty-Trash action is broken (missing API method) so it FAILS "what can I do". AICommandCenter (Ctrl+K palette): "Where am I?" and "What can I do?" good (quick commands, recent history). But discoverability fails - it is a small floating Sparkles FAB with no label, easily mistaken for the green WhatsApp FAB stacked right below it; a first-time user will not know Ctrl+K opens a CRM command palette.

**Standards present:** Plan Enforcement (entitlements.js + pricing.js, live usage, soft levels 80/90/100, master switch), Recycle Bin (two: CRM leads 90d, Studio media 30d), Undo/Restore (restore from both trash bins), Global Notifications (NavBar bell + persistent feed via notificationsAPI), Global confirm/alert dialog (lib/confirm.js, themed, keyboard-driven), Command Palette (AICommandCenter Ctrl+K, CRM-scoped), Keyboard Shortcuts (Ctrl+K open, Esc close, Enter run/confirm), Feature flags + percentage rollouts + per-workspace overrides (entitlements.js), Exports (workspace export URL in api.js), Audit Logs (auditAPI, enterprise feature flag), Responsive Design (PlanLock/PlanWelcome media queries, r-* utilities)
**Standards missing:** Plan UX consistency - PlanWelcomeModal/PlanBanner/PlanChip target dead tiers (free/starter/growth) and are never mounted, so onboarding + persistent upgrade affordances are effectively absent for real Creator/Studio/Studio+ users, Unified global assistant - two separately branded copilots (AICommandCenter, StudioCopilot) with no shared entry point or identity; a third FAB (FloatingChat) collides visually, Universal Search - no cross-module search; AICommandCenter only queries CRM, FloatingChat only searches leads, WhatsApp multi-account management UI (backend exists, page does not use it), Notification reach into Studio / Media jobs / Comms unread (feed is CRM+contracts+bookings only), Drafts (no draft state anywhere in this layer), Bulk Actions in trash bins (no select-all / bulk restore; Studio trash has no Empty-all), Saved Views / Command Palette parity across modules (Studio has none)

**Receives from:** workspace_plan table -> entitlements.getEntitlements (plan tier), plan_features/plan_limits/feature_flags/entitlement_overrides -> resolver, leads.created_at, cs_documents.sent_at, ms_assets/ms_exports.size_bytes, workspace_members, platform_accounts -> pricing.computeUsage (live metrics), /api/workspace/plan-info -> lib/plan.js PlanProvider -> every gated screen via usePlan(), notificationsAPI + leadsAPI + remindersAPI -> NavBar notification feed, leadsAPI messages + SSE /api/events -> FloatingChat, mediaAPI.copilot + PRESETS + project route -> StudioCopilot, whatsappService.getLegacyStatus -> whatsapp/page.js
**Should hand off to:** usePlan().hasFeature / atLimit -> NavBar nav-lock, PlanLock overlays, per-module gates, UsageWarnings -> /settings?tab=plan upgrade, AICommandCenter results -> /leads/:id navigation, StudioCopilot actions -> create gallery from keepers, batch edits, navigate within /studio, FloatingChat openInFloatingChat(lead) custom event -> openable from any lead page, Plan-lock CTAs -> /settings?tab=workspace (INCONSISTENT with UsageWarnings which uses ?tab=plan), MISSING OUT: copilot has no handoff into Contracts/Booking/Invoices; trash restore does not emit an audit/notification; WhatsApp page does not link to platform-accounts to add more accounts

---

## 6. Deliverable 4 — Missing integrations between modules

WappFlow's data model is genuinely well-integrated at the DATABASE level — almost every entity (invoices, contracts, bookings, projects, print orders, meetings) carries a lead_id, the lead-merge handler dynamically reassigns every lead_id-bearing table, and a unified /client/[token] portal aggregates galleries+contracts+invoices+albums+orders+milestones. The two strongest handoffs are gallery-publish (notifies client over WhatsApp + writes lead timeline + feeds portfolio) and contract-signing (opt-in automations that move pipeline, auto-create an invoice/deposit, and create a project). The weaknesses are at the UI/workflow seam, not the schema: (1) winning a lead does NOT promote it to a Client — is_client must be set by a second manual click that nothing prompts, leaving the top-level Clients module empty; (2) the lead profile is a dead-end for starting work — it can create an Invoice but offers no "New Contract", "New Shoot", "New Booking", or "Share Client Portal" action, so each of those means leaving to a different module and re-selecting the same client; (3) the unified client portal — the single best cohesion feature — is surfaced in exactly ONE buried place (Contracts Vault) and is never auto-sent; (4) CRM, Contracts Studio, and Media Studio live in three separate navigation shells with no in-context jump; (5) analytics is siloed (main Reports reads only leads.actual_sale, ignoring invoices/contracts/bookings); (6) the public Booking module creates a lead but no project/shoot and no Google Calendar event; (7) lead permanent-delete uses a hardcoded child-table list that orphans contracts/bookings/projects/meetings. There are no fully BROKEN links — every intended handoff either works or is a manual re-entry; the gaps are "manual" and "missing" handoffs plus discoverability of the integration that already exists.

**Intended chain:** Lead -> Contract -> Booking -> Project -> Gallery -> Album -> Invoice -> Client Portal -> Timeline -> Communications -> Analytics

| Handoff | Status | Evidence | Recommendation |
|---|---|---|---|
| Communications (inbound) -> Lead | OK  seamless | whatsapp-service.js:287 and :876 auto-INSERT a lead on first inbound WhatsApp message (status 'New', platform_source 'whatsapp'). booking.js:152-157 and print-store.js:117-121 also find-or-create a lead from public surfaces. Inbound IG/FB route through the same resolver. | No change needed — funnel entry is automatic across all public channels. |
| Lead -> Contract | ~   manual | contracts-studio cs_documents has lead_id and the New-document modal lets you link a lead (contracts/page.js:191 passes lead_id). But the lead profile (leads/[id]/page.js:1302-1328) quick actions are only Invoice/Email/Schedule/WA-Call — there is NO 'New contract' action. To draft a contract for a lead you must leave to Contracts Studio and re-search the same client. | Add a 'New contract' quick action on the lead profile that deep-links to the contracts New-document flow with ?lead_id= prefilled (the backend csAPI.create already accepts lead_id). |
| Contract (signed) -> Invoice | OK  seamless | contracts-studio.js:257-307 runAutomations(): on full completion, if settings.automations.create_invoice is on, it converts the client's actual package/addon selection to line items and INSERTs an invoice (with deposit support), writes contact_history, and records a cs_event. Gated behind an opt-in toggle in the doc settings (contracts/[id]/page.js:497). | Strong. Consider defaulting create_invoice ON for type='contract'/'retainer' so the common case needs no configuration. |
| Contract (signed) -> Booking | X   missing | runAutomations (contracts-studio.js:257-307) can move_pipeline, create_invoice, and create_project (ms_projects) — but there is no create_booking action and no write to the bookings table. A signed 'Booking & retainer' contract (the template seeded at contracts-studio.js:40) never produces a bookings row or a calendar hold. | Add an optional create_booking automation that inserts a bookings row (and/or a meetings calendar event) from a date block in the signed document, mirroring create_invoice. |
| Lead (Closed - Won) -> Client | ~   manual | Setting status to 'Closed - Won' (server.js:1911-1934) only updates the lead row + contact_history; it never sets is_client. Promotion requires a separate PUT /api/leads/:id/client (server.js:1892). The NBA engine explicitly returns null for Won leads (leads-list/page.js:50), so nothing prompts the move, and the top-level Clients page (clients/page.js:41) shows only is_client=1 rows — so it stays empty after a win. | Either auto-set is_client=1 when status becomes 'Closed - Won' (server.js status handler), or have the NBA/win flow surface a one-click 'Move to Clients' prompt. Backward-compatible and additive. |
| Booking -> Project/Shoot | X   missing | booking.js:134-171 (public booking) creates/finds a lead, inserts a bookings row, sets a reminder, logs contact_history, and notifies — but never creates an ms_projects shoot. A confirmed wedding booking produces a calendar row with no project to ingest photos into; the photographer must manually create the shoot and re-link the client in Media Studio. | On booking confirmation, optionally create an ms_projects row (lead_id linked, project_type from the service, shoot_date from start_at) so the shoot exists the moment a date is booked. |
| Booking -> Calendar (meetings) | X   missing | Two unrelated systems: the public Booking module (bookings table) and Google-Calendar Meetings (meetings table, server.js:5384). booking.js never inserts a meetings row or a Google Calendar event, so self-booked appointments never appear on the studio's connected calendar (server.js:5384 only fires from the manual ScheduleMeetingModal). | When workspace_integrations has a Google Calendar token, mirror new bookings into a calendar event (reuse the meetings insert path) so all appointments live in one calendar. |
| Lead/Project -> Gallery | ~   manual | ms_projects.lead_id links the shoot to the CRM (media-studio.js:59) and the Studio New-shoot modal lets you link a client (studio/page.js:76-93, backend validates at media-studio.js:408). But the lead profile's LeadStudioSection (leads/[id]/page.js:548-582) returns null when the lead has zero shoots, hiding the only 'New shoot' button; and that button just does open('/studio') with no lead context (line 580), forcing a re-search of the same client. | Always render a 'Start a shoot' affordance on the lead profile and pass ?lead_id= to /studio so the New-shoot modal prefills the client (createProject already accepts lead_id). |
| Gallery -> Album | OK  seamless | ms_albums carries project_id (media-studio.js:1345 / studio-ai.js:36) and the public client portal pulls albums by the lead's project ids (server.js:5515). Album pages can be built from gallery favorites within the same project context. | No change needed for the data link. |
| Gallery/Contract -> Invoice | OK  seamless | Contracts auto-create invoices (contracts-studio.js:287). invoices.lead_id ties the invoice to the client and the portal lists them (server.js:5510). Manual invoice creation from the lead profile also works (leads/[id]/page.js:319). | See the separate invoice-origin finding: invoices have no contract_id/booking_id back-link, and the Invoices page itself has no create button. |
| Invoice -> Contract/Booking (back-link) | X   missing | The invoices table (server.js:331-354) has lead_id but NO contract_id or booking_id column. An auto-created invoice records its origin only as free text in notes ('Auto-created from signed contract: <title>', contracts-studio.js:281). There is no clickable link from an invoice back to the contract or booking that generated it. | Add nullable source_type/source_id columns to invoices, set them in runAutomations, and render a 'From contract X' link on the invoice — closes the loop without a rewrite. |
| Gallery delivered -> Client Portal | ~   manual | Gallery publish (media-studio.js:1630-1662) sends the client a bare /g/[token] gallery link and writes the lead timeline. The unified /client/[token] portal (server.js:5498-5526) that aggregates galleries+contracts+invoices+albums+orders+milestones is generated by a separate endpoint and is surfaced only in Contracts Vault (contracts/vault/page.js:23). The client therefore receives fragmented per-asset links instead of one hub, and the portal is never auto-sent. | Offer the unified /client/[token] link from the lead profile and from gallery-publish/contract-send dialogs; optionally send the portal link instead of the bare gallery link so the client lands on the full hub. |
| Client Portal -> Timeline | OK  seamless | Media events (project/upload/gallery_published/proofing) call emitToLead (media-studio.js:345-355) which writes activity_timeline + contact_history; comms room messages/calls mirror to the lead timeline (comms.js:152,410); meetings write activity_timeline (server.js:5390); contracts/bookings/invoices write contact_history. GET /api/leads/:leadId/timeline (server.js:4790-4818) merges activity_timeline + messages + notes + contact_history into one feed. | Two parallel timeline tables (activity_timeline vs contact_history) are both folded in, so it works — but standardize new modules on activity_timeline, and fold invoices/meetings/bookings rows directly so they appear even when no history row was written. |
| Project room -> Communications | OK  seamless | comms project_rooms links a chat channel to an entity (comms.js:68-72); room messages and call lifecycle events resolve the room's lead and mirror to activity_timeline (comms.js:152, 406-413, validated by test-comms.js:127-135). | No change needed — internal comms correctly attach to lead/project context. |
| Store order -> Invoice/Payment | X   missing | Public print orders (print-store.js:99-129) link to the lead via the gallery's project, write contact_history, and WhatsApp a thank-you, but only INSERT an ms_print_orders row at status 'new' ('We will be in touch to finalize it'). No invoice or payment record is created, so there is no path from an order to getting paid. | On order creation, optionally create an invoice (and/or a payments.link) from the order lines so store revenue flows through the same billing pipeline as everything else. |
| All modules -> Analytics | ~   manual | reports/overview (server.js:2356-2430) computes revenue solely from leads.actual_sale where status='Closed - Won' and reports leads/pipeline/sources/agent-perf/response-time. It never reads invoices (actual paid), cs_documents (signed value), bookings, or media delivery. Contracts has its own analytics (contracts-studio.js:830+, contracts/analytics page) and Command Center has cc-reports — each siloed. | Add invoice-paid revenue and signed-contract value to the main Reports overview (or a unified 'Business' tab) so the dashboard reflects real money, not just lead-stage estimates. |

**Integration findings:**
