---

## 7. Deliverable 5 — Performance bottlenecks

32 performance findings (backend data layer + frontend rendering/bundle).

#### `backend-perf-3` messages.wa_message_id has no index — dedup full-scans on every inbound message and in a 200-row sync loop — HIGH / S effort / high impact
- **Where:** server.js:624 adds the column (ALTER TABLE messages ADD COLUMN wa_message_id TEXT) but the only messages index is idx_messages_lead ON messages(lead_id) (server.js:757). The dedup key wa_message_id is queried with no index in two write hot paths: whatsapp-service.js:365 'SELECT id FROM messages WHERE wa_message_id = ?' on every inbound WhatsApp message, and server.js:1709 the same query INSIDE the per-message loop of /api/leads/:leadId/messages/sync (loop at 1706, fetchHistory pulls up to 200 messages at 1702).
- **Problem:** Each duplicate check is a full table scan of messages. For the sync endpoint that is up to 200 sequential full scans per lead-open (a true N+1); for live inbound it is one full scan per received message. As the messages table grows (the largest write-volume table), both paths degrade linearly and stall the request/worker thread.
- **Fix:** Add CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_messages_wa ON messages(wa_message_id) WHERE wa_message_id IS NOT NULL. Then drop the per-row SELECT entirely and switch the inserts at server.js:1712 and whatsapp-service.js:373 to INSERT OR IGNORE, letting the unique index enforce dedup in a single statement.

#### `backend-perf-4` ms_assets (largest table) indexed only by project_id — workspace/sync/storage scans miss the index — HIGH / S effort / high impact
- **Where:** media-studio.js:149 — the only ms_assets index is idx_ms_assets_project ON ms_assets(project_id). Multiple workspace-scoped queries cannot use it: sync.js:26 'SELECT * FROM ms_assets WHERE workspace_id=? AND updated_at>? ORDER BY updated_at' (offline-first delta, runs frequently); media-studio.js:550 storage byType 'WHERE workspace_id=? AND deleted_at IS NULL GROUP BY type'; media-studio.js:906 trash sweep 'WHERE deleted_at IS NOT NULL AND deleted_at < ...'; command-center.js:337 per-workspace 'SUM(a.size_bytes) FROM ms_assets WHERE a.workspace_id=w.id'.
- **Problem:** All of these full-scan the highest-row-count table (one row per imported photo/video, easily tens of thousands per studio). The sync delta in particular runs on every reconnect/poll of the desktop and web offline layer, so a slow scan there is felt constantly.
- **Fix:** Add two indexes: CREATE INDEX idx_ms_assets_ws_updated ON ms_assets(workspace_id, updated_at) to cover the sync delta + ORDER BY, and CREATE INDEX idx_ms_assets_ws_deleted ON ms_assets(workspace_id, deleted_at) to cover storage rollups and the trash sweep. These directly serve the queries above with no code change.

#### `frontend-perf-2` Dashboard fans out up to 30 sequential getById calls for reminders an existing single endpoint already returns — HIGH / S effort / high impact
- **Where:** app/dashboard/page.js:754-757: `const reminderPromises = leadsData.slice(0,30).map(lead => leadsAPI.getById(lead.id).then(r => r.data.reminders || []))` then `await Promise.all(reminderPromises)`. This issues up to 30 full lead-detail requests on every dashboard load just to collect reminders. A dedicated endpoint already exists: backend server.js:2097 `app.get('/api/reminders/upcoming', ...)` and is wired in lib/api.js:175 as `remindersAPI.getUpcoming()` — but the dashboard does not use it. NavBar.js:297 already uses getUpcoming() correctly, proving the endpoint works.
- **Problem:** The dashboard makes ~31 HTTP round-trips (1 leads + 30 lead-detail) where 2 would suffice, and silently shows reminders for only the first 30 leads (data-correctness bug: a reminder on the 31st-newest lead never appears). This is the slowest part of first-paint after login.
- **Fix:** Replace the slice(0,30).map(getById) block with a single `remindersAPI.getUpcoming()` call inside the existing `Promise.all` at dashboard/page.js:743. This removes ~30 requests, fixes the 30-lead truncation, and reuses code NavBar already relies on.

#### `frontend-perf-3` PlanProvider context value is rebuilt every render, re-rendering every usePlan() consumer app-wide — HIGH / S effort / high impact
- **Where:** lib/plan.js:72-97 constructs a fresh `value` object literal (with freshly-created hasFeature/hasLimit/quotaFor/usageLevel/atLimit closures) on EVERY render of PlanProvider, then passes it to `<PlanContext.Provider value={value}>` (line 99). Nothing is wrapped in useMemo/useCallback. PlanProvider wraps the entire authenticated app (providers.js:13 → {children}) and its own `setInterval(fetchPlan, 5*60*1000)` (plan.js:43) calls setPlanInfo, forcing a provider re-render every 5 minutes. usePlan() is consumed in 9 files including the global NavBar (3x), UsageWarnings, PlanWelcomeModal, settings (4x), reports, leads-list, team, knowledge (grep: 15 call sites).
- **Problem:** Because the context value identity changes on every provider render, React notifies and re-renders every usePlan() subscriber even when plan data is unchanged — including large pages like settings (3173 lines) and leads-list (1325 lines). The 5-minute background refresh and any ancestor re-render cascades into the whole consumer tree.
- **Fix:** Wrap the `value` object in `useMemo(() => ({...}), [planInfo, loading, fetchPlan])` and wrap hasFeature/hasLimit/quotaFor/usageLevel/atLimit in useCallback (deps: [planInfo]). This is a few-line change that eliminates app-wide re-render churn with no behavior change.

#### `crm-leads-8` Bulk 'Move to stage' and 'Move to Clients' fire one request per lead sequentially with no batched endpoint or progress UI — HIGH / M effort / high impact
- **Where:** leads-list/page.js:970-979 loops `for (const id of ids) { await leadsAPI.updateStatus(id, {status}); }` and 996-1002 loops `await leadsAPI.setClient(id, true)` — both sequential awaits. A batched pattern already exists for trash: POST /api/leads/bulk-trash runs a single `UPDATE … WHERE id IN (…)` (server.js:5150-5163).
- **Problem:** Selecting 50 leads and moving them to a stage fires 50 serialized round-trips (seconds of frozen UI with no spinner), and individual failures are swallowed by empty catch blocks so the user gets a success toast even on partial failure. It's also inconsistent with bulk-trash/bulk-assign which are single calls.
- **Fix:** Add POST /api/leads/bulk-status and POST /api/leads/bulk-client mirroring bulk-trash (single IN-clause UPDATE in a transaction, addContactHistory per id, logAudit, SSE). Swap the frontend loops for one call each with a loading state on the bulk bar.

#### `backend-perf-1` auth middleware runs 4 DB queries on every authenticated request — HIGH / M effort / high impact
- **Where:** server.js:158-206 — the auth() middleware that guards essentially every /api route runs four sequential better-sqlite3 queries per request: SELECT workspace_id,business_name,full_name FROM users (171), SELECT role,permissions FROM workspace_members (177), SELECT user_id FROM workspace_members WHERE role='super_admin' (182), and SELECT status FROM workspaces (195). Additionally getWorkspaceId() (server.js:2696-2699) re-runs a 5th 'SELECT workspace_id FROM users' inside chat handlers (called at 2718) even though req.workspaceId is already populated by auth.
- **Problem:** Because auth is on the hot path of every single API call, these 4 queries multiply across the entire app. A page that fires 8 parallel API calls on load executes 32 of these lookups before any real work happens. The role lookup and owner lookup hit the same workspace_members table twice; the data is also effectively static per session yet re-fetched every request.
- **Fix:** Collapse the user+role+owner lookups into one query (e.g. a single SELECT joining users to workspace_members for the caller's role, plus a cached owner/status). Add a short-lived in-process cache keyed by userId+workspaceId (e.g. 30-60s TTL Map, invalidated on member/plan/suspend mutations) so repeated requests in a burst skip the DB entirely. Delete the redundant getWorkspaceId() call in the chat handlers and use req.workspaceId directly (it is already set at server.js:173).

#### `backend-perf-2` Photo-cull asset grid: 6 correlated subqueries per row over up to 10,000 rows — HIGH / M effort / high impact
- **Where:** media-studio.js:843-875 — GET /api/media/projects/:id/assets sets limit = Math.min(parseInt(limit)||100, 10000) (848, comment: 'high cap for full-shoot culling') then selects each asset with 6 inline correlated subqueries against ms_asset_scores (lines 860-865: sharpness, quality, exposure, high_clip, shadow_clip, dup_group), each '(SELECT value FROM ms_asset_scores s WHERE s.asset_id=a.id AND s.score_type=? LIMIT 1)'. The only supporting index is idx_ms_scores_asset ON ms_asset_scores(asset_id) (media-studio.js:151) — no (asset_id, score_type) composite.
- **Problem:** On a full-shoot cull view (the explicit 10k use-case), this executes up to 60,000 correlated subquery seeks in a single request, each seeking by asset_id then filtering score_type without a composite index. This is the slowest read path in Media Studio and it is exactly the screen the product wants to feel instant.
- **Fix:** Replace the 6 subqueries with one LEFT JOIN + conditional aggregation pivot: JOIN ms_asset_scores once and use MAX(CASE WHEN score_type='quality' THEN value END) AS quality, ... GROUP BY a.id. Add CREATE INDEX idx_ms_scores_asset_type ON ms_asset_scores(asset_id, score_type) so the join is covered. This turns ~60k seeks into one indexed scan of the relevant score rows.

#### `frontend-perf-1` Global NavBar refetches the ENTIRE leads table every 60s on every authenticated page — HIGH / M effort / high impact
- **Where:** components/NavBar.js:277 sets `setInterval(loadNotifData, 60000)`. loadNotifData (NavBar.js:292-319) calls `leadsAPI.getAll(null)` (line 296) which hits `/api/leads` — backend server.js:1493-1532 returns the full leads table for the workspace with NO LIMIT/pagination (`SELECT * FROM leads ... ORDER BY last_message_at DESC`). The entire payload is fetched only to compute `today = all.filter(l => new Date(l.created_at).toDateString() === now.toDateString())` (NavBar.js:300) for a notification badge. NavBar is mounted on every authenticated route, so a studio with thousands of leads transfers + JSON-parses its whole CRM every minute, on every open tab, regardless of what page the user is on.
- **Problem:** A notification-badge count drives a full-table download every 60 seconds across the whole app. On a large workspace this is hundreds of KB of JSON parsed on the main thread every minute per tab, plus repeated DB scans server-side — pure waste that makes the app feel heavy and drains battery/bandwidth, with zero user-visible value beyond a single integer.
- **Fix:** Add a lightweight backend endpoint (e.g. GET /api/notifications/summary) that returns today-lead count + urgent-reminder count + unread feed as a small JSON object, and have loadNotifData call that instead of leadsAPI.getAll(null). Also raise the poll interval to ~120s or replace the poll entirely by consuming the SSE `lead_created` frames the app already emits (FloatingChat already holds an EventSource). Reuse the existing dedicated remindersAPI.getUpcoming() rather than deriving urgency client-side.

#### `frontend-perf-4` Leads list renders every row unvirtualized and unmemoized, fed by an unpaginated all-leads fetch — HIGH / L effort / high impact
- **Where:** app/leads-list/page.js:1140 `leads.map((lead, i) => { ... })` renders ALL filtered leads directly into the DOM — each row is a 9-column CSS grid with inline style objects, two inline onMouseEnter/onMouseLeave handlers (lines 1148-1149), and multiple inline IIFEs (e.g. the platform-chip IIFE at 1165-1176 and the phone IIFE at 1183-1192). No react-window/virtuoso, no React.memo row component (grep for memo/LeadRow in the file returns nothing), no slice cap (unlike the dashboard which slices, and Studio which caps at visibleCount=400). The data comes from `leadsAPI.getAll(...)` (line 740) → backend server.js:1493 returns all rows unpaginated. `memberById` (line 829) does an O(n) `members.find` per row inside the map.
- **Problem:** A workspace with a few thousand leads mounts a few thousand complex grid rows at once, recreating all inline handlers/styles on every keystroke in the search box (applyFilters writes a new `leads` array → full remount). Typing in search, sorting, or tag-toggling visibly janks on large datasets — exactly the CRM screen power users live in.
- **Fix:** Extract the row into a `React.memo`'d <LeadRow> component (hoist the inline style/handler closures out of the map), and virtualize the list with react-window (or at minimum apply the same visibleCount-slice pattern Studio uses at studio/[id]/page.js:498). Longer-term, add server-side pagination/limit to /api/leads and load incrementally. Replace `members.find` per row with a memoized member map.

#### `frontend-perf-5` Dashboard eagerly bundles recharts + @hello-pangea/dnd; next/dynamic is used nowhere in the app — MED / M effort / high impact
- **Where:** app/dashboard/page.js statically imports `@hello-pangea/dnd` (line 5: DragDropContext/Droppable/Draggable) and `recharts` (lines 16-18: BarChart/Bar/PieChart/etc.), both heavy libraries, into the first authenticated screen. package.json confirms `@hello-pangea/dnd ^18.0.1` and `recharts ^3.8.1`. A repo-wide grep for `next/dynamic` returns ZERO matches in src/ — no component or library anywhere is code-split beyond Next's automatic per-route split. reports/page.js also statically pulls recharts.
- **Problem:** Recharts and a full drag-and-drop engine are shipped in the dashboard's initial JS even though the charts are below the fold and the Kanban board is one of several views. This inflates the post-login Time-to-Interactive on the most-visited authenticated page. The total absence of next/dynamic means no lazy boundaries exist anywhere to trim god-page bundles.
- **Fix:** Lazy-load the chart blocks and the DnD board via `next/dynamic(() => import(...), { ssr: false, loading: ... })` in dashboard/page.js (and the Recharts blocks in reports/page.js). This is additive and backward-compatible. Establishing one next/dynamic pattern also unlocks splitting other heavy widgets (AICommandCenter, RoomPanel/HuddleModal, the album/video editors).

#### `gap-6` Dashboard fires up to 30 per-lead requests on every load to assemble reminders — HIGH / M effort / medium impact
- **Where:** dashboard/page.js:754-757: `leadsData.slice(0,30).map(lead => leadsAPI.getById(lead.id) ...)` then Promise.all — i.e. 30 individual GET /leads/:id round-trips on every dashboard mount purely to collect reminders. fetchLeads() (731-738) and the lead_created/missed_sync handlers also re-fetch ALL leads with leadsAPI.getAll(null) (no pagination param).
- **Problem:** Opening the dashboard issues 1 list call + analytics + tags + 30 detail calls; each detail call returns the full lead payload (messages, notes, history) just to read reminders. On a populated workspace this is dozens of requests and a visibly slow, janky first paint — the exact 'god-page / unbounded fetch' failure mode, but with a precise, fixable cause.
- **Fix:** Add a single GET /api/reminders?upcoming=1 (or include an upcoming_reminders array on the leads list response) so the dashboard fetches reminders in one call; stop calling getById in a loop. Make fetchLeads/getAll accept a bounded page size instead of null (fetch-all).

#### `studio-library-6` Studio Brain recommendation is recomputed (with table writes) on every cull-page load — MED / S effort / medium impact
- **Where:** cull/page.js:99 calls brainsAPI.recommendations() on mount. That endpoint (brains.js:123-137) calls deriveCreatorBrain() AND deriveStyle() unconditionally; deriveCreatorBrain runs INSERT...ON CONFLICT for several keys (brains.js:56-58) and deriveStyle scans up to 5000 score rows then UPSERTs a style_profiles row (brains.js:69-90) — on a GET, every page open.
- **Problem:** A read-path GET performs aggregate scans + multiple writes each time any user opens a cull screen. On a busy workspace this is needless write amplification and lock contention on SQLite, and couples a cheap UI hint to an expensive recompute. It is also a subtle data-integrity smell (GET mutates).
- **Fix:** Cache the derived brain/style (updated_at already exists) and recompute lazily — e.g. only re-derive if stale (>N hours) or when cull decisions changed since last derive. Have GET /recommendations read the cached style_profiles/creator_brain rows and only call derive on an explicit /derive POST (which already exists). Small change in brains.js.

#### `studio-video-5` Export modal polls instead of consuming the already-emitted SSE event — MED / S effort / medium impact
- **Where:** The worker already broadcasts ms_video_export progress/done frames (media-worker.js:790-808). ExportModal ignores them and runs setInterval(getVideoExport, 1500) until done/failed (video/[timelineId]/page.js:901-907). No web file consumes ms_video_export (grep found zero). The codebase's documented SSE pattern is es.onmessage + switch on data.type (used in dashboard/leads/chat).
- **Problem:** Redundant 1.5s polling of a per-export endpoint for the full render duration (potentially minutes for 4K), duplicating data already pushed over the workspace SSE stream. Inconsistent with the rest of the app's realtime pattern.
- **Fix:** Subscribe to the workspace SSE stream and switch on data.type==='ms_video_export' (matching exportId) to drive progress/done, per the SSE memo. Keep a single fallback poll only if SSE is unavailable.

#### `booking-2` Admin booking list never live-updates despite SSE being broadcast — MED / S effort / medium impact
- **Where:** booking.js:167 broadcasts broadcastToWorkspace(ws,'booking_created',...) on every create, and reschedule/cancel re-broadcast 'booking_created' (booking.js:195/208). app/bookings/page.js loads bookingAPI.list() once in a mount useEffect (line 22) and never subscribes to the SSE stream. MEMORY notes the app's SSE pattern is es.onmessage + switch on data.type.
- **Problem:** A studio with /bookings open sees no new bookings arrive — they must reload. The infrastructure to make it real-time already exists and is being paid for (the broadcast fires), but the consumer is missing.
- **Fix:** Subscribe to the existing workspace SSE stream on the bookings page and, on data.type==='booking_created', re-fetch bookingAPI.list() (or append). This matches the documented es.onmessage switch pattern and is a few lines.

#### `backend-perf-5` No busy_timeout / synchronous pragmas despite a concurrent in-process write worker — MED / S effort / medium impact
- **Where:** server.js:38-41 opens the DB and sets only db.pragma('journal_mode = WAL'). A grep across backend/ finds no busy_timeout, synchronous, cache_size, or mmap_size pragma anywhere (only scripts/clean_and_seed_demo.js sets foreign_keys). Meanwhile media-worker.js:873-882 runs a setInterval loop that writes to the same DB (ms_jobs claim at 818, asset UPDATEs throughout) concurrently with request-thread writes.
- **Problem:** Under WAL, the default synchronous=FULL adds extra fsyncs versus the recommended synchronous=NORMAL. More importantly, with no busy_timeout a write that collides with the background worker's write throws SQLITE_BUSY immediately instead of briefly waiting, surfacing as random 500s during media import/export bursts.
- **Fix:** Right after WAL is enabled (server.js:41) add db.pragma('synchronous = NORMAL'); db.pragma('busy_timeout = 5000'); (and optionally cache_size = -16000 for a 16MB page cache). All are safe, additive, single-line changes that remove a class of intermittent BUSY failures and reduce write latency.

#### `studio-library-7` Project detail polls the full asset list (limit 5000) every 20s, re-setting state mid-interaction — MED / M effort / medium impact
- **Where:** studio/[id]/page.js:195 `poll = setInterval(() => { refreshAssets(); refreshGalleries(); }, 20000)`. refreshAssets (:179) does listAssets with limit:5000 and setAssets(...) wholesale. The grid renders up to visibleCount(400) items; a wholesale setAssets re-renders the masonry every 20s regardless of whether the user is selecting or scrolling.
- **Problem:** On large shoots this is a heavy refetch + full re-render on a fixed timer, purely to pick up client favourites/comments. It can cause visible jank and, combined with selection state, risks surprising the user during multi-select. The galleries refresh is the only part that truly needs polling (favourites/comments live on galleries, not assets).
- **Fix:** Poll only refreshGalleries() on the interval (favourites/comments live there); refresh assets only after an action (upload/watermark/edit) or via the existing broadcastToWorkspace SSE events (ms_gallery_published etc. already broadcast). If asset polling is kept, diff by id/variants before setAssets to avoid re-rendering unchanged items.

#### `contracts-11` Document and lead lists are fetched unbounded (no pagination/LIMIT) — MED / M effort / medium impact
- **Where:** GET /cs/documents has no LIMIT and SELECTs d.* (loads the blocks column for every row even though shapeDoc omits it by default) — contracts-studio.js:455,216-224. NewDocModal and BulkSendModal call leadsAPI.getAll(null) to load ALL leads client-side (page.js:177, 276); the Overview itself loads all docs and filters in-memory (page.js:51-59).
- **Problem:** For a studio with thousands of documents/leads this loads the entire table on every Overview visit and every modal open, and the SQL pulls each document's full blocks JSON off disk into memory needlessly. The client-side filtering also means search/filter never hits an index. Fine today, a wall later — and it's a quiet one-OS-feel killer when the app gets sluggish at scale.
- **Fix:** Add pagination + a server-side search param to /cs/documents, and SELECT explicit columns (exclude blocks) for the list query. Make the New/Bulk modals query leads by search term server-side rather than getAll(null). Backend already filters by status/type/lead_id, so extending it is low-risk.

#### `comms-8` RoomPanel polls every 6s despite SSE being available — MED / M effort / medium impact
- **Where:** RoomPanel sets setInterval(() => load(cid), 6000) (RoomPanel.js:46) and re-fetches the full last-50 messages each tick. The main chat already replaced polling with SSE (page.js:418-467, comment line 416 'replaces the old 3s poll'). afterMessage already broadcasts chat_message to the whole workspace, so room messages are on the bus.
- **Problem:** Every open lead/project/contract detail page issues a getMessages call every 6 seconds per viewer — N tabs × M open entities of constant DB load, and a 6s latency on a feature meant to feel live. It also duplicates the transport the rest of comms abandoned.
- **Fix:** Subscribe RoomPanel to the shared SSE stream and filter chat_message frames by channel_id === room channel; drop the interval (or keep a slow 60s reconcile as a safety net). Consider a tiny shared SSE context so chat + RoomPanel + NavBar share one EventSource instead of each opening its own.

#### `comms-12` Unbounded message load (no pagination / infinite scroll) — MED / M effort / medium impact
- **Where:** loadMessages fetches the latest 60 (page.js:497) and there is no 'load older' control; the GET route supports a `before` cursor (server.js:2760) but the client never uses it. Scroll always jumps to bottom on any messages change (page.js:402-404), so even if older messages were prepended the view would snap down.
- **Problem:** Channel history older than 60 messages is unreachable in the UI, and the unconditional scrollIntoView would fight any back-scroll. For long-running channels this hides history and makes search the only way back.
- **Fix:** Add 'Load earlier messages' (or scroll-up trigger) using the existing `before` cursor, and only auto-scroll-to-bottom when the user is already near the bottom (track scroll position) so reading history isn't interrupted by new messages.

#### `settings-team-7` Every Settings tab refetches on mount with no cache; revisiting a tab re-hits the API — MED / M effort / medium impact
- **Where:** PresetsTab fetchPresets (line 322), LostReasonsTab (417), EmailTemplatesTab (493), AutoReplyTab (604), TagsTab (716), NotificationsTab (807), IntegrationsContent (1040), DataPrivacyTab auditAPI.getLogs (1245), WorkspaceTab workspaceAPI.get (1409), ConnectionsTab (1770), EmailSendingTab (2524), EmailReceivingTab (2693), AICommandTab (3004). Tabs are conditionally rendered (lines 2477-2494) so each is unmounted when inactive and refetches on every revisit.
- **Problem:** Navigating away and back to any tab re-issues its network request with no stale-while-revalidate, making Settings feel slow on repeat visits and multiplying server load.
- **Fix:** Lift per-tab data into a lightweight cache, or keep tab components mounted with display:none, or adopt a simple SWR/react-query pattern so each tab fetches once per session. Low risk since each tab owns its own state.

#### `platform-8` NavBar notification poll re-fetches ALL leads every 60s on every page just to compute a badge — MED / M effort / medium impact
- **Where:** NavBar.js:277 `const poll = setInterval(loadNotifData, 60000)` and loadNotifData (NavBar.js:295) calls `leadsAPI.getAll(null)` (unbounded) purely to filter today's leads (line 300) and feed the badge. FloatingChat (FloatingChat.js:101) and AICommandCenter also call leadsAPI.getAll(null) — full lead list fetches.
- **Problem:** On a workspace with thousands of leads, every authenticated tab downloads the entire lead list once a minute (plus on every floating-chat open) just to derive a small "new today" count. This is wasteful bandwidth/CPU and contradicts the speed/polish goal.
- **Fix:** Add a lightweight `/api/notifications/summary` (or extend notificationsAPI.get) that returns counts + today's new-lead count server-side, and have NavBar consume that instead of getAll(null). Paginate/scope the floating-chat and command-center lead fetches.

#### `backend-perf-6` broadcastToWorkspace runs a members query per event, including per ffmpeg progress tick — MED / M effort / medium impact
- **Where:** server.js:849-853 — broadcastToWorkspace() runs 'SELECT user_id FROM workspace_members WHERE workspace_id=?' on every call, then fans out. It is invoked 71 times across modules (booking, comms, contracts, media-studio, media-worker, etc.). The worst case is media-worker.js:798: inside runFfmpeg onProgress it calls broadcastToWorkspace(...,'ms_video_export',{progress:pct}) on every progress tick, and media-worker.js:380/475/687/704 fire it once per processed asset during bulk ingest.
- **Problem:** Every realtime event pays a DB round-trip just to resolve recipients. A single video export emits dozens-to-hundreds of progress ticks, each running a members lookup; a bulk photo import of N assets runs N members lookups. The member list changes rarely, so this is repeated wasted work on the realtime path.
- **Fix:** Cache the workspace member-id list in a Map (TTL ~60s, or invalidate on member add/remove via the existing member mutation handlers) and have broadcastToWorkspace read from it. Separately, throttle the ffmpeg progress broadcast in media-worker.js:796-799 (e.g. only emit when pct changes by >=2% or every ~750ms) to cut tick volume regardless.

#### `backend-perf-7` Core list endpoints have no pagination/LIMIT (leads, invoices, contracts, lead messages) — MED / M effort / medium impact
- **Where:** server.js:1493-1536 GET /api/leads builds 'SELECT * FROM leads ... ORDER BY last_message_at DESC' with no LIMIT and then enriches every row in JS. server.js:2180-2185 GET /api/invoices 'SELECT * FROM invoices WHERE user_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC' no LIMIT. contracts-studio.js:455 GET /api/cs/documents 'SELECT d.*, l.customer_name ... ORDER BY d.updated_at DESC' no LIMIT. server.js:1597-1613 GET /api/leads/:leadId/messages 'SELECT * FROM messages WHERE lead_id=? ORDER BY timestamp ASC' no LIMIT (and idx_messages_lead does not cover the timestamp sort).
- **Problem:** These return the entire table for the workspace/lead in one payload. A studio with thousands of leads or a chat thread with thousands of synced WhatsApp messages ships a multi-MB JSON and forces a full sort each request — the opposite of the snappy single-OS feel the product is targeting. The leads list also runs an extra full sort the index doesn't help with for the messages thread.
- **Fix:** Add limit/offset (or keyset) pagination to /api/leads, /api/invoices, /api/cs/documents consistent with the pattern already used at media-studio.js:848 and command-center.js:322. For lead messages, default to the most recent N (e.g. ORDER BY timestamp DESC LIMIT 100 then reverse, with a 'before' cursor like the chat handler at server.js:2755-2763) and add a composite index idx_messages_lead_ts ON messages(lead_id, timestamp) to cover the sort.

#### `frontend-perf-6` FloatingChat (global) and each page both open their own EventSource to /api/events, doubling concurrent SSE connections — MED / M effort / medium impact
- **Where:** components/FloatingChat.js:65 opens `new EventSource(.../api/events?token=...)`, and FloatingChat is mounted globally inside NavBar (NavBar.js:713 `<FloatingChat />`). Simultaneously each major page opens its OWN second EventSource to the same endpoint: app/dashboard/page.js:665, app/chat/page.js:422, app/leads/[id]/page.js (grep confirms `new EventSource`), app/control/events/page.js. So on the dashboard the browser holds 2 long-lived connections to /api/events; on a lead profile, another 2. Each handler independently parses every frame.
- **Problem:** HTTP/1.1 browsers cap ~6 connections per origin and SSE holds each open indefinitely; running 2 redundant streams per page burns half that budget against the app's own API host and doubles server-side connection load and client-side JSON parsing of identical frames. It also risks starving other XHRs to the same host.
- **Fix:** Centralize a single shared EventSource in one provider (e.g. an SSEProvider in providers.js or a small lib/events.js singleton) and have FloatingChat, dashboard, chat, and leads/[id] subscribe to a shared emitter via context/callbacks instead of each constructing its own EventSource. This collapses N connections per page to 1 and de-duplicates frame parsing.

#### `frontend-perf-7` Studio project loads up to 5000 assets in a single fetch with no pagination — MED / M effort / medium impact
- **Where:** app/studio/[id]/page.js:180 `mediaAPI.listAssets(id, { limit: 5000 })` pulls up to 5000 asset records in one request on project open, stored in `assets` state. Rendering is mitigated (shown.slice(0, visibleCount) with visibleCount=400, studio/[id]/page.js:176/498, images use loading="lazy" at line 508), but the full 5000-row JSON is fetched, parsed, and held in memory, and `shown = ... assets.filter(...)` (line 175) recomputes on every render without useMemo.
- **Problem:** A large wedding/event shoot (multi-thousand assets) front-loads a multi-MB JSON parse and a large in-memory array before the user can interact, and re-filters the whole array on each render. The rendering layer is windowed but the data layer is not, so first-load latency scales with shoot size.
- **Fix:** Page the asset fetch (e.g. limit 400 + cursor) aligned with the existing visibleCount-based 'show more' UI so network and render grow together, and wrap the `shown` filter in useMemo([assets, mediaTab]). This keeps the proven lazy-render UX while removing the 5000-row up-front payload.

#### `crm-leads-15` Leads list loads and filters the entire workspace client-side with no pagination or virtualization — MED / L effort / medium impact
- **Where:** fetchAll calls leadsAPI.getAll with only a client/platform/account filter (leads-list/page.js:734-744); the backend ORDER BY last_message_at DESC returns all rows (server.js:1493-1532). applyFilters() re-copies and re-sorts allLeads on every keystroke/filter change (732, 760-791) and the table renders every row (1140) with no windowing.
- **Problem:** Fine at a few hundred leads, but a busy multi-channel studio accumulating thousands will see slow search-as-you-type, large initial payloads, and heavy re-renders. It also means status counts and the action queue recompute over the full array on each change.
- **Fix:** Keep the snappy client-side UX for small workspaces but add server-side pagination + search params (the query already supports status/platform/assigned filters) and switch to fetching a page when allLeads exceeds a threshold; or virtualize the table rows. Debounce the search input as a quick first step (S).

#### `platform-12` WhatsApp status polls every 2 seconds indefinitely, even when fully connected — MED / S effort / low impact
- **Where:** app/whatsapp/page.js:22 `const interval = setInterval(fetchStatus, 2000)` with no clear condition; fetchStatus (line 26) hits /api/whatsapp/status with cache:'no-store' every 2s for as long as the tab is open, regardless of status==='connected'.
- **Problem:** Once connected there is nothing to poll for; a 2s no-store poll wastes server/network for an idle page. Tight polling is only needed while a QR is pending.
- **Fix:** Back off the interval: poll at 2s only while status is qr_ready/initializing, then drop to 15-30s (or stop) once connected; clear on unmount (already returned). Prefer the SSE /api/events channel the rest of the app uses for status pushes.

#### `galleries-8` Album editor load issues an N+1 query per slot — LOW / S effort / low impact
- **Where:** GET /api/media/albums/:id (media-studio.js:1846-1853) iterates pages, then for every filled slot runs a separate 'SELECT ... FROM ms_assets WHERE id = ?'. A 40-page album with 4-up grids issues ~160 single-row queries on every load and every refetch (the editor refetches after each page add/layout/reorder — studio/[id]/albums/[albumId]/page.js:87-94).
- **Problem:** Bounded but wasteful; combined with the editor's full refetch-on-every-mutation pattern it makes album editing feel sluggish on larger albums.
- **Fix:** Collect all slot asset_ids across pages, fetch them in one 'WHERE id IN (...)' query, and map in memory. Optionally switch the editor's mutations to optimistic local updates (it already does this for slots at :66) and drop the full refetch on layout/reorder.

#### `backend-perf-8` /api/cs/overview sums revenue by loading every signed contract's totals into JS — LOW / S effort / low impact
- **Where:** contracts-studio.js:443 — inside GET /api/cs/overview (432-447) revenue is computed by db.prepare("SELECT totals FROM cs_documents WHERE workspace_id=? AND status IN ('signed','completed')").all(ws).forEach(r => { revenue += Number(J(r.totals,{}).total||0); }). totals is a JSON text column parsed per row in JS.
- **Problem:** The contracts dashboard loads every signed/completed document row and JSON-parses each just to produce one revenue number. It grows linearly with closed contracts and runs on every dashboard open, when the other tiles on the same handler already use efficient GROUP BY aggregates (e.g. byStatus at 436).
- **Fix:** Persist the contract total into a dedicated numeric column (e.g. total_amount REAL) at sign/complete time, then compute revenue with a single SELECT SUM(total_amount) ... WHERE status IN ('signed','completed'). Backfill once from the existing totals JSON. This removes the per-row parse and matches the aggregate style of the rest of the overview.

#### `backend-perf-9` Public gallery portal inserts an access row on every GET with no rate limiting — LOW / S effort / low impact
- **Where:** media-studio.js:2588-2634 GET /api/media/portal/:token is unauthenticated and on every successful load inserts into ms_gallery_access (2603-2604: 'INSERT INTO ms_gallery_access (...) VALUES (...,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)'). It also loads all gallery assets unbounded (2598-2601) plus several extra queries (favCounts 2596, proofing 2608-2610, store check 2613, folders 2619) per request.
- **Problem:** Each refresh, bot crawl, or repeated client visit appends a row, so ms_gallery_access grows unbounded with no dedup/throttle, and every hit on a public link runs ~6 queries with no rate limit — an easy amplification target on a surface that faces the open internet.
- **Fix:** Throttle the access-row write (e.g. upsert one row per token per session/day instead of per request, or only record when access_token is newly seen) and apply the same lightweight rate limit used on other public token routes. Keep the asset load but consider paginating very large galleries for first paint.

#### `studio-library-9` Decisive culling persists one HTTP PUT per photo with no batching or offline buffering — LOW / M effort / low impact
- **Where:** cull/page.js:184-190 decide() does applyLocal then `await mediaAPI.cullAsset(target.id, { decision })` — one PUT per keystroke. Rapid culling (P/X held down through a 1000-photo shoot) fires ~1000 sequential PUTs. The optimistic local update is good, but there is no debounce/queue, and a transient network blip silently swallows the write (catch {} at :189) with no retry, risking lost decisions.
- **Problem:** Fast culling is the core loop; at speed it generates a request storm and any dropped request silently loses a decision (the UI shows the local optimistic state, so the user never knows). Both a scalability and a data-integrity concern on flaky connections.
- **Fix:** Buffer decisions and flush via the existing /cull/bulk endpoint (debounced, e.g. every 500ms or N decisions), with a small retry + a subtle 'saving...' indicator and an error toast on failure. Reuses mediaAPI.bulkCull. Keeps the instant optimistic UX while making persistence robust and cheap.

#### `command-center-11` No mobile/responsive handling for the control shell — LOW / M effort / low impact
- **Where:** ControlShell.js:64 uses a fixed width:232 sticky sidebar with no collapse/hamburger and no r-* responsive utilities (the rest of wappflow-web adopted an r-* mobile system per MEMORY mobile-responsive note). Wide tables (customers, support, audit, ai recent) have no horizontal-scroll affordance on small screens.
- **Problem:** On a phone/tablet the sidebar consumes a third of the viewport and tables overflow. Founders increasingly triage on mobile (the desktop app even wraps /control), so the control plane is effectively desktop-only.
- **Fix:** Add a collapsible sidebar (hamburger under a breakpoint) and wrap data tables in an overflow-x container, reusing the existing r-* responsive conventions from the main app shell for consistency.

---

## 8. Deliverable 6 — Recommended implementation order

_Sequenced so foundational/shared work lands first (so later work reuses it), then high-impact quick wins, then the integration chain, then the standards rollout, then the named high-priority features._

### Phase 0 — Truth & Integrity (dead code purge + security/money spine)
**Goal:** Make the product honest and safe before building on it: delete phantom UI, reconcile the plan/pricing/currency vocabulary with the live entitlements engine, and close the security and money-rail defects that everything downstream will ride on.
**Why now:** This is the cheapest maturity available and it is a precondition for trust. Phantom features and dead tiers actively mislead users and contradict the live engine; the security/integrity defects are not polish — they are correctness and money bugs whose blast radius dwarfs their fix cost. Later integration work (store->payment, booking->deposit, invoice ledger) must be built on verified, idempotent, workspace-scoped rails, so the spine goes first.

| Item | Effort | Impact | Why | Closes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Authenticate and workspace-scope WhatsApp status/disconnect endpoints** | M | high | /api/whatsapp/status and /api/whatsapp/disconnect have NO auth and return global, workspace-agnostic state — a cross-tenant leak and a footgun. | `platform-1` |
| **Verify Stripe webhook signatures and add payment idempotency** | S | high | The Stripe webhook accepts unsigned payloads and shop checkout has no payment-id idempotency / is over-clickable — direct money-integrity and double-charge risk. | `invoices-store-13`, `invoices-store-14` |
| **Route Mark-as-Paid through the payments rail (one ledger, reconciled)** | M | high | Manual Mark-as-Paid bypasses payments — no money record, no audit, no timeline; and two unreconciled paths to 'paid' exist. Unify on payments.settle so the ledger is the single source of truth. | `invoices-store-3`, `gap-8` |
| **Workspace-scope invoice and lead-detail queries** | S | medium | Invoice queries are user_id-scoped not workspace_id-scoped, so team-created records go missing — a data-visibility integrity bug. | `crm-clients-9` |
| **Reconcile plan/pricing/currency vocabulary to the live engine** | M | high | Dead tiers (free/starter/growth) and PKR-rendered-as-$ propagate across Command Center, Settings overlays, and plan widgets, contradicting the creator/studio/studio_plus entitlements engine. | `command-center-3`, `command-center-4`, `settings-team-2`, `platform-14` |
| **Delete dead plan/trash UI and fix broken stubs** | M | high | PlanWelcomeModal/PlanBanner/PlanChip target removed tiers and are never mounted; Empty Trash throws (leadsAPI.emptyTrash missing); /settings?tab=billing lands on a blank page. Remove or repair — phantom UI is negative trust. | `platform-2`, `platform-3`, `platform-4`, `settings-team-1` |
| **Mount the impersonation banner with an exit path** | S | high | Command Center impersonation has no visible state and no exit — a critical safety gap for an internal control plane operating cross-tenant. | `command-center-1` |

### Phase 1 — Design-System Primitive Layer
**Goal:** Ship one canonical component library (Badge/Pill, Modal, Toast, Input, EmptyState, Loading/Skeleton, confirm) plus tokenized status colors, radii, and typography — then adopt it module by module so divergence cannot recur.
**Why now:** This is the single highest-multiplier foundation. ~22 design-system findings and a long tail of per-page styling/confirm/empty/loading findings collapse the moment shared primitives exist and are adopted. Building it first means every later phase (shell, lists, integration UI, new features) reuses it for free, so we never re-pay the styling cost.

| Item | Effort | Impact | Why | Closes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Canonical status/badge system with a single lead-status color source** | M | high | The lead-status color map is redefined in 6 files with divergent values and there is no shared Badge/Pill — the same lead looks different on every screen. One source of truth + one Badge primitive fixes it everywhere. | `design-system-1`, `design-system-2`, `contracts-9`, `booking-5`, `gap-8` |
| **Shared Modal + universal confirm adoption** | L | high | 17 modals hand-roll overlay/escape logic and 6+ surfaces still fire native window.confirm/alert (including public client pages and a message telling clients to run a server command). One Modal + mandatory confirm() retires all of it. | `design-system-3`, `design-system-4`, `crm-leads-12`, `command-center-2`, `command-center-8`, `galleries-5`, `studio-library-10` |
| **Input, EmptyState, Loading/Skeleton, and unified Toast primitives** | M | high | 16 files define local input styles, 30+ bespoke empty states, ~20 loading strings across 3 spinners, and per-page toast reimplementations — all dissolve into four shared components with consistent radii and type scale. | `design-system-5`, `design-system-6`, `design-system-7`, `design-system-8`, `design-system-9`, `settings-team-11`, `crm-clients-10`, `booking-11` |
| **Retheme hard-coded dark surfaces to tokens** | S | medium | AddLeadModal, the comms sidebar, and the Storage settings page are hard-coded dark, breaking module consistency — retheme onto the new tokens as the first adopters that prove the system. | `crm-leads-1`, `comms-15`, `settings-team-13` |

### Phase 2 — One Shell & In-App Navigation
**Goal:** Collapse the four divergent shells into a single layout.js with persistent chrome, in-app (not new-tab) module switching, correct active states, contextual back/breadcrumbs, and App Router error/not-found/loading boundaries.
**Why now:** The new-tab, four-shell model is the root cause of the 'not one product' feeling and the orphaned navigation. A single shell is the canvas on which deep links, badges, and search live, so it must precede the integration and notification phases. Adding error boundaries here also protects every subsequent change from raw crash screens.

| Item | Effort | Impact | Why | Closes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Unify on a single layout.js with in-app module switching** | L | high | Four divergent shells with per-page wrapping and new-tab switching fracture the experience; one layout with client-side navigation is the spine of 'one OS'. | `navigation-ia-1`, `navigation-ia-5`, `contracts-1`, `integration-4` |
| **Fix active states, contextual back, and breadcrumbs** | M | high | Media Studio nav never shows active state, lead/client detail highlights nothing and always says 'Back to Dashboard', and Studio sub-pages have label-less back nav — basic orientation is broken. | `navigation-ia-3`, `navigation-ia-6`, `navigation-ia-7`, `navigation-ia-2` |
| **Add error.js / not-found.js / loading.js boundaries across routes** | M | high | There are ZERO App Router boundaries across 80+ routes, so any render error shows a raw crash. A shared set of boundaries is a platform-wide resilience standard. | `gap-2` |
| **Rename and unify Communications nav vocabulary** | S | medium | The NavBar mislabels team chat as 'Inbox' and the module name is inconsistent app-wide — a cheap terminology fix that belongs with the shell pass. | `comms-4` |

### Phase 3 — Platform Safety Net (Recycle Bin, Undo, Audit)
**Goal:** Build one canonical recycle-bin + undo + audit-log primitive and adopt it across every module that performs destructive actions.
**Why now:** Many 'destructive action' findings are the same single absent foundation. Two inconsistent trash systems already exist (90 vs 30 day retention, different shells); consolidating to one and extending it is far cheaper than per-module bins. This is also a named producer feature set (Recycle Bin, Undo, Audit Center) and a precondition for safe bulk actions in Phase 4.

| Item | Effort | Impact | Why | Closes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Unify the two recycle bins into one retention/restore/empty model** | L | high | Two trash systems with conflicting retention, confirm mechanisms, and empty-all support — consolidate to one canonical bin most modules can mount. | `platform-6`, `gap-7` |
| **Extend recycle-bin + undo to galleries, invoices, contracts, bookings, members/memories** | M | high | Hard deletes with no undo across galleries, invoices, contracts, bookings, and settings objects are irreversible data loss; one shared primitive covers them all. | `galleries-10`, `invoices-store-12`, `contracts-4`, `booking-9`, `settings-team-8`, `studio-video-6`, `crm-leads-16` |
| **Unify audit logging across all mutations** | M | medium | Bulk-trash/bulk-assign skip the audit that sibling mutations record, and CC audit/event filters are missing despite backend support — one audit write path + filterable Audit Center closes both. | `crm-leads-14`, `command-center-5` |
| **Cascade-or-guard deletes so leads don't orphan related rows** | M | medium | Lead permanent-delete orphans contracts, bookings, shoots, meetings, and portal rows — soft-delete + cascade rules built on the new bin prevent orphan integrity bugs. | `integration-5` |

### Phase 4 — Shared List Infrastructure & Data-Layer Performance
**Goal:** Build reusable list infrastructure (pagination, virtualization, saved views, bulk actions, sort/filter/search, caching) and add the missing DB indexes/pragmas behind it.
**Why now:** God-pages and backend data-layer performance are the same root: lists built without shared infra over unindexed, unbounded queries. Fixing the substrate once delivers the producer's Saved Views and Bulk Actions features everywhere and removes a whole class of frontend and backend perf findings. It also makes Phase 3's bulk-undo safe at scale.

| Item | Effort | Impact | Why | Closes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Add hot-path indexes and concurrency pragmas** | M | high | messages.wa_message_id and ms_assets lack indexes (full-scans on every inbound message and on workspace/storage scans), and there is no busy_timeout/synchronous pragma despite a concurrent write worker. | `backend-perf-3`, `backend-perf-4`, `backend-perf-5`, `backend-perf-1`, `backend-perf-2`, `backend-perf-6`, `backend-perf-8`, `backend-perf-9` |
| **Paginate/LIMIT all core list endpoints** | M | high | Leads, invoices, contracts, lead messages, and chat messages are fetched unbounded; Studio loads up to 5000 assets per fetch — server-side pagination is the precondition for virtualized lists. | `backend-perf-7`, `contracts-11`, `comms-12`, `frontend-perf-7`, `studio-library-7` |
| **Virtualize and memoize the canonical list component** | M | high | The leads list renders every row unvirtualized/unmemoized over an all-leads fetch; one shared virtualized list fixes the god-page class. | `frontend-perf-4`, `crm-leads-15` |
| **Server-side Saved Views + Bulk Actions as shared infra** | L | high | Saved views are per-browser localStorage and bulk moves fire one sequential request per lead; lists across Clients, Galleries, Contracts lack views/sort/search/export entirely. Build once, mount everywhere. | `crm-leads-11`, `crm-leads-8`, `crm-clients-3`, `galleries-7`, `settings-team-9`, `crm-leads-13` |
| **Kill redundant global fetches and context churn** | S | high | The NavBar refetches the entire leads table every 60s on every page just to compute a badge; PlanProvider rebuilds its context value every render; the dashboard fans out 30 per-lead calls — all cheap, high-traffic wins. | `frontend-perf-1`, `platform-8`, `frontend-perf-3`, `frontend-perf-2`, `gap-6` |

### Phase 5 — Unified Real-Time, Notifications & Universal Search
**Goal:** Consolidate to one SSE connection and one notification bus with full cross-module coverage, and ship Universal Search + Command Palette over the indexed data layer.
**Why now:** Notification/SSE inconsistency is one absent bus, not many bugs. With the single shell (Phase 2) and indexed lists (Phase 4) in place, this is where the Global Notification Center, badges, and Universal Search/Command Palette finally have a home and a backing store. These are named producer features that must sit on this infra rather than be hand-rolled.

| Item | Effort | Impact | Why | Closes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Single SSE connection consumed via the unnamed-frame switch** | M | high | FloatingChat and each page open separate EventSources (doubling connections); dashboard/booking/admin lists silently drop payment_paid, cs_signed, booking_created, ms_* events; RoomPanel and WhatsApp status poll instead of consuming SSE. | `frontend-perf-6`, `gap-5`, `booking-2`, `comms-8`, `studio-video-5`, `platform-12`, `studio-video-4` |
| **Global Notification Center with full cross-module reach** | L | high | Mentions/replies never reach the bell, the mentions inbox has an API but no UI, there is no comms unread badge, and the feed misses Studio/Media-jobs/Comms — one bus + bell closes the set. | `comms-2`, `comms-3`, `comms-5`, `comms-1`, `platform-11`, `contracts-3`, `crm-leads-10` |
| **Universal Search + Command Palette** | L | high | The standards matrix flags Universal Search missing from the NavBar; clients/contracts/contacts aren't reachable via global search and there's no command palette despite a Ctrl+K listener — build over the indexed data layer. | `crm-clients-8`, `contracts-12`, `platform-13` |

### Phase 6 — Subsystem Consolidation (one canonical implementation each)
**Goal:** Collapse the duplicated parallel subsystems — 3 reel engines, 2 album editors, 2 schedulers, 2 invoice templates, multiple AI FABs — down to one canonical implementation each.
**Why now:** Consolidation removes a whole class of divergence findings and is a precondition for the intelligence/handoff features (you can only wire Video Intelligence into one editor, only hand off from one reel engine). Doing it after the shared primitive and list layers exist means the surviving implementation is rebuilt on the canonical components, not re-forked.

| Item | Effort | Impact | Why | Closes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Collapse the three reel systems and two editors into one** | L | high | Three overlapping reel-generation systems with two divergent editors, incompatible artifacts, a dead-end Story-Engine editor with no export, and divergent transition vocabularies — one engine + one editor is the only sane base for Video Intelligence. | `studio-video-1`, `studio-video-2`, `studio-video-3`, `studio-video-9`, `studio-video-10`, `studio-video-13` |
| **Unify the two album editors and fix the ms_albums schema clash** | L | high | Two album editors with different data models/routes and an ms_albums table created with two incompatible schemas by media-studio.js and studio-ai.js — a critical data-integrity defect plus a divergence; one editor, one schema, N+1 removed. | `galleries-3`, `studio-library-2`, `galleries-8` |
| **Unify the two schedulers and the invoice templates** | L | high | Two disconnected scheduling systems with no shared model and two divergent invoice print/PDF templates — consolidate so booking and invoicing each have one canonical surface ahead of their handoffs. | `booking-7`, `invoices-store-11` |
| **Consolidate AI FABs into one assistant entry** | M | medium | Three uncoordinated floating assistants/FABs and two parallel Studio AI surfaces with no shared mental model; one entry point with consistent error/loading states. | `platform-7`, `studio-library-8`, `platform-15` |

### Phase 7 — Contact-Centric Integration Chain & Handoffs
**Goal:** Build the contact-centric data graph and the Universal Timeline so winning a lead, signing a contract, finishing a reel, and taking a booking each propagate to client/invoice/project/portfolio/timeline — and surface the handoff CTAs in-context.
**Why now:** This is the critical theme (18 findings) and the heart of 'one OS.' It depends on every prior foundation: one shell for deep links, the data layer for the graph, the notification bus for handoff signals, and consolidated subsystems to hand off from. With those in place, the integration chain becomes wiring, not rework — delivering the producer's Universal Timeline and Universal Relationship Graph.

| Item | Effort | Impact | Why | Closes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Win-a-lead -> Client promotion and a true customer-360** | M | high | Winning a deal silently leaves Clients empty, the client profile omits contracts/bookings, lifetime revenue sums one deal field, and converted clients still show the lead stepper — the contact spine is broken at conversion. | `integration-1`, `crm-clients-1`, `crm-clients-2`, `crm-clients-6`, `crm-clients-7`, `crm-clients-5` |
| **Lead/contact action hub: New Contract / Shoot / Booking / Invoice / Portal** | M | high | Lead and client detail pages are dead ends with no contract/booking/invoice/portal actions and no Invoice/Contract back-links; this CTA hub is where deep links surface. | `integration-2`, `integration-6`, `contracts-2`, `studio-library-5`, `crm-clients-4`, `invoices-store-4` |
| **Wire the missing integration links** | L | high | Contract->Booking, Booking->Project/Shoot, Booking->Calendar, Store order->Invoice/Payment, and Public booking->shoot/event are all missing — these are the broken segments of the integration chain. | `integration-7`, `booking-8`, `invoices-store-7`, `client-portal-4`, `studio-video-7` |
| **Universal Timeline & Activity Feed over the relationship graph** | L | high | Activity is fragmented (CRM timeline loads empty needing manual refresh; three overlapping activity views; booking activity indistinguishable; portfolio/reels don't log CRM activity); analytics ignores invoices/contracts/bookings. One timeline fed by the graph fixes all. | `crm-leads-5`, `crm-leads-6`, `booking-6`, `galleries-11`, `integration-8`, `studio-library-5` |

### Phase 8 — Branded, Unified Public Client Journey
**Goal:** Give every public surface consistent studio branding (including the never-rendered uploaded logo), per-studio link metadata, in-journey navigation, and actionable success states.
**Why now:** The public journey is the studio's brand in front of their clients, yet the six public pages each brand differently, none render company_logo, and shared links preview as generic WappFlow marketing. This depends on the design-system layer (Phase 1) for consistent chrome and benefits from the integration graph (Phase 7) for cross-surface links. High external impact, contained scope.

| Item | Effort | Impact | Why | Closes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Brand every public surface with the studio identity and logo** | M | high | company_logo is never rendered on any public page; the gallery (most-shared link) shows no studio name and a 'WappFlow' footer; /pay shows no brand at all — clients never see the studio they hired. | `client-portal-1`, `client-portal-2`, `galleries-4`, `gap-1` |
| **Per-studio link metadata for shareable previews** | M | high | Every shared link previews as generic WappFlow marketing copy with no per-studio Open Graph metadata. | `client-portal-3` |
| **Make the portal truly unified with actionable, navigable states** | M | high | The 'unified' portal omits booking/pay/shop, unpaid invoices aren't clickable to pay, success states are dead ends, and there's no cross-surface navigation — connect them into one journey with next steps. | `client-portal-4`, `client-portal-5`, `client-portal-6`, `client-portal-7`, `client-portal-10`, `client-portal-9` |
| **Surface and auto-share the portal link from CRM** | S | high | The portal — the best cohesion feature — is buried in Contracts Vault, absent from lead/client detail, and never auto-shared; expose and auto-generate it at conversion. | `integration-3`, `client-portal-8` |

### Phase 9 — Correctness, Standards Rollout & First-Run Guidance
**Goal:** Close the remaining data-correctness defects (timezones, slot integrity, lifecycle), roll out accessibility/a11y standards, and add first-run affordances, terminology, and feature discovery.
**Why now:** With foundations and integration done, this phase hardens correctness and lifts the floor on standards. These items are individually smaller but collectively define 'mature': correct times, no double-bookings, keyboard/screen-reader support, and guidance so users find what we built. Account recovery is included here as a real gap that doesn't fit elsewhere.

| Item | Effort | Impact | Why | Closes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Fix timezone correctness and booking slot integrity** | M | high | Booking times are studio-local-naive but rendered in the viewer's TZ and bypass the shared formatter; the double-booking guard checks exact start only, allowing overlapping bookings of differing durations. | `gap-3`, `gap-4`, `booking-4`, `booking-10` |
| **Complete invoice/booking lifecycle and admin controls** | M | high | Overdue is never computed (dead stat/filter), invoices can't be edited, email-send is fully broken, and owners can't cancel/reschedule a booking or trust the confirmation claim. | `invoices-store-1`, `invoices-store-2`, `invoices-store-5`, `invoices-store-15`, `invoices-store-10`, `invoices-store-8`, `invoices-store-9`, `booking-1` |
| **Accessibility primitives across public and onClick-heavy UI** | L | medium | Only 19 aria-labels and 34/100 files use any aria/role/alt against 1074 onClick handlers, with no keyboard/screen-reader story — bake a11y into the shared components and public pages. | `gap-10`, `client-portal-9` |
| **First-run guidance, terminology, discovery, and account recovery** | L | medium | 22 weak-guidance findings (next-best-action on detail pages, feature-discovery nudges for Booking/Print Store/Reels, consistent role labels) plus no forgot-password flow leave users stranded and features unfound. | `crm-leads-9`, `crm-leads-7`, `invoices-store-6`, `studio-video-8`, `booking-12`, `navigation-ia-9`, `settings-team-3`, `settings-team-6` |

### Phase 10 — Named Intelligence & Advanced Features
**Goal:** Land the producer's named high-priority features as thin layers over the now-complete foundations: Studio/Creator/Video Intelligence, Gallery Expiry, Lead Follow-up & Duplicate Detection, Command Center Health Dashboard & Intelligence, Workspace Branding, Keyboard Shortcuts, and Draft System.
**Why now:** These were sequenced last on purpose: each depends on foundations now in place (consolidated reel editor for Video Intelligence, the relationship graph for Creator Brain, the notification bus for Follow-up, the safety net for Drafts, the design system for Branding). Built now, they are features — not new bespoke subsystems — and they reuse everything before them.

| Item | Effort | Impact | Why | Closes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Studio Brain, Creator Brain & Video Intelligence wired into real surfaces** | L | high | Studio Brain recomputes with table writes on every cull load and Video Intelligence is a disconnected stub; with one reel editor and the graph in place, wire intelligence in cleanly and stop the redundant writes. | `studio-library-6`, `studio-video-11`, `studio-video-12`, `studio-library-9`, `studio-library-11` |
| **Gallery Expiry (named feature) and gallery editing** | M | medium | Gallery Expiry is phantom — expires_at exists but is never set/read/exposed — and galleries can't be edited after creation; deliver the named feature plus edit on the consolidated gallery surface. | `studio-library-3`, `galleries-1`, `galleries-2` |
| **Lead Follow-up & proactive Duplicate Detection** | M | medium | Named CRM features: reminders are passive with no due-time notification (now possible on the notification bus) and there's no proactive name/email duplicate detection at capture, with a dead-end duplicate-on-create error. | `crm-leads-3`, `crm-leads-4`, `crm-leads-2` |
| **Command Center Health Dashboard & Intelligence** | L | medium | Named CC features: the operational/infra Health dashboard is unbuilt, role-based UI gating is missing, and Founder Inbox signals can't be acknowledged — build over the now-clean CC vocabulary and audit infra. | `command-center-7`, `command-center-6`, `command-center-9`, `command-center-12`, `command-center-11` |
| **Workspace Branding, Keyboard Shortcuts, Draft System & multi-account WhatsApp** | L | medium | Named platform features that ride the foundations: branding tokens, app-wide keyboard shortcuts, draft persistence (chat drafts lost on channel switch), and the modern per-account WhatsApp API the plan already sells (2-5 accounts) but the UI ignores. | `comms-11`, `platform-5`, `platform-9`, `platform-10` |

## 9. Mandatory product-standards coverage matrix

| Standard | Priority | Present in | Missing where it matters | Recommendation |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Universal Search** | critical | Command-Center | CRM-Leads, CRM-Clients, Dashboard, Studio-Library, Studio-Video, Galleries, Contracts, Booking, Invoices-Store, Communications, Settings-Team | NavBar.js has zero search input (only logo+nav+bell+avatar, lines 404-625); the only real cross-object search is the platform control plane (command-center.js /cc/search consumed by ControlShell + ExportButton flow). Every product module has at best a per-page client-side filter. For the 'one cohesive OS' goal, add a global search endpoint over leads/clients/galleries/contracts/invoices/bookings and a NavBar search box (reuse the Command-Center /cc/search pattern). Note AICommandCenter (Ctrl+K) is NOT search - it POSTs to /api/ai/command and only queries CRM leads (AICommandCenter.js:75). |
| **Global Notifications** | high | CRM-Leads, CRM-Clients, Galleries, Contracts, Booking, Client-Portal, Platform | Studio-Video, Invoices-Store, Communications, Command-Center, Studio-Library, Settings-Team | The bell + persistent feed exist in NavBar (MiniNotifPanel consumes notificationsAPI.get(), NavBar.js:310,476-494) and backend notify() fires for lead-create, contract sign, bookings, gallery favourite/comment/collection. Gaps: Studio-Video render-completion only SSE-broadcasts (no notify() row), invoices/payments use broadcastToWorkspace (realtime, not the persistent feed), Comms @mentions have an API but no feed entry, Command-Center high-severity inbox items have no in-app alert. unread.js is per-viewer lead-unread localStorage tracking, NOT a notification center. Route render/payment/mention events through notify() so the bell covers all modules. |
| **Audit Logging** | high | CRM-Clients, Galleries, Contracts, Invoices-Store, Studio-Library, Studio-Video, Command-Center, Settings-Team | CRM-Leads, Booking, Communications | logAudit reaches 9 files / 54 call-sites (contracts-studio.js, media-studio.js, reel-engine.js, server.js). Coverage is inconsistent rather than absent: in CRM-Leads, create/merge/bulk-upload call logAudit but bulk-assign and bulk-trash do NOT; Booking create/reschedule/cancel call none; Communications logs no channel create/delete/pin/edit; invoices logs create but not mark-paid/update/delete. Command-Center is the gold standard (cc_audit with before/after diffs, command-center.js:210-222). Backfill logAudit on the listed mutating routes for parity. Note: audit_logs is also an enterprise feature flag (entitlements.js:58), but the writes should happen regardless. |
| **Bulk Actions** | high | CRM-Leads, Studio-Library, Galleries, Contracts | CRM-Clients, Booking, Invoices-Store, Studio-Video, Communications, Command-Center, Settings-Team | CRM-Leads is the reference (select/assign/round-robin/trash/move/export, leads-list/page.js:705,837); Studio-Library multi-selects assets for watermark/auto-edit/delete; Galleries multi-select to add-to-gallery; Contracts has bulk-SEND only (no bulk delete/status on the overview). Notably absent where it clearly matters: Clients (zero), Invoices/orders (no bulk send/mark-paid/delete), Reels list (one-at-a-time delete), Command-Center Customers (no bulk-suspend/plan-change), Team/Knowledge (one-by-one). The checkbox/selectedIds pattern already exists in 16 files - extend it to these lists. |
| **Recycle Bin** | high | CRM-Leads, Studio-Library | CRM-Clients, Galleries, Contracts, Booking, Invoices-Store, Studio-Video, Communications, Settings-Team | Exactly two bins exist: CRM leads 90-day (trash/page.js, is_deleted) and Studio media 30-day (studio/trash/page.js, deleted_at). Everything else hard-deletes despite the app clearly having the soft-delete pattern: galleries/portfolio items (immediate), contracts (hard delete backend:521-524), invoices (hard DELETE server.js:2247), video timelines (hard delete media-studio.js:2288-2295), chat messages/channels, team members, knowledge memories. Cancelled bookings vanish with no restore UI. Extend deleted_at + a trash view to contracts/invoices/galleries at minimum - these are records users will delete by mistake. |
| **Command Palette** | high | — | CRM-Leads, CRM-Clients, Dashboard, Studio-Library, Studio-Video, Galleries, Contracts, Booking, Invoices-Store, Communications, Command-Center, Settings-Team, Platform | There is NO real command palette. AICommandCenter.js owns Ctrl+K (line 44) but it is an AI chat panel that POSTs free-text to /api/ai/command and only resolves CRM leads/stats/reminders (QUICK_COMMANDS, lines 22-29) - it cannot navigate to galleries/contracts/settings or run actions like 'new invoice'. The prior pass over-credited Platform here; mark it absent. A genuine palette (fuzzy object jump + action runner) reusing a new global-search index would be the single highest-leverage 'cohesive OS' feature alongside Universal Search, and would resolve the Ctrl+K collision. |
| **Feature Discovery** | high | Contracts | CRM-Leads, CRM-Clients, Studio-Library, Studio-Video, Galleries, Booking, Invoices-Store, Communications, Command-Center, Settings-Team | Only Contracts has a rich help/discovery surface (help/page.js accordion). Everywhere else, powerful features are unlabeled icon buttons with no tour/coachmark: Leads (merge-duplicates, round-robin, WhatsApp-group), Studio (Cull, Studio AI), Booking (the booking link is buried below settings), Invoices (Print Store reachable only via a ghost button in studio/page.js:184, not in NavBar), Command-Center (~20 surfaces reachable only by URL). There IS a PlanLock/upgrade-prompt system but no positive feature-education layer. For a multi-module OS, add empty-state nudges + a first-run checklist; this is high-leverage because the product is feature-rich but undiscoverable. |
| **Workspace Branding** | high | Contracts, Invoices-Store, Galleries, Booking, Settings-Team | Client-Portal, Communications, Studio-Library, Studio-Video, Command-Center | Branding is captured in Settings (logo, company name, signature, currency) and partially applied: contracts letterhead, invoice document (invoices/page.js:39,161), portfolio (accent + 10 themes + studio name), booking public/manage pages (name + monogram). Big gaps on the surfaces clients actually see: company_settings.company_logo is shown on ZERO public surfaces; the client gallery g/[token] is hardcoded gold #c2a878 + 'WappFlow Media Studio' footer; accent color is hardcoded #6366f1/#c2a878 across portal/shop/pay/book except folio. Also no generateMetadata on any public route, so shared links preview as generic 'WappFlow' not the studio. Chat sidebar is hardcoded #1e1e2e. Surface the stored logo/accent on every public surface + add per-route generateMetadata - critical for a white-label-capable OS (white_label is a sold Studio+ feature, entitlements.js:82). |
| **Permissions** | high | CRM-Leads, CRM-Clients, Command-Center, Settings-Team | Galleries, Contracts, Booking, Invoices-Store, Studio-Library, Studio-Video, Communications | A real RBAC system exists: 4 roles + 9-permission matrix + per-member overrides (team/page.js:16-41), enforced server-side (role='user' restricted to assigned leads, server.js:1504; Command-Center requirePerm on every destructive route, command-center.js:202-207). But the matrix only governs CRM/settings/invoices/whatsapp keys - module-level enforcement is missing: Contracts has approval but no per-role send/delete gating, Studio destructive routes use a single canManage flag, anyone can delete any chat channel (chatAPI.deleteChannel, no admin check), bookings owner actions are ungated. Extend the existing permission keys to cover contracts/studio/comms actions and check them on those routes. |
| **Plan Enforcement** | high | ALL | Communications | This is genuinely platform-global and well-built: entitlements.js is the single resolver (config-as-data plan_* tables, flags, per-workspace overrides, sold-but-unbuilt guard), pricing.js does live usage + soft levels 80/90/100, MODULE_GATES gate /api/media + /api/video-ai (server.js:5434-5442), usePlan()/PlanLock/LockedOverlay gate UI, lead/contract_send/storage caps enforced on create. Two real defects, not coverage gaps: (1) Communications has no feature gate despite team_collaboration gating Team/Knowledge; (2) the plan-UX affordances are broken - PlanBanner/PlanChip/PlanWelcomeModal target dead tiers (free/starter/growth) and return null for real Creator/Studio/Studio+ users (PlanLock.js:112,161), and LockedOverlay/UpgradeCta route to a stale /settings?tab=workspace. Fix the tier strings + mount the banner, and gate Comms. Per-render AI metering on video exports is also absent. |
| **Activity Timeline** | medium | CRM-Leads, CRM-Clients, Galleries, Contracts, Booking, Invoices-Store, Communications, Command-Center | Studio-Video, Studio-Library, Settings-Team | Strong coverage: contact_history (server.js) + emitToLead (media-studio.js) mirror media/booking/invoice/contract events onto the lead timeline; contracts has cs_events, Command-Center has per-workspace platform_events. Gaps that make sense to close: Studio-Video timelines emit no per-entity history (emitToLead exists in media-studio but isn't wired to video renders), and Settings-Team logs are flat workspace lists not scoped per-member/per-document. Wire video renders through emitToLead and add per-member event scoping. |
| **Saved Views** | medium | CRM-Leads, Studio-Library | CRM-Clients, Contracts, Booking, Invoices-Store, Studio-Video, Galleries, Communications, Command-Center, Settings-Team | Only two files implement saved views, both via per-browser localStorage (leads-list/page.js wf_lead_views:41-44, and studio/[id]/page.js); they are not shared across team or devices. Every other list (Clients, Contracts overview, Bookings, Invoices, Reels, Galleries, Command-Center Customers/Health/Audit) has only fixed tabs or free-text search. Promote saved views to a server-side per-workspace concept and roll out to the high-traffic lists first (Clients, Invoices, Contracts, Command-Center). |
| **Drafts** | medium | Contracts, Studio-Video | CRM-Leads, CRM-Clients, Galleries, Booking, Invoices-Store, Communications, Studio-Library, Settings-Team | Real autosave drafts exist in Contracts (debounced save, contracts/[id]/page.js:48-55) and Studio-Video (debounced 800ms, video/[timelineId]/page.js:113-122; ms_timelines.source=ai_draft). Elsewhere 'draft' is only a status with no autosave UX: Invoices save as draft but can't be sent/edited, Galleries have a draft status but no draft UX. True data loss bugs: AddLeadModal/composer lose input on close, chat loses typed-but-unsent text on channel switch, Settings tabs discard edits on tab switch. Add an unsaved-changes guard + localStorage draft to the lead/invoice/chat composers and Settings forms. |
| **Undo** | medium | CRM-Clients, Studio-Library, Booking | CRM-Leads, Galleries, Contracts, Invoices-Store, Studio-Video, Communications, Command-Center, Settings-Team | True reversibility today is data-layer, not inline-toast: Move-to-Clients is reversible (clients/page.js:45), Studio cull edits undo (copy/paste/reset-to-original), booking cancel is a soft status flip. There is no toast-with-undo anywhere - bulk trash/move in Leads, gallery delete, contract delete (hard delete backend:521-524), invoice delete (hard DELETE server.js:2247), timeline split/delete, chat delete, and Command-Center suspend/plan-change all commit immediately. Add a 5-second optimistic undo toast on bulk/destructive actions; pair with soft-delete where currently hard-deleting. |
| **Exports** | medium | CRM-Leads, Studio-Library, Studio-Video, Command-Center, Settings-Team | CRM-Clients, Galleries, Contracts, Booking, Invoices-Store, Communications | The reusable ExportButton.js (CSV/JSON, respects filters) is wired ONLY to Command-Center (uses cc_token + /api/cc/export, lines 20-23). Module-level exports are ad-hoc: Leads CSV (leads-list:881), Studio gallery-ZIP/album-PDF/video-MP4 (export pipelines), Settings full-workspace JSON. Missing where it clearly matters: Clients (no CSV), Contracts (no list/analytics CSV - only per-doc signed PDF), Bookings (no CSV/ICS, and clients get no .ics 'add to calendar'), Invoices/payments (no CSV, single-invoice print only). Generalize ExportButton off cc_token to the main app token and add list-export endpoints for Clients/Contracts/Invoices/Bookings. |
| **Analytics** | medium | Dashboard, CRM-Leads, Contracts, Galleries, Command-Center | Booking, Studio-Library, Studio-Video, Invoices-Store, Communications, CRM-Clients | Good where present: a dedicated Dashboard/reports module (analytics is a Studio-tier feature, NavBar.js:174-175), Contracts funnel (sent->viewed->signed, analytics/page.js + backend:826-850), portfolio view_count + gallery favourite/comment counts, Command-Center platform health. Captured-but-never-surfaced data is the recurring miss: Booking has no conversion/no-show/popular-service metrics, Comms captures call_sessions/duration but never shows them, Studio has no cull-throughput/keep-rate/delivery-turnaround, Invoices has no payments dashboard (GET /api/payments + paymentsAPI exist, api.js:552-553, but no page consumes them), Clients has no financial rollup from real invoices. Build the Payments dashboard first (data already there), then booking + studio metrics. |
| **Responsive Design** | medium | CRM-Leads, CRM-Clients, Dashboard, Studio-Library, Galleries, Contracts, Booking, Invoices-Store, Client-Portal, Communications, Settings-Team | Studio-Video, Command-Center | Broadly solid per the mobile-responsive effort: r-* utility library + per-shell @media blocks, NavBar collapses to a hamburger at 768px (NavBar.js:842-848), client surfaces use clamp()/single-column. Two desktop-only holdouts: the Studio-Video editor (3-pane rail/stage/inspector + timeline, no mobile block, explicitly flagged) and the Command-Center ControlShell (fixed 232px sidebar with no collapse, tables overflow narrow screens, ControlShell.js:64). Command-Center is founder-only so it's the lower priority; add a mobile/collapsed layout to the video editor (or a clear 'open on desktop' gate) to avoid a broken experience on phones. |
| **Keyboard Shortcuts** | low | Studio-Library, Studio-Video, Communications, Platform | CRM-Leads, CRM-Clients, Dashboard, Galleries, Contracts, Booking, Invoices-Store, Command-Center, Settings-Team | keydown handlers cluster in editors/modals only: cull screen (studio/[id]/cull, comprehensive), video editor (Space/S/Del/arrows, video/[timelineId]/page.js:295-306), chat (Enter/Esc), the global confirm dialog (confirm.js), and the AICommandCenter Ctrl+K toggle. No list-level shortcuts (j/k navigation, new-record, quick-actions) anywhere in CRM/Contracts/Booking/Invoices/Command-Center. Lower priority than search/notifications; if pursued, ship a small global hotkey layer (new lead, focus search, go-to-module). |
| **Imports** | low | CRM-Leads, Settings-Team | CRM-Clients, Studio-Library, Galleries, Contracts, Invoices-Store, Booking | Only CRM-Leads has a real importer (POST /api/leads/bulk-upload with dedupe + plan cap). Knowledge supports upload/crawl but no CSV memory import. No import path for clients (they're derived from leads, so arguably N/A), shoots/galleries, contract templates/clauses, or invoices. Lowest-priority of the data standards - most modules are populated by in-app workflows, not migration. Consider a clauses/templates import for Contracts and a CSV importer if studios migrate from another CRM. Skip for Dashboard/Comms/Client-Portal/Command-Center (not record-authoring). |

## 10. Full findings appendix (by theme)

### No shared design system / component library

**`crm-leads-1` — AddLeadModal uses a hard-coded Tailwind dark theme, breaking visual consistency with the entire module**  
HIGH · M effort · high impact · consistency · _CRM — Leads & Pipeline_  
- **Evidence:** components/AddLeadModal.js:47-152 uses Tailwind classes with hard-coded dark colors: `bg-[#0f1117]`, `border-white/10`, `text-white`, `text-gray-400`, gradient `from-violet-600 to-cyan-600`. Every other surface in the module (leads-list/page.js, leads/[id]/page.js, BulkAssignModal, CreateGroupModal, MergeDuplicatesModal) uses CSS-variable inline styles (`var(--surface)`, `var(--text)`, `var(--border)`).
