- **Evidence:** Only d/[token] distinguishes 'expired' vs 'declined' vs 'not available' (d/[token]/page.js:81-86); g/shop/pay/book/portal collapse every error to one generic message (e.g. shop/page.js:39 'Shop unavailable', pay/page.js:17 'Payment not found'). Identity: the gallery persists the visitor name in localStorage (g/[token]:41,63) but shop (shop/page.js:12) and book (book/page.js:17) re-ask name/phone/email with no prefill.
- **Problem:** Failure copy, link-expiry behaviour, and how the client's name/contact are remembered differ per surface, so the journey feels stitched together. A client who already typed their name in the gallery must retype it to order prints.
- **Recommendation:** Standardize a shared 'link unavailable / expired' component and message set across the public routes, add expiry awareness to gallery/shop/pay/book where tokens can expire, and reuse the localStorage contact (wf_gallery_contact) to prefill the shop/booking name fields.

### Missing recycle-bin / undo / audit safety net

**`platform-2` — "Empty Trash" button throws — leadsAPI.emptyTrash() does not exist**  
HIGH · S effort · high impact · data-integrity · standard: Recycle Bin / Bulk Actions · _Platform Plumbing (entitlements, pricing, plan UX, notifications, confirm, AI copilots, trash, WhatsApp, API client)_  
- **Evidence:** app/trash/page.js:72 `await leadsAPI.emptyTrash();` inside handleEmptyAll. lib/api.js leadsAPI (lines 45-87) defines getTrash, restore, permanentDelete, bulkTrash — but NO emptyTrash. The button is the primary header action (trash/page.js:92).
- **Problem:** Clicking "Empty Trash" calls an undefined method -> TypeError -> caught by the generic catch (page.js:73) showing "Could not empty the trash". The headline destructive action is permanently broken; users must delete leads one-by-one.
- **Recommendation:** Add `emptyTrash: () => api.post('/leads/empty-trash')` to leadsAPI (and the backend route if missing), or change handleEmptyAll to loop permanentDelete over the loaded ids. Verify a backend endpoint exists before wiring.

**`studio-video-6` — No exports library, recycle bin, or undo — destructive actions are irreversible**  
HIGH · M effort · medium impact · data-integrity · standard: Recycle Bin / Undo · _Media Studio — Video & Reel_  
- **Evidence:** Timeline delete is a hard DELETE of ms_timelines + ms_video_exports behind a single confirm() (video/page.js:43-47; media-studio.js:2288-2295). The editor's split/delete-clip/clear-keyframes mutate doc with no history stack (page.js:206-237, 300). The rest of Studio has trash/recycle per the CRM memo.
- **Problem:** A photographer can permanently lose an edited reel (and all its renders) with one mis-click, and there is no undo for timeline edits — both out of step with the recycle-bin + safe-delete patterns elsewhere in WappFlow.
- **Recommendation:** Soft-delete timelines (deleted_at, like ms_assets which already have it) with a trash view, and add a bounded undo/redo stack (doc snapshots) in the editor wired to Ctrl+Z/Ctrl+Shift+Z alongside the existing shortcuts.

**`contracts-4` — Delete is a permanent hard-delete with no recycle bin or undo**  
HIGH · M effort · medium impact · data-integrity · standard: Recycle Bin / Undo · _Contracts Studio_  
- **Evidence:** Overview row delete uses window.confirm then csAPI.remove (page.js:116); backend DELETE hard-removes the document AND its signers, events, and approvals (contracts-studio.js:521-524). There is no trash/restore endpoint in csAPI (api.js:454-489).
- **Problem:** One mis-click (the trash icon sits inline on every row) irrecoverably destroys a signed contract along with its legal audit trail (signers, IP/timestamp events, signature hash) — the very evidence a contract module must preserve. MEMORY notes a studio trash/recycle bin already exists elsewhere in the app, so this is also an inconsistency.
- **Recommendation:** Soft-delete: add a deleted_at column, filter it out of list/vault/analytics, and add a Recycle Bin view + restore (mirror the existing studio trash pattern). Block hard-deletion of documents in signed/completed status entirely. At minimum, replace the inline confirm() with the app's useConfirm dialog used on the leads page (lib/confirm) for consistency.

**`booking-9` — No way to view or restore cancelled bookings; no audit trail**  
MED · M effort · medium impact · standards · standard: Recycle Bin / Undo + Audit Logging · _Booking & Scheduling_  
- **Evidence:** bookingAPI list excludes cancelled (booking.js:114 `status!='cancelled'`), and there is no endpoint or UI to list cancelled bookings or un-cancel them. None of the create/reschedule/cancel handlers call logAudit, even though server.js passes logAudit into peer modules like contracts-studio (server.js:5548-5549).
- **Problem:** A mis-cancel (by client via token, or future owner action) is unrecoverable from the UI, and there is no record of who/what cancelled — inconsistent with the audit/recycle standards the rest of the platform follows. Data is soft-deleted but the soft-delete is invisible, so it provides no actual recovery affordance.
- **Recommendation:** Pass logAudit into the booking module and log create/reschedule/cancel. Add a 'Cancelled' filter/section in the admin list backed by a query that includes cancelled rows, with a Restore action (status back to 'confirmed' after a fresh clash check).

**`invoices-store-12` — Invoice delete is a hard, unrecoverable DELETE with no undo/recycle**  
MED · M effort · medium impact · standards · _Invoices, Payments & Store_  
- **Evidence:** server.js:2245-2250 DELETE /api/invoices/:id runs a raw DELETE; invoices/page.js handleDelete (361-374) confirms then removes. No soft-delete column, no trash, and the delete is not audit-logged (no logAudit in the route).
- **Problem:** Financial records are permanently destroyed with one confirm, no recovery, and no audit entry — risky for AR and inconsistent with the trash/recycle pattern the app uses elsewhere (per memory: studio trash). Accidental deletes are unrecoverable and invisible.
- **Recommendation:** Soft-delete invoices (deleted_at) with a Recycle Bin and restore, and add logAudit on delete. At minimum, log the deletion for the audit trail.

**`settings-team-8` — Member removal, memory delete, and document delete are permanent with no undo/recycle bin**  
MED · M effort · medium impact · data-integrity · _Settings, Team, Onboarding & Knowledge_  
- **Evidence:** team/page.js handleRemove (line 529) -> workspaceAPI.removeMember with only a confirm(). knowledge/page.js handleDeleteMemory (207) and handleDeleteDoc (152, copy 'This cannot be undone') hard-delete via confirm(); deleting a document cascades and removes all memories extracted from it. No soft-delete/undo anywhere.
- **Problem:** Destructive admin actions rely solely on a modal confirm; an accidental member removal or document delete (cascading to its memories) is irreversible, inconsistent with the product-maturity goal of safe operations.
- **Recommendation:** Add an Undo toast (~5s, soft-delete flag) for member removal and knowledge document/memory deletion, mirroring whatever Recycle Bin pattern the rest of the app uses. At minimum soft-delete documents so the cascade to memories is recoverable.

**`galleries-10` — Gallery delete is immediate with no recycle bin, unlike assets**  
MED · M effort · medium impact · data-integrity · standard: Recycle Bin / Undo for destructive actions · _Galleries, Albums & Portfolio (client delivery)_  
- **Evidence:** Assets have a full soft-delete + Trash flow (media-studio.js:914 delete, 926 trash list, 939 restore). Galleries (DELETE not shown but unprotected) and portfolio items (removeItem → deletePortfolioItem optimistic, studio/portfolio/page.js:76) are hard-deleted with no undo and no recycle bin. Deleting a published gallery also orphans its share link, favorites, comments, and proofing.
- **Problem:** Inconsistent destructive-action safety within the same module: the app teaches users that deletes are recoverable (Trash for assets) then silently isn't for galleries/portfolio. A mis-click destroys client interaction history.
- **Recommendation:** At minimum add an undo toast on portfolio item removal and a confirm dialog on gallery delete; ideally extend the existing trash pattern to galleries (status='archived' already exists in the schema enum at media-studio.js:1259, so soft-delete is a small step).

**`platform-6` — Two recycle bins are inconsistent: retention (90 vs 30 days), confirm mechanism, and Empty-all support**  
MED · M effort · medium impact · consistency · standard: Recycle Bin / Consistency · _Platform Plumbing (entitlements, pricing, plan UX, notifications, confirm, AI copilots, trash, WhatsApp, API client)_  
- **Evidence:** CRM trash (app/trash/page.js): 90-day retention (line 39-43, banner line 103), uses the global useConfirm() for both restore and delete, has Empty-Trash. Studio trash (app/studio/trash/page.js): 30-day retention (line 11, banner line 55), uses raw `window.confirm(...)` (line 37) for purge with no confirm on nothing else, has NO empty-all, uses a bespoke toast (line 19) instead of confirm/alert.
- **Problem:** The same conceptual feature (Recycle Bin) behaves and looks different across modules — different retention windows, one bypasses the themed global dialog with a browser confirm, one lacks bulk empty. This is precisely the "pile of modules" feel the maturity effort is trying to remove.
- **Recommendation:** Unify: pick one retention policy (or document the difference in both banners), replace window.confirm in studio/trash with useConfirm({tone:'danger'}), and add a shared bulk-select + Empty-all to both. Consider a single <RecycleBin> primitive parameterized by entity type.

**`crm-leads-16` — No drafts/undo for destructive or in-progress lead actions**  
LOW · M effort · medium impact · standards · _CRM — Leads & Pipeline_  
- **Evidence:** AddLeadModal resets formData on close (AddLeadModal.js:28) with no persistence; the message composer state (newMessage) is component-local. Bulk trash/move-to-stage/move-to-clients show a success toast (leads-list/page.js:975, 1002, 1297) with no inline Undo — recovery is only via the separate Trash page.
- **Problem:** Accidentally moving 30 leads to the wrong stage or trashing the wrong selection has no quick reversal, and a half-written lead/message is lost on a misclick — friction users feel and a maturity gap.
- **Recommendation:** Add an 'Undo' action to the post-bulk toast (re-applying the previous status / restoring) and persist AddLeadModal + composer drafts to localStorage keyed by lead, restoring on reopen.

**`crm-leads-14` — Bulk-trash and bulk-assign skip audit logging that sibling lead mutations record**  
LOW · S effort · low impact · standards · _CRM — Leads & Pipeline_  
- **Evidence:** logAudit is called on create (server.js implied via merge/bulk-upload), merge (2042), bulk-upload (1588), client toggle (1906). It is NOT called in bulk-assign (1452-1462) or bulk-trash (5150-5163), which only emit SSE.
- **Problem:** Audit trail is inconsistent: who reassigned or mass-trashed 40 leads is unrecoverable, while a single merge is logged. For a multi-user CRM this is a compliance/cohesion gap.
- **Recommendation:** Add logAudit(workspaceId, userId, 'bulk_assign'|'bulk_trash', 'lead', null, { ids, assigned_to|count }) to both routes.

### Saved views, bulk actions, list parity & discoverability gaps

**`crm-clients-3` — Clients page lacks bulk actions, saved views, sort, and export that the Leads list has**  
HIGH · M effort · high impact · consistency · _CRM — Clients & Contacts_  
- **Evidence:** Leads list (leads-list/page.js) has bulk select (705,837), bulk assign/round-robin (97), bulk trash (709), Saved Views (41 wf_lead_views), Export CSV (881), merge duplicates (897). Clients page (clients/page.js, 141 lines total) has only free-text search (82) and a per-card 'move back'. Both are views over the same leads table.
- **Problem:** Two surfaces onto the same data feel like products from different teams. A user who learns to multi-select, export, and save views in Leads loses all of it the moment a contact becomes a Client — exactly the fragmentation the maturity push is trying to kill.
- **Recommendation:** Promote the Clients page to reuse the Leads list's table/selection toolkit (or at minimum add: multi-select + Export CSV + a sort control). Factor the Leads list's selection bar + export into a shared component and mount it on Clients with client-appropriate columns (revenue, last contact, contracts/bookings count).

**`crm-leads-13` — No pipeline board (kanban) view — pipeline is list + per-lead stepper only**  
MED · L effort · high impact · ux · _CRM — Leads & Pipeline_  
- **Evidence:** The only pipeline visualizations are the status-filter tabs on the list (leads-list/page.js:1101-1114) and the read/click stepper on the detail page (leads/[id]/page.js:1429-1457). There is no column/board component and no drag-to-move handler anywhere in the module.
- **Problem:** Studios think of deals as columns (New → Contacted → … → Won). The absence of a board makes stage-level triage slow (you filter one status at a time) and is a notable gap vs. the 'feels like one OS' bar, since bulk stage movement is the daily pipeline action.
- **Recommendation:** Add a board view toggle on the leads list that reuses the existing data + STATUS_META, with columns per stage and drag-to-move calling the proposed bulk-status endpoint. Additive (a view toggle), reuses existing components and colors; reserve as L effort given DnD.

**`studio-video-8` — Reels are nearly undiscoverable — one conditional ghost button**  
HIGH · S effort · medium impact · discoverability · standard: Discoverability / Feature Discovery · _Media Studio — Video & Reel_  
- **Evidence:** The only entry to the whole video module from a shoot is a 'Reels' ms-btn-ghost that renders ONLY when assets.length>0 (studio/[id]/page.js:363). The Story Engine reel is buried inside the Studio AI modal under an 'Album draft' section (page.js:724-729). There is no Reels surface on the main /studio dashboard.
- **Problem:** A first-time user with an empty shoot sees no hint the product can make video at all; even with assets, 'Reels' is a low-emphasis ghost button competing with Studio AI. The flagship video capability hides behind discovery.
- **Recommendation:** Promote a persistent 'Video / Reels' entry in the project header (even on empty shoots, leading to the empty-state hero that already exists in video/page.js:85-100), and remove the redundant Story-Engine reel block from the Studio AI modal once systems are consolidated.

**`studio-library-4` — No storage indicator in Studio — users discover quota only via a 413 mid-upload, and the error drops the usage numbers the API returns**  
HIGH · M effort · medium impact · discoverability · _Media Studio — Library, Cull & AI_  
- **Evidence:** Backend returns rich data on block: media-studio.js:571 `res.status(413).json({ error:'Storage limit reached', storage_limit:true, used_gb, limit_gb, pct })`. Frontend onUpload (studio/[id]/page.js:216-217) shows only `err.response?.data?.error` as a generic banner — used_gb/limit_gb/pct are discarded. Studio settings page (settings/page.js, 325 lines) has zero storage/quota references; storageAPI.usage (api.js:309 -> /media/storage) exists but Studio never calls it. StudioShell has no usage chip.
- **Problem:** Storage is invisible until it blocks a working photographer mid-import, and even then the message is a bare 'Storage limit reached' with no number and no upgrade path — exactly when the user needs context and a CTA. The data to do better is already in the response and an endpoint already aggregates it.
- **Recommendation:** Add a small storage meter to StudioShell (or Studio settings) reading storageAPI.usage, turning amber at warn/critical. On the 413, render used_gb/limit_gb/pct plus an 'Upgrade plan' link (the warn() copy already points to /settings/storage). Reuses storage-enforce status shape; no backend change.

**`invoices-store-8` — Store page never surfaces the actual shareable shop link**  
HIGH · M effort · medium impact · ux · _Invoices, Payments & Store_  
- **Evidence:** studio/store/page.js:37 only describes the URL pattern in prose ('/shop/<gallery-token>'). There is no gallery list and no copy-link control anywhere on the page; the shop link only renders to clients on g/[token]:202-203 when store_enabled.
- **Problem:** After configuring products the studio has no way to obtain or copy a specific gallery's shop URL to send a client. The handoff from 'I set up products' to 'share the shop' is missing, so the feature is effectively unusable without manually constructing tokens.
- **Recommendation:** Add a 'Shop links' section to studio/store listing published galleries with a copy-to-clipboard /shop/<token> button (reuse the gallery list query). Mirror the share-link pattern already used for galleries.

**`crm-leads-11` — Saved Views are per-browser localStorage — not shared across the team or even the user's other devices**  
MED · M effort · medium impact · integration · _CRM — Leads & Pipeline_  
- **Evidence:** leads-list/page.js:42-44 VIEWS_KEY = 'wf_lead_views' read/written via localStorage; saveCurrentView uses window.prompt (line 804) and writeViews to localStorage only. No server persistence.
- **Problem:** A manager who builds 'Hot — needs follow-up' can't share it with the team, and loses it on a new device or after clearing storage. For a team CRM this is a real cohesion/permissions gap, and window.prompt is inconsistent with the app's custom modals (useConfirm is imported and used elsewhere in this same file).
- **Recommendation:** Persist views to a saved_views table scoped by workspace + user with an optional 'shared' flag, served via an API; fall back to localStorage when offline. Replace window.prompt with the existing modal/confirm pattern.

**`crm-clients-8` — Clients are not reachable via global/command search; client cards hide email**  
MED · M effort · medium impact · discoverability · _CRM — Clients & Contacts_  
- **Evidence:** No global search in NavBar.js (only a settings query-param read at 335). AICommandCenter Ctrl+K quick commands (AICommandCenter.js:22-29) cover hot/new/won leads, reminders, stats — no clients command. Client card shows phone only (clients/page.js:109); email exists on the record (used in search filter at :56) but is never displayed.
- **Problem:** To find a specific client you must navigate to /clients and scroll/search there; there's no cross-app jump-to-client. And the card withholds email, a primary contact channel for studios, even though the data is present.
- **Recommendation:** Add clients to a universal search/command surface (extend AICommandCenter with a 'Show clients'/jump-to-contact action, or add a NavBar search that queries leads incl. is_client). Show email on the client card next to phone for parity with the Invoices row (invoices/page.js:504).

**`galleries-7` — Galleries list has no filter, sort, search, or saved views**  
MED · M effort · medium impact · discoverability · standard: Saved Views / Filtering · _Galleries, Albums & Portfolio (client delivery)_  
- **Evidence:** The galleries grid (studio/[id]/page.js:396-448) renders all galleries for a project ordered by created_at DESC (media-studio.js:1547) with no filter by status (draft/published), no sort, and no search. There is no project-level or cross-project gallery index — galleries only exist inside a single shoot page.
- **Problem:** A working studio accumulates many galleries per shoot and many shoots; with no way to find 'all unpublished' or 'galleries awaiting selection', the photographer must eyeball. This is the 'Saved Views' standard, absent here.
- **Recommendation:** Add lightweight status chips (All / Draft / Published / Awaiting selection) above the galleries grid as a client-side filter to start; longer term expose galleries in Universal Search. Keep it consistent with the library's existing media-tab filter pattern (page.js:458).

**`contracts-12` — Contracts are absent from the main Dashboard and any workspace-level search**  
MED · M effort · medium impact · discoverability · standard: Universal Search / Analytics surfacing · _Contracts Studio_  
- **Evidence:** dashboard/page.js imports leadsAPI/analyticsAPI but no csAPI (grep shows only analyticsAPI.get at line 745). Workspace-level universal search including contracts exists only in the Command-Center control plane (ControlShell.js:92 'Search workspaces, users, leads, contracts'), not in the operator-facing app.
- **Problem:** Signed contracts are booked revenue and awaiting-signature contracts are the hottest part of the pipeline, yet neither appears on the dashboard or in any in-app search the studio owner uses day-to-day. Discoverability of the module's state requires navigating into the separate Contracts tab.
- **Recommendation:** Add an "Awaiting signature / recently signed" widget to the dashboard (csAPI.overview already returns byStatus + activity + revenue), and include contract documents in whatever workspace search the app exposes so a user can jump to a document by title/client from anywhere.

**`settings-team-9` — No bulk actions or multi-select on Team members or Knowledge memories**  
MED · M effort · medium impact · standards · _Settings, Team, Onboarding & Knowledge_  
- **Evidence:** team/page.js renders member cards with per-row Role/Perms/Remove (lines 710-722); no select-all. knowledge/page.js memories render per-card Edit/Delete (lines 545-546) and documents per-row delete (line 416); no multi-select. The 'Auto-Learned' stat (line 310) implies memories accumulate but must be pruned one at a time.
- **Problem:** A workspace with many auto-learned memories or a large team has no way to bulk-delete or bulk-change-role - friction as workspaces grow, inconsistent with bulk-action expectations elsewhere.
- **Recommendation:** Add row checkboxes + a contextual bulk bar (Delete selected / Change role) to the Team members list and the Knowledge memories grid, reusing the CRM leads bulk-action component if one exists.

**`settings-team-12` — Profile page is thin and account security is split/undiscoverable (no 2FA, password lives elsewhere)**  
MED · M effort · medium impact · discoverability · _Settings, Team, Onboarding & Knowledge_  
- **Evidence:** profile/page.js edits only full_name, phone, bio, avatar (form line 30); email and role are read-only (lines 220, 296-301). Password change lives in settings PasswordTab (line 1521); there is no 2FA, session management, or per-user notification prefs on Profile.
- **Problem:** A user looking to secure their account goes to 'My Profile' but finds only name/phone/bio; password is buried in Settings > Change Password and 2FA does not exist, so account security is undiscoverable and split across surfaces.
- **Recommendation:** Add a 'Security' section to Profile (or at least a Change Password card linking to settings?tab=password) and consolidate per-user preferences there, so the two halves of 'my account' connect.

**`command-center-6` — No role-based UI gating — limited-role admins see actions they cannot perform**  
MED · M effort · medium impact · discoverability · _Command Center (internal platform control plane — /control + /api/cc/*)_  
- **Evidence:** /cc/me returns permissions (command-center.js:263-265) and ControlShell stores admin (ControlShell.js:40) but grep shows no page reads admin.permissions/adminPerms. Routes enforce requirePerm server-side (e.g. impersonate/plans/overrides at command-center.js:421-455), returning 403; the UI surfaces that only via alert() (customers/[id]:21).
- **Problem:** A support- or finance-scoped admin sees Suspend/Impersonate/Change-plan buttons that fail with a raw 'Missing permission' alert on click. This is poor discoverability and an inconsistent permission story (enforced on backend, invisible on frontend). The whole nav is also shown regardless of role.
- **Recommendation:** Read req permissions from /cc/me into a shared context and hide/disable action buttons (and optionally nav items) the current admin lacks, mirroring the requirePerm map. This makes the role model visible and removes dead-click 403s.

**`booking-12` — Discoverability: the booking link is buried below all settings with no feature-discovery nudge**  
LOW · S effort · medium impact · discoverability · _Booking & Scheduling_  
- **Evidence:** The shareable URL bar only renders when `url` already exists (bookings/page.js:48), i.e. after the first save. A brand-new studio lands on a config form with no link, no example, and no callout that this is how clients self-book; the link, once present, sits above a wall of services/availability/rules/intake cards. There is no coachmark or empty-state CTA pointing here from Dashboard or Leads.
- **Problem:** First-time users may not realize they have a public booking page or where to find/share the link, so the module's core value (clients self-scheduling into the pipeline) goes unused. The strong public page never gets traffic.
- **Recommendation:** Show the link bar (with a 'Save to activate' state) at the top even before first save, add a one-line 'Share this link with clients to let them self-book' helper, and add a discovery entry point (e.g. a Dashboard card or an empty-Leads CTA: 'Let clients book themselves').

**`platform-13` — AICommandCenter keeps an outside-click + Ctrl+K listener but no in-product discoverability label**  
LOW · S effort · medium impact · discoverability · standard: Feature Discovery / Keyboard Shortcuts · _Platform Plumbing (entitlements, pricing, plan UX, notifications, confirm, AI copilots, trash, WhatsApp, API client)_  
- **Evidence:** AICommandCenter.js:95-128 renders only a 52px Sparkles FAB with a hover-only tooltip 'AI Assistant · Ctrl+K' (opacity:0 until hover, line 122-124). It sits directly above the green FloatingChat FAB (NavBar.js:712-713).
- **Problem:** A first-time user has no persistent cue that this is a CRM command palette or that Ctrl+K opens it; the tooltip only appears on hover and the two stacked FABs are easy to confuse. The platform's most powerful navigation tool is nearly invisible.
- **Recommendation:** Add a small persistent label/kbd hint near the FAB or a one-time coachmark on first login, and a Ctrl+K affordance in the NavBar search area. Differentiate it visually from the WhatsApp FAB.

**`comms-13` — Icon-only header actions (search/pins/huddle) hurt first-time discoverability**  
LOW · S effort · medium impact · discoverability · _Communications (Team Chat + Project Rooms + Huddles/Calls + Floating WhatsApp Chat)_  
- **Evidence:** The channel header search and pins buttons are 34px icon-only squares with only title attributes (page.js:923-930); thread/pin/react message actions are also icon-only (MessageBubble 311-333). There are no coachmarks or labels for huddles, threads, or rooms anywhere.
- **Problem:** A first-time user is unlikely to discover threads, pins, voice notes, or huddles — the features that differentiate this from the old basic chat. The 'maturity' goal depends on people actually finding what's already built.
- **Recommendation:** Add short text labels on the header actions at wider widths (icon+label, collapse to icon on mobile), and a one-time feature-discovery tip pointing at threads/huddle the first time a user opens /chat. Reuse whatever coachmark pattern other modules use for consistency.

**`terminology-1` — test**  
LOW · S effort · low impact · terminology · _terminology_  
- **Evidence:** test
- **Problem:** test
- **Recommendation:** test

**`studio-library-11` — Library grid has no keyboard shortcuts, command palette, or saved views despite the cull screen setting a high bar**  
LOW · M effort · low impact · consistency · _Media Studio — Library, Cull & AI_  
- **Evidence:** studio/[id]/page.js has only the Lightbox keydown (arrows/Esc/space, :36-45); the library grid itself has no shortcuts for select-all, filter, delete, or upload. The cull screen by contrast has a full shortcut map. No saved cull filters (cull FILTERS reset to 'all' each visit) and no command palette anywhere in Studio.
- **Problem:** Power-user ergonomics are inconsistent within the same module: the cull screen rewards keyboard use richly while the library — where bulk operations (watermark/auto-edit/delete/add-to-gallery) live — is mouse-only. This inconsistency makes the module feel like two products.
- **Recommendation:** Add a minimal library shortcut set (A select-all-visible, U upload, Del trash selected, / focus search, arrow-driven selection) and persist the last cull filter/sort per project in localStorage (ms-prefs already exists). A command palette is optional/nice-to-have; the per-screen shortcut parity is the higher-value fix.

### Duplicated / divergent subsystems within a module

**`studio-library-2` — ms_albums table is created with two incompatible schemas by media-studio.js and studio-ai.js**  
CRIT · M effort · high impact · data-integrity · _Media Studio — Library, Cull & AI_  
- **Evidence:** media-studio.js:1345 `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ms_albums (... spec, cover_asset_id, pdf_status, pdf_storage_key, pdf_pages, pdf_built_at ...)`. studio-ai.js:36 `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ms_albums (... title, page_count, spec, status, created_at)` — NO pdf_* columns, ADDS page_count. Both INSERT into the same table with different column sets (studio-ai.js:176 inserts page_count; media-studio.js album route at :1820 reads cover_asset_id/pdf_*). Two UI surfaces read it: /studio/[id]/album/[albumId] uses studioAiAPI.getAlbum, /studio/[id]/albums/[albumId] uses mediaAPI.getAlbum.
- **Problem:** Because CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS no-ops if the table already exists, whichever module mounts second silently gets a table missing the columns it needs. studio-ai inserts rows without pdf_* fields that the media-studio album/PDF pipeline expects (and vice-versa: page_count may not exist for media-studio's CREATE). Albums created in one surface are malformed/invisible in the other, and a column-missing INSERT/SELECT can throw at runtime. This is a latent data-corruption + crash risk and a clear cohesion failure (two album engines pretending to be one).
- **Recommendation:** Pick one canonical ms_albums schema (the media-studio.js superset) and have studio-ai.js use safeAlter-style additive ALTERs for page_count instead of a competing CREATE; OR rename the studio-ai table to ms_ai_albums. Then unify the two album editor routes onto one. No data migration needed if you ALTER to add the union of columns. Backward-compatible.

**`studio-video-2` — Story-Engine reel editor is a dead end — no render or export**  
CRIT · M effort · high impact · ux · standard: Workflow completeness (3-question test: 'what next?') · _Media Studio — Video & Reel_  
- **Evidence:** reel/[reelId]/page.js has only ChevronUp/Down reorder, duration/transition/motion inputs and a 'Save reel' button (line 40, 51). There is no export/render action and video-ai.js exposes no render endpoint for ms_reel_plans (only GET/PUT). The page text claims 'The render uses your existing video engine' (line 53) which is false.
- **Problem:** From the Studio AI modal, 'Generate reel plan' -> 'Edit timeline ->' (page.js:729) lands the user in an editor where the only outcome is a saved plan they can never turn into a video. This is a broken promise and a workflow that cannot complete.
- **Recommendation:** Either (a) add a 'Render' button that converts the ms_reel_plans plan into an ms_timelines EDL (reuse reel-engine.planToTimeline) and redirects to the real editor/export, or (b) retire this screen entirely and point Studio AI's reel button at the Auto-reel/AI-draft path that already produces a renderable timeline.

**`studio-video-1` — Three overlapping reel-generation systems with two divergent editors**  
CRIT · L effort · high impact · architecture · standard: Consistency / single source of truth · _Media Studio — Video & Reel_  
- **Evidence:** video-ai.js mounts /api/video-ai/projects/:id/reel writing ms_reel_plans (separate table, separate template lib ms_template_library) opened at reel/[reelId]/page.js; media-studio.js mounts /templates/:id/apply + /ai-drafts (video-templates.js PACKS) AND reel-engine.js mounts /reel-render — both writing ms_timelines opened at video/[timelineId]/page.js. server.js:5544 + 5579 mount video-ai and reel-engine; media-studio.js:2139/2199 add the third path.
- **Problem:** The same user goal ('make an AI reel from this shoot') has three backends, two database schemas (ms_reel_plans vs ms_timelines), two template libraries (7 niche slot-templates in video-ai.js vs 12 Creative Packs in video-templates.js), and two editors of wildly different capability. This is the opposite of 'one operating system' — it's three half-products sharing a name.
- **Recommendation:** Pick ms_timelines + the real editor as the single source of truth. Make the Story Engine (video-ai.js) emit an ms_timelines EDL via planToTimeline-style conversion (reel-engine.js already does exactly this) and route its UI to /video/[timelineId]. Deprecate /reel/[reelId] and ms_reel_plans, or convert-on-open. Consolidate the two template libraries into video-templates.js PACKS.

**`studio-video-3` — Two AI reel entry points on the Reels list produce incompatible artifacts**  
HIGH · M effort · high impact · consistency · standard: Consistency / terminology · _Media Studio — Video & Reel_  
- **Evidence:** video/page.js header offers both 'Auto-reel' (reelAPI.render -> reel-engine.js, builds a story-arc timeline and immediately renders, line 56-64) and 'AI drafts' (mediaAPI.generateAiDraft -> video-ai-drafts.js, builds a pack-style editable timeline, line 159-166). Both claim to use 'best shots / Track-0 scores' but use different ranking (reel-engine buildPlan hero/energy vs video-ai-drafts rankMedia quality/cull/faces) and different output (auto-rendered vs editable draft).
- **Problem:** Two buttons inches apart both say 'AI builds a reel from your best shots' yet behave differently and rank differently, with no explanation of when to use which. Users cannot predict the result, undermining trust and the feeling of one coherent tool.
- **Recommendation:** Merge into one 'AI reel' affordance: AI drafts (editable) as the primary, with an 'and render now' option that internally calls the same builder then enqueues an export. Unify the two ranking functions (rankMedia vs buildPlan) so 'best shots' means one thing across the module.

**`settings-team-5` — Workspace administration is split between Settings > Workspace tab and /team (duplicate data, double the clicks)**  
HIGH · M effort · high impact · consistency · _Settings, Team, Onboarding & Knowledge_  
- **Evidence:** settings WorkspaceTab (lines 1400-1514) calls workspaceAPI.get() and renders role banner + workspace-name editor + read-only top-5 members + 'Manage Team & Permissions ->' to /team. team/page.js (lines 478-500) also calls workspaceAPI.get() and renders the full member list + invite + permissions + logs. Workspace NAME editing exists only in Settings; member management only in /team. The Settings sidebar additionally has a 'Team & Workspace' shortcut (lines 2441-2449).
- **Problem:** Two screens fetch the same workspace payload and overlap; an admin who wants to rename the workspace AND add a member must visit both, and there are three entry points (Workspace tab, sidebar shortcut, /team) for one concern.
- **Recommendation:** Pick one home: move workspace-name (and future branding) into /team as a 'Workspace' tab beside Members/Permissions/Logs, and make the Settings 'Workspace' tab a thin summary card linking to /team. Removes the duplicate workspaceAPI.get and the split mental model.

**`galleries-3` — Two divergent album editors with different data models, routes, and entry points**  
HIGH · L effort · high impact · architecture · standard: One canonical workflow per task · _Galleries, Albums & Portfolio (client delivery)_  
- **Evidence:** studio/[id]/albums/[albumId]/page.js uses mediaAPI (/media/albums/* → ms_albums + ms_album_pages, page/slot model, print-PDF export) and is reached from the shoot page 'Albums' button (page.js:362). studio/[id]/album/[albumId]/page.js uses studioAiAPI (/studio-ai/albums/* → spec.spreads spread/grid model, no PDF) and is reached only from the Studio AI panel 'Open editor →' (page.js:721). Confirmed distinct in lib/api.js:415-424 vs 516-518. Singular 'album' vs plural 'albums' in the URL is the only distinguishing signal.
- **Problem:** This is the single biggest cohesion violation in the module: 'design an album' means two unrelated tools depending on the click path, with no cross-navigation, no shared data, and no indication that the other exists. It directly contradicts the 'one operating system' goal and will confuse users and future maintainers.
- **Recommendation:** Pick the page/slot print-PDF album (ms_albums) as the canonical product since it ships a deliverable. Make the Studio AI panel generate INTO an ms_album (autofill keepers/spreads as pages) and route 'Open editor →' to /studio/[id]/albums/[albumId]. Deprecate the /studio-ai/albums spread editor (or fold its grid layouts in as page templates). Unify terminology and the route to the plural 'albums'.

**`booking-7` — Two disconnected scheduling systems with no shared model or cross-link**  
HIGH · L effort · high impact · architecture · _Booking & Scheduling_  
- **Evidence:** This module (public /book/[slug], backend/booking.js, bookings table) and ScheduleMeetingModal.js (Google Meet via /leads/:id/meetings + Calendly link, server-side meetings) are entirely separate. The modal reads /integrations/status (ScheduleMeetingModal.js:40) and posts to /leads/:id/meetings (line 63); it never touches booking_settings or the bookings table. Nav only surfaces 'Bookings' (NavBar.js:173); the modal is reachable only from inside a lead (leads/[id]/page.js:1241).
- **Problem:** For a product trying to feel like ONE operating system this is the most jarring seam: a self-serve booking link and an internal 'schedule a meeting' that share no availability, no calendar, no conflict-checking, and no list. A studio could public-book a slot and then Google-schedule a meeting on top of it with zero awareness. Users will be confused about which 'scheduling' is the real one.
- **Recommendation:** Unify under one Scheduling surface over time: feed Google-Calendar busy times into computeSlots so the public link respects real calendar conflicts, and surface ScheduleMeetingModal meetings in the same Bookings list. Short term (additive, no rewrite): on the lead detail, show the lead's bookings alongside meetings, and have ScheduleMeetingModal honor booking conflicts. This is the highest-leverage cohesion fix in scope.

**`platform-7` — Three uncoordinated floating assistants/FABs with different identities and no shared entry**  
MED · L effort · high impact · consistency · standard: Command Palette / Workspace Branding · _Platform Plumbing (entitlements, pricing, plan UX, notifications, confirm, AI copilots, trash, WhatsApp, API client)_  
- **Evidence:** NavBar.js:712-713 renders AICommandCenter (FAB at bottom:90,right:24,z998, Sparkles, indigo) + FloatingChat (FAB at bottom:24,right:24,z9000, MessageSquare, green) on every CRM page. StudioShell.js:136 renders StudioCopilot (FAB bottom:22,right:22,z380, Sparkles, accent) on studio pages. AICommandCenter hits /api/ai/command; StudioCopilot hits /api/media/copilot — two different AI brains.
- **Problem:** The product presents two different "AI assistant" buttons depending on which section you are in (different brand, icon, position, capabilities), plus a third green chat FAB stacked directly below the CRM one. Users cannot build a single mental model of "the assistant", and the two Sparkles FABs at nearly identical positions look like a bug.
- **Recommendation:** Define one assistant identity + entry point (single FAB + Ctrl+K) that routes to the right brain by context (CRM vs Studio) behind the scenes, and give FloatingChat a distinct role/position so it does not read as a duplicate. At minimum, standardize FAB position/z-index/label across the three.

**`studio-video-10` — Editor exposes effects/transitions in the UI that the engine renders, but the client transition picker omits engine capabilities (crossDissolve/slide/push/zoom/blur)**  
MED · S effort · medium impact · consistency · standard: Consistency (UI vs engine capability) · _Media Studio — Video & Reel_  
- **Evidence:** buildExportCommand handles crossDissolve via alpha and treats fade/crossDissolve as crossfades (video-engine.js:230, 297-306), and the inspector hint tells users 'Fade/Dissolve crossfade when clips overlap' (page.js:692) — but the client TransitionPicker only offers none/fade/dipToBlack/dipToWhite (video-constants.js:38-41), so 'crossDissolve' is unreachable from the UI despite being the documented overlap behaviour and used by reel-engine.js (line 40).
- **Problem:** Users are told dissolves exist and the engine + Auto-reel use them, yet they cannot select crossDissolve (or slide/push/zoom) on a clip. The UI underexposes the engine, making hand edits weaker than the AI output.
- **Recommendation:** Add crossDissolve (and any other engine-supported, preview-able transitions) to the client TRANSITIONS list with a CSS/JS preview, or document why only dips are user-selectable. Keep the client list a strict subset that the preview can actually show.

**`invoices-store-11` — Two divergent invoice print/PDF templates**  
MED · S effort · medium impact · consistency · _Invoices, Payments & Store_  
- **Evidence:** invoices/page.js:24-112 buildInvoiceHTML renders a premium branded invoice (logo, gradient, status pill). leads/[id]/page.js:327-344 handlePrint writes a totally different bare-bones HTML invoice (plain table, no logo/branding/status).
- **Problem:** The same invoice prints differently depending on whether you print it from the lead's create modal vs the invoices page, so customers can receive inconsistent documents. It also duplicates invoice-rendering logic that will drift.
- **Recommendation:** Extract buildInvoiceHTML into lib (e.g. lib/invoiceDoc.js) and use it from both the invoices page and the lead InvoiceModal print so there is one canonical invoice document.

**`studio-library-8` — Two parallel AI surfaces (library Studio AI modal vs cull Top picks/Studio Brain) with no shared mental model or entry point**  
MED · M effort · medium impact · discoverability · _Media Studio — Library, Cull & AI_  
- **Evidence:** Library exposes AI via a small ghost 'Studio AI' button (studio/[id]/page.js:477) opening StudioAIModal (selections/album/reel + 'Re-score project'). The cull screen exposes a different AI vocabulary: 'Top picks' (cull/page.js:511), per-photo hero/quality/scene/face/smile in the info panel (:644-680), 'Studio Brain' recTip (:438), and AI auto-enhance. Auto-edit-to-house-style lives only on library multiselect (:470). These are different doors to overlapping intelligence with inconsistent naming ('Studio AI' vs 'Studio Brain' vs 'AI suggests' vs 'Top picks').
- **Problem:** A first-time user cannot form one model of 'what the AI does here'. The same underlying ms_asset_scores power selections, top-picks, auto-edit and per-photo hints, but they are scattered across two screens under three names with no cross-references (e.g. cull never links to Studio AI selections; the library Studio AI never points back to cull). It reads as a pile of AI features, not one assistant.
- **Recommendation:** Standardize one name and one entry affordance ('Studio AI') used identically in library + cull, with consistent iconography (Wand2). Add cross-links: from cull Top picks, a 'Generate a Best-Of gallery' shortcut into the same selection engine; from the Studio AI modal, a 'Review in cull' deep-link. Align the 'Studio Brain' label with whatever single name you choose. Mostly copy/IA, low risk.

**`studio-video-9` — Transition & motion vocabularies diverge across the three systems**  
MED · M effort · medium impact · terminology · standard: Terminology / consistency · _Media Studio — Video & Reel_  
- **Evidence:** video-engine.js TRANSITIONS = [fade, crossDissolve, slide, push, zoom, blur, dipToBlack, dipToWhite] (line 42); the editor's client TRANSITIONS only exposes [none, fade, dipToBlack, dipToWhite] (video-constants.js:38-41); the Story-Engine editor uses ['cut','fade','whip'] (reel/[reelId]/page.js:10); video-ai.js system templates use transitions 'cut'/'whip'/'fade' (line 47-55) that the renderer does not implement (buildExportCommand only handles the engine set). Motion is 'kenburns/pan/static' in one place, 'alt/zoomIn/zoomOut/pan' in another (video-templates.js:40).
- **Problem:** The same concept has three different label sets, and the Story-Engine templates reference transitions ('whip','cut') the actual ffmpeg engine cannot render, so even if its plans could render they'd silently degrade. Inconsistent terminology makes the module feel like separate tools.
- **Recommendation:** Define one transition/motion enum (the video-engine.js set) and have every template library, editor and constants file import/mirror it. Map legacy 'cut'->none and 'whip'->a real engine transition during consolidation.

**`studio-video-11` — Video Intelligence is a disconnected score-weighting stub, not wired into the editor**  
MED · L effort · medium impact · architecture · standard: Video Intelligence (current state vs gap) · _Media Studio — Video & Reel_  
- **Evidence:** video-ai.js /cull ranks whole video assets by weighted scores (best_moments/best_clips/etc, line 95-110) and /story builds a beat list, but neither produces scene-level in/out points; buildPlan/generateStory pick whole assets with fixed role durations (reel-engine.js ROLE_DUR, video-ai.js per-beat). The editor trims clips manually with no AI 'best moment' assist. No scene_cut/action scores are consumed to auto-trim (the comment in video-ai.js:7 lists them as 'arriving via Track-0' but they are not used to set clip in/out).
- **Problem:** The promised 'Video Intelligence' (auto-pick the best 3 seconds of a 2-minute clip) does not exist at clip level — the AI only orders whole assets. The gap between the marketing ('best moments') and the behaviour (whole-clip ranking) is large, and the cull endpoint output is not surfaced in the timeline editor at all.
- **Recommendation:** Do not build now; document the gap. The minimal real step is to consume per-segment video scores (scene_cut/motion) to set clip in/out when a video is added to the timeline, and surface /video-ai/cull results as a 'suggested moments' rail in the editor rather than an unused endpoint.

**`galleries-9` — Duplicated, hand-rolled export-polling logic across three client surfaces**  
LOW · M effort · medium impact · consistency · standard: DRY shared interaction patterns · _Galleries, Albums & Portfolio (client delivery)_  
- **Evidence:** Near-identical setInterval(2000)/tries>40 polling for async exports is reimplemented in g/[token]/page.js:102-108 (ZIP), studio/[id]/page.js:322-329 (gallery ZIP), and studio/[id]/albums/[albumId]/page.js:103-108 (album PDF). Each has subtly different state shapes ({status,url} vs exports map) and the same magic numbers.
- **Problem:** Three copies of the same fragile polling loop drift independently (e.g. different error handling, different timeout messaging), which is exactly the kind of inconsistency the maturity push targets.
- **Recommendation:** Extract a shared usePollingExport(startFn, statusFn) hook (or a small pollUntilReady util) and use it in all three places so timeout, error, and ready handling are identical. Reuses existing endpoints; no API change.

**`studio-video-13` — Editor playback is approximate and can desync; no real overlap crossfade in preview**  
LOW · M effort · low impact · ux · standard: UX (WYSIWYG fidelity) · _Media Studio — Video & Reel_  
- **Evidence:** Preview advances the playhead via requestAnimationFrame delta accumulation (page.js:141-151) and only ever shows the single top-most clip (activeClip, line 154), so on overlapping clips the preview hard-cuts while the export crossfades (engine alpha path). The inspector even tells users overlaps crossfade (page.js:692) but the preview never shows it.
- **Problem:** What-you-see is not what-you-get at clip overlaps and transitions, so users tune crossfades blind and must re-render to judge them. Minor but erodes confidence in the editor.
- **Recommendation:** Render the two overlapping clips with opacity interpolation during the overlap window in the stage (mirror the engine's alpha crossfade), at least for fade/crossDissolve. Lower priority than the structural findings.

### Phantom / dead / broken features

**`command-center-1` — Impersonation banner is never mounted — no visible state and no exit path during impersonation**  
CRIT · S effort · high impact · ux · _Command Center (internal platform control plane — /control + /api/cc/*)_  
- **Evidence:** components/ImpersonationBanner.js defines the banner (reads cc_impersonating, exposes Exit), and app/impersonate/page.js:30-32 sets cc_impersonating/cc_imp_name/cc_prev_token then redirects to /dashboard. But grep for 'ImpersonationBanner' across all of wappflow-web/src returns ONLY its own definition — it is imported in no layout/provider (not app/layout.js, app/providers.js, nor any dashboard shell).
- **Problem:** After an admin clicks Impersonate (customers/[id]:46) they are dropped into the customer's account with zero indication they are impersonating and no Exit button. Their own admin session is stashed in localStorage (cc_prev_token) with no UI to restore it — the only recovery is manually clearing localStorage. This is a safety, audit-clarity, and trust failure for a cross-tenant control plane.
- **Recommendation:** Render <ImpersonationBanner/> in the root app layout (app/layout.js) or providers so it shows whenever cc_impersonating==='1'. Also add a 'storage' event listener (or re-check on route change) so it reacts within the SPA, and pass the real mode so the 'read-only (writes blocked)' text reflects write mode.

**`settings-team-1` — NavBar 'Plan & billing' links to /settings?tab=billing but no such tab exists - lands on a blank page**  
CRIT · S effort · high impact · integration · _Settings, Team, Onboarding & Knowledge_  
- **Evidence:** NavBar.js lines 64, 589, 798 all push '/settings?tab=billing'. settings/page.js TABS (line 55) defines the id as 'plan' not 'billing', and the render switch (lines 2477-2494) has no activeTab==='billing' branch. settings/page.js line 2373 does `if (params.get('tab')) setActiveTab(params.get('tab'))` with no alias/validation, so setActiveTab('billing') matches nothing and the content area renders empty.
- **Problem:** The most common path to billing (account menu 'Plan & billing', the plan badge, the founder badge) deep-links to a tab id that does not exist, so users see Settings with a blank right pane and no hint to click 'Plan & Billing' in the sidebar.
- **Recommendation:** Either rename the tab id 'plan' to 'billing' (and update TABS + the 3 NavBar pushes consistently) OR add an alias map in the settings useEffect: const ALIAS={billing:'plan'}; const t=params.get('tab'); if(t) setActiveTab(ALIAS[t]||t). Also fall back to a valid default when the requested tab id is unknown.

**`invoices-store-1` — Send invoice via Email is completely broken (no API method, no backend route)**  
CRIT · M effort · high impact · integration · _Invoices, Payments & Store_  
- **Evidence:** invoices/page.js:274 `await invoicesAPI.sendEmail(invoice.id, { to, subject, message })` and the per-row Send button at 511 open SendInvoiceModal; but lib/api.js:178-184 invoicesAPI = { getAll, getById, create, update, delete } has NO sendEmail; grep of backend (server.js, payments.js) finds NO invoice email/send route.
- **Problem:** The headline 'Send via Email' action and the list's per-row send icon both throw a TypeError ('invoicesAPI.sendEmail is not a function'). Invoices literally cannot be delivered to customers from the product — the core invoice workflow (create -> send -> pay) is severed at 'send'. handleEmailSent (376) even optimistically flips draft->pending, so the UI lies about a send that never occurred.
- **Recommendation:** Add a backend route POST /api/invoices/:id/send-email that renders the existing buildInvoiceHTML-equivalent server side, optionally mints a pay link, emails via the existing mail transport, then sets status pending + logAudit + addContactHistory; add invoicesAPI.sendEmail in api.js mapping to it. Until then, the modal should not exist.

**`studio-library-1` — Cull "Top picks" spotlights the AI's recommendation but cannot apply it — the /cull/bulk endpoint is never called**  
HIGH · S effort · high impact · ux · _Media Studio — Library, Cull & AI_  
- **Evidence:** cull/page.js:175-179 computes `recommended` (top keepRate% by hero score); :511-514 renders the "Top picks" toggle that only highlights; the recommendation banner text (recTip from brainsAPI.recommendations) literally says "auto-flag the bottom X% by hero score for a faster first pass." Yet there is NO button to act on it. mediaAPI.bulkCull (api.js:371) and POST /api/media/projects/:id/cull/bulk (media-studio.js:1004) are fully implemented and validated but `grep bulkCull` across the studio web tree returns zero call sites.
- **Problem:** The single biggest speed lever in the cull workflow — "keep the AI's top picks in one click, then refine" — is built on the backend, promised in the UI copy, and visually teased, but the photographer must still arrow through every photo pressing P. The feature stops one button short of paying off.
- **Recommendation:** Add a "Keep top picks" action next to the Top picks toggle (and a "Reject the rest") that calls mediaAPI.bulkCull(id, Array.from(recommended), 'keep'), then applyLocal-updates decisions and advances. Keep it advisory/reversible (decisions already restore individually). Reuses the existing endpoint; ~half a day.

**`studio-video-4` — Render completion produces no persistent notification; finished MP4 is unrecoverable if the modal closes**  
HIGH · S effort · high impact · integration · standard: Global Notifications / Drafts recoverability · _Media Studio — Video & Reel_  
- **Evidence:** Worker on done only calls broadcastToWorkspace(ws,'ms_video_export',{status:'done',url}) (media-worker.js:806-808) — no notifications row. ExportModal polls getVideoExport every 1.5s and shows the download only inside the modal (video/[timelineId]/page.js:901-961). There is no exports-history UI; GET /timelines/:id/exports exists (media-studio.js:2321) but nothing in the web app renders it.
- **Problem:** Renders run in the background 'so you can keep working' (the modal literally says so, line 941), but if the user navigates away or closes the modal, the completed reel has no notification and no surface to retrieve it — the export is effectively lost until re-rendered.
- **Recommendation:** On export done, write a global notifications row (reuse notify() as galleries/bookings do, media-studio.js:2670) linking to the timeline. Add a small 'Renders' list in the editor (consume GET /timelines/:id/exports) showing past exports with download links.

**`galleries-1` — Gallery Expiry is completely unimplemented despite a reserved schema column**  
HIGH · M effort · high impact · data-integrity · standard: Gallery Expiry / Link lifecycle · _Galleries, Albums & Portfolio (client delivery)_  
- **Evidence:** ms_galleries.expires_at is declared at media-studio.js:1262 but grep shows it is the ONLY reference — it is never written (PUT /galleries/:id at :1577 whitelists only title/visibility/status/settings/password; publish at :1638 never sets it) and never read. loadPublishedGallery (:2584) and the portal GET (:2588) have no expiry check, so a link lives forever. CreateGalleryModal (studio/[id]/page.js:74) has no expiry control, and g/[token] has no 'expired' state (contrast d/[token]:83 which renders 'Link expired').
- **Problem:** This is the explicitly named high-priority planned feature for this module, and the studio business model (deliver, then nudge re-purchase/archival) depends on it. Right now galleries are permanent public links with no control, which is both a storage/cost and a sales-leverage gap.
- **Recommendation:** Wire the existing column end-to-end: add an optional expiry (e.g. 30/90/365 days or custom) to CreateGalleryModal and a new 'Gallery settings' edit modal; accept expires_at in PUT /galleries/:id; enforce in loadPublishedGallery by returning 410/{expired:true} when CURRENT_TIMESTAMP > expires_at; and render an 'expired — ask your photographer for a new link' state in g/[token] mirroring d/[token]. Keep it additive and backward-compatible (NULL = never expires).

**`galleries-2` — No way to edit a gallery after creation (visibility, password, downloads, title)**  
HIGH · M effort · high impact · ux · standard: Edit/Settings for created entities · _Galleries, Albums & Portfolio (client delivery)_  
- **Evidence:** CreateGalleryModal (studio/[id]/page.js:74-114) is the only place visibility/password/download_policy/title are set. The gallery cards (page.js:397-447) expose Publish, Copy link, Export ZIP, Request selections — but no 'Settings'/'Edit' action. The backend PUT /api/media/galleries/:id (media-studio.js:1572) fully supports updating these fields, so the capability exists but is unreachable from the UI.
- **Problem:** A photographer who set the wrong download policy, needs to add/rotate a password, or fix a gallery title must delete and rebuild the gallery (losing the share link, favorites, comments, and proofing). This is a daily-friction gap that makes the product feel half-finished.
- **Recommendation:** Add a 'Settings' (gear) action on each gallery card opening an edit modal that reuses CreateGalleryModal's fields (prefilled) and calls mediaAPI.updateGallery → PUT /galleries/:id. Include the new expiry control here. This also gives Gallery Expiry its natural home.

**`settings-team-3` — No forgot-password / account recovery flow - locked-out users are stranded**  
HIGH · M effort · high impact · ux · _Settings, Team, Onboarding & Knowledge_  
- **Evidence:** login/page.js line 187 contains the comment '{/* placeholder for forgot-password */}' but renders no link. Glob for forgot-password/reset-password routes returns no files. The only password change path is settings PasswordTab (line 1521) which requires the CURRENT password (authAPI.changePassword), useless to someone who forgot it.
- **Problem:** Any email/password user who forgets their password (including invited teammates who set their own) has zero self-service recovery and must contact support - a hard funnel blocker for a multi-seat product.
- **Recommendation:** Add a 'Forgot password?' link in the placeholder slot -> /forgot-password (email entry) -> backend token email -> /reset-password?token. Reuse the existing auth-page styling and the token pattern already proven in accept-invite.

**`platform-3` — PlanWelcomeModal is dead code: built for removed tiers (free/starter/growth) AND never mounted**  
HIGH · M effort · high impact · consistency · standard: Feature Discovery / Drafts-Onboarding · _Platform Plumbing (entitlements, pricing, plan UX, notifications, confirm, AI copilots, trash, WhatsApp, API client)_  
- **Evidence:** components/PlanWelcomeModal.js:21 PLAN_CONFIG has only `free`, `starter`, `growth`. The current catalog (entitlements.js:94-116) is creator/studio/studio_plus/enterprise. Effect at PlanWelcomeModal.js:124 `if (tier==='enterprise' || !PLAN_CONFIG[tier]) return;` — every real tier hits !PLAN_CONFIG[tier] and bails. Grep shows the component is imported NOWHERE (only self-references).
- **Problem:** The entire onboarding "here is what you unlocked / what is still locked" moment never fires for any real user. This is exactly the feature-discovery / first-run polish the maturity push wants, and it is silently inert.
- **Recommendation:** Rewrite PLAN_CONFIG keyed by creator/studio/studio_plus/enterprise (reuse the included/locked copy from getAllPlans features), then mount <PlanWelcomeModal/> inside providers.js next to UsageWarnings so it actually renders on first login.

**`platform-4` — PlanBanner and PlanChip target dead tiers and are never imported — no persistent plan visibility in-nav**  
HIGH · M effort · high impact · discoverability · standard: Plan Enforcement / Workspace Branding · _Platform Plumbing (entitlements, pricing, plan UX, notifications, confirm, AI copilots, trash, WhatsApp, API client)_  
- **Evidence:** PlanLock.js:112 `if (!plan || plan==='growth' || plan==='enterprise') return null;` and PlanLock.js:126 className uses `plan==='free'`/`'starter'`; PlanChip (PlanLock.js:157-180) branches on free/starter/growth/enterprise. None of creator/studio/studio_plus match, so PlanBanner would mis-style and PlanChip shows a generic chip. Grep: neither PlanBanner nor PlanChip is imported anywhere.
- **Problem:** There is no persistent, always-visible plan/usage chip in the NavBar for paying users — only the transient, dismissable UsageWarnings strip. Upgrade nudges (the revenue + cohesion surface) are absent, and the components that would provide them are written for tiers that no longer exist.
- **Recommendation:** Update tone/branch logic to creator/studio/studio_plus/enterprise, then render <PlanChip/> in the NavBar top-right next to the notification bell (data already in usePlan()). Drop the legacy free/starter CSS branches.

**`comms-3` — Mentions inbox has a full API but zero UI**  
HIGH · M effort · high impact · discoverability · _Communications (Team Chat + Project Rooms + Huddles/Calls + Floating WhatsApp Chat)_  
- **Evidence:** Backend GET /api/comms/mentions returns the 50 latest mentions with channel + body (comms.js:253-264) and commsAPI.mentions exists (api.js:283). The unread endpoint also returns a `mentions` count (comms.js:246). chat/page.js never calls commsAPI.mentions and renders no mentions view; there is no @ / activity tab in the sidebar.
- **Problem:** The headline feature of modern chat — 'what mentioned me?' — is built and unreachable. Users have no aggregated view of where they were tagged.
- **Recommendation:** Add an 'Mentions & reactions' item at the top of the sidebar (above Channels) that opens a panel backed by commsAPI.mentions, with the count from commsAPI.unread().mentions shown as a badge. Clicking a row routes to /chat?channel= and marks read (markRead already clears mentions).

**`comms-6` — Active call exists in backend but there's no 'Join call in progress' affordance**  
HIGH · M effort · high impact · ux · _Communications (Team Chat + Project Rooms + Huddles/Calls + Floating WhatsApp Chat)_  
- **Evidence:** Backend GET /api/comms/channels/:id/active-call returns the live (not-ended) call for a channel (comms.js:514-520) and commsAPI.activeCall is wired (api.js:301). The chat header only ever renders 'Huddle' and 'Video call' START buttons (page.js:931-944); nothing calls activeCall, and startHuddle always creates/joins via callStart. The HuddleModal is never opened by an inbound call_invite SSE frame either.
- **Problem:** If A starts a call, B (already in /chat) sees no in-app banner to join — call_invite is broadcast but the chat page's SSE switch (page.js:427-463) has no case for call_invite/call_event. B can only 'start' a call, which luckily reuses the live session, but the UX gives no indication a call is happening. Teammates effectively can't join calls.
- **Recommendation:** In the chat SSE handler add cases for call_invite/call_event/call_missed to (a) show a ringing toast with Accept→opens HuddleModal for that room, and (b) render a persistent 'Call in progress — Join' bar in the channel header when activeCall is set. On channel select, call commsAPI.activeCall to seed that state.

**`comms-9` — RoomPanel is a feature dead-end disconnected from the main comms surface**  
MED · M effort · high impact · integration · _Communications (Team Chat + Project Rooms + Huddles/Calls + Floating WhatsApp Chat)_  
- **Evidence:** RoomPanel renders a minimal composer + plain-text bubbles (RoomPanel.js:82-104) with no threads, reactions, pins, edit, mentions, or read state, and no link to open the same channel in /chat. The channel it talks to IS a real chat_channels row (comms.js:548-553) fully usable in /chat, but nothing surfaces that. Body is rendered as raw text (line 87) while /chat renders sanitized HTML via FormattedText — so the same message looks different in the two places.
- **Problem:** Two render paths for the same data (raw text vs FormattedText) and a capability cliff between the embedded room and /chat break the 'one OS' feel. Users can't escalate an entity discussion into the full chat, and formatted messages appear broken inside rooms.
- **Recommendation:** Add an 'Open in Team chat ↗' link in RoomPanel header that routes to /chat?channel=<room channel id> (depends on the ?channel= fix), and reuse the FormattedText renderer so message rendering is identical. Longer term, render RoomPanel via a shared MessageBubble to inherit reactions/threads.

**`invoices-store-2` — Overdue status is never computed — stat card and filter are permanently dead**  
HIGH · M effort · medium impact · data-integrity · _Invoices, Payments & Store_  
- **Evidence:** No backend code ever writes status='overdue' (grep for overdue in backend returns nothing). parseInvoice (server.js:2174-2178) only re-hydrates items. invoices/page.js:439 renders an 'Overdue' stat counting i.status==='overdue' and 461 renders an 'Overdue' filter chip.
- **Problem:** due_date is captured but never acted on. The Overdue KPI always reads 0 and the Overdue filter always returns empty, regardless of how many invoices are past due. This is a visible broken promise on the main invoices dashboard and removes the single most useful AR signal for a studio.
- **Recommendation:** Compute overdue derived status in parseInvoice (status==='pending' && due_date < today => 'overdue') so it is consistent without a writer, OR add a lightweight daily sweep that flips pending+past-due to overdue. Prefer the derived approach to stay additive and avoid a cron dependency.

**`invoices-store-5` — Invoices cannot be edited after creation**  
HIGH · M effort · medium impact · ux · _Invoices, Payments & Store_  
- **Evidence:** InvoiceViewModal (invoices/page.js:114-258) exposes Print, Send, Payment link, Mark Paid, Delete — but no Edit. The list row click only opens the view modal (498). Backend PUT /api/invoices/:id (2229) fully supports editing items/totals/dates.
- **Problem:** A typo in an amount, line item, or due date can only be fixed by deleting and recreating the invoice from the lead — which also burns an invoice number (counter only increments, 2203-2208), leaving gaps. The edit capability exists in the API but is unreachable from the UI.
- **Recommendation:** Add an Edit action in the view modal that reuses the InvoiceModal form pre-filled from the invoice and calls invoicesAPI.update. Gate editing to draft/pending (not paid) to protect AR integrity.

**`studio-library-3` — Gallery Expiry is a phantom feature: expires_at column exists but is never set, read, or exposed**  
MED · L effort · medium impact · standards · _Media Studio — Library, Cull & AI_  
- **Evidence:** media-studio.js:1262 declares `expires_at TIMESTAMP` on ms_galleries. Publish (media-studio.js:1630-1662) never sets it. loadPublishedGallery (media-studio.js:2584 `SELECT * ... WHERE share_token=? AND status='published'`) does not check expiry; the portal handler (:2588) serves regardless of date. No web UI references expiry (grep across studio web = 0 hits). The CreateGalleryModal and gallery PUT route expose only visibility/password/download policy.
- **Problem:** A standard client-gallery feature (and a stated planned feature) is half-scaffolded — the schema implies it works, but a photographer cannot set an expiry, and an 'expired' gallery would stay publicly accessible forever. This is both a missing-feature gap and a data/expectation-integrity risk.
- **Recommendation:** Build the loop end-to-end: add an optional expiry picker to CreateGalleryModal + gallery settings; set expires_at on publish; in loadPublishedGallery return 410/'gallery expired' when expires_at < now; add a 'Expired' badge in the galleries list. Add a daily sweep (mirror purgeExpiredTrash) only if you want auto-unpublish. Plan-gate if desired via entitlements.

**`command-center-7` — System Health (operational/infra) dashboard is unbuilt; 'Health' page is customer-scoring only**  
MED · L effort · medium impact · integration · _Command Center (internal platform control plane — /control + /api/cc/*)_  
- **Evidence:** COMMAND-CENTER-SPEC.md:227 lists system/page.js (Section 20 System Health, pm2/os, CPU/RAM/disk) and :268/:357 mark it partial/unbuilt. No system/ directory exists under app/control and no /cc/system* endpoint exists in command-center.js or any cc-*.js. The existing /control/health page is purely customer churn/health scoring.
- **Problem:** The control plane has no operational health view (uptime, process/memory, error rate, AI failure spikes, queue/SSE connection counts). For a 'run the whole platform' surface this is the notable capability gap, and the shared 'Health' label risks confusion between customer-health and system-health.
- **Recommendation:** Add a System Health page backed by a /cc/system endpoint using process.uptime()/os/heap plus cheap DB-derived signals (recent ai_usage failure rate from the AI ledger, SSE client count from ccSse, suspended/error counts). Keep it additive; rename the nav entry to 'Customer Health' (label already says this) to disambiguate.

**`settings-team-16` — Plan & Billing upgrade CTAs are a dead-end 'contact us' toast (no self-serve checkout)**  
LOW · L effort · medium impact · ux · _Settings, Team, Onboarding & Knowledge_  
- **Evidence:** settings/page.js PlanBillingTab upgrade onClick (lines 1382-1385): enterprise -> mailto, all others -> showToast('Contact us to switch... Self-serve checkout is coming soon.'). No payment integration is invoked.
- **Problem:** Every upgrade CTA across the app funnels here, but the action is a dead-end toast, so the conversion path the locked overlays promise cannot complete in-product (consistent with the deferred-Stripe note in memory; flagged as a cohesion gap, not a net-new feature).
- **Recommendation:** Without building payments now, make the dead-end honest and consistent: replace the toast with a 'Request upgrade' modal that captures intent + emails sales, and ensure all upgrade entry points reach a working /settings?tab=plan (see the billing-id bug).

**`command-center-12` — Founder Inbox computed signals can't be snoozed/acknowledged; only persisted items dismiss**  
LOW · S effort · low impact · ux · _Command Center (internal platform control plane — /control + /api/cc/*)_  
- **Evidence:** inbox/page.js:55 shows a dismiss (X) button only when it.persisted is true; computed signals (churn_risk, expansion, ai_cost, suspended) from command-center.js:774-786 have no it.id and so cannot be acted on — they only disappear when the underlying condition clears.
- **Problem:** A founder who has reviewed a churn-risk signal can't acknowledge/snooze it; it stays in the inbox until the score changes, so the inbox never reaches a clean 'reviewed' state and high-volume signals create fatigue.
- **Recommendation:** Add a lightweight snooze/ack for computed signals (persist a dismissal keyed by kind+workspace_id with a TTL) so reviewed items hide for a window, while still re-surfacing if the condition worsens.

### Notifications, SSE & real-time inconsistency

**`crm-leads-5` — Unified Timeline tab loads empty and requires a manual 'Refresh Timeline' click**  
HIGH · S effort · high impact · ux · _CRM — Leads & Pipeline_  
- **Evidence:** leads/[id]/page.js:2661-2666 shows 'Unified Timeline / Click Refresh Timeline to load all activity' and only fetches on the button click at line 2656. The backend GET /api/leads/:leadId/timeline already assembles activity+messages+notes+history in one query (server.js:4791-4818) and fetchAll() does not call it.
- **Problem:** The tab that is supposed to be the at-a-glance activity feed reads as broken/empty on arrival. It fails the 'Where am I / what happened' test and is a glaring polish gap for a maturity-focused release.
- **Recommendation:** Call timelineAPI.get(leadId) inside the existing `if (activeTab === 'timeline')` lazy-load effect (the same pattern already used for the 'vertical' tab at lines 1170-1173), so the timeline populates automatically the first time the tab is opened. Keep the manual Refresh button as a secondary action.

**`comms-1` — Notification deep-links to /chat?channel=X are ignored — calls & mentions open the wrong channel**  
HIGH · S effort · high impact · integration · _Communications (Team Chat + Project Rooms + Huddles/Calls + Floating WhatsApp Chat)_  
- **Evidence:** comms.js emits notify/push URLs of `/chat?channel=${channelId}` (lines 445, 489) and broadcasts call_invite/chat_mention SSE frames, but chat/page.js never reads a query param: loadChannels() always selects chs[0] (lines 487-490) and there is no useSearchParams / location.search handling anywhere in the file.
- **Problem:** Clicking an incoming-call, missed-call, or (future) mention notification lands the user on the default channel, not the channel the event happened in. The entire ringing/missed-call UX dead-ends because there is no way to navigate to the live call from the alert.
- **Recommendation:** In chat/page.js read `?channel=` (Next useSearchParams) on mount and after loadChannels; if present, select that channel (creating a lightweight DM/room descriptor if it isn't in the channels list, as openDm already does). This makes every existing notify() URL functional with no backend change.

**`comms-5` — No comms unread / mention badge in the global nav**  
HIGH · S effort · high impact · discoverability · _Communications (Team Chat + Project Rooms + Huddles/Calls + Floating WhatsApp Chat)_  
- **Evidence:** NavBar setNotifBadge sums derivedCount (today's leads + urgent reminders) + feedUnread (notifications table) (NavBar.js:314-318). It never calls commsAPI.unread(), so per-channel unread counts and the mentions count never surface on the Inbox/Team nav item. The data exists and is already used inside /chat (page.js:475).
- **Problem:** A user on any other screen has no signal that they have unread team messages or mentions. The only way to know is to open /chat. Real-time team chat without an ambient unread badge defeats its purpose.
- **Recommendation:** Have NavBar (or a shared store) poll/subscribe commsAPI.unread() and render a red dot/count on the Team nav item (sum of unread + a stronger style when mentions>0). Reuse the existing SSE connection by listening for chat_message/chat_mention frames to keep it live.

**`booking-3` — Public success screen claims a confirmation was sent even when none was**  
HIGH · M effort · high impact · integration · _Booking & Scheduling_  
- **Evidence:** book/[slug]/page.js:45 unconditionally renders 'A confirmation has been sent. See you then.' But confirmation only sends when lead.customer_phone exists: booking.js:166 guards `if (lead.customer_phone)` and bookingSend returns {skipped:true} with no phone (server.js:5528-5532). The public form explicitly accepts email-only bookings (phone placeholder says 'for WhatsApp confirmation', email is a separate field; backend allows name + (phone||email), booking.js:141).
- **Problem:** A client who books with email only gets a false promise and zero confirmation — no email is ever sent. Reschedule (booking.js:194) and cancel (booking.js:207) notifications are likewise phone-only, so email clients are silently dropped from every touchpoint. This erodes trust at the most important moment (the confirmation).
- **Recommendation:** Add an email fallback: when no phone is present but email is, send the confirmation/reschedule/cancel via the existing email-sending capability (email_sending is a resolved feature in entitlements). At minimum, make the success copy conditional — only show 'confirmation sent' when a channel actually fired (have the POST response return a `confirmation_channel` and branch on it).

**`comms-2` — @mentions and thread-replies never reach the global notification feed/bell**  
HIGH · M effort · high impact · integration · _Communications (Team Chat + Project Rooms + Huddles/Calls + Floating WhatsApp Chat)_  
- **Evidence:** afterMessage notifies mentions and thread-roots only via broadcastToUser(...) + sendPushToUser(...) (comms.js:168-169, 178-179). It never calls notify(), which is the only path that INSERTs into the notifications table the NavBar bell reads (server.js notify() at 890; NavBar reads notificationsAPI.get() at 309). Calls, by contrast, do call notify() (445, 489).
- **Problem:** If a user is offline (no live SSE) when @mentioned or replied to, the mention is invisible everywhere except the /comms/mentions inbox — which has no UI. Mentions are the single most important 'come back' signal in team chat and they silently disappear.
- **Recommendation:** In afterMessage, additionally call notify(workspaceId, { type:'mention', title:`${sender} mentioned you`, body, url:`/chat?channel=${channel_id}`, icon:'💬', userId:uid }) (guarded by isDnd) for each mention and for the thread-root author. Reuse the same DND guard already present. Pairs naturally with the ?channel= fix above.

**`studio-video-5` — Export modal polls instead of consuming the already-emitted SSE event**  
MED · S effort · medium impact · performance · standard: Performance / SSE pattern consistency · _Media Studio — Video & Reel_  
- **Evidence:** The worker already broadcasts ms_video_export progress/done frames (media-worker.js:790-808). ExportModal ignores them and runs setInterval(getVideoExport, 1500) until done/failed (video/[timelineId]/page.js:901-907). No web file consumes ms_video_export (grep found zero). The codebase's documented SSE pattern is es.onmessage + switch on data.type (used in dashboard/leads/chat).
- **Problem:** Redundant 1.5s polling of a per-export endpoint for the full render duration (potentially minutes for 4K), duplicating data already pushed over the workspace SSE stream. Inconsistent with the rest of the app's realtime pattern.
- **Recommendation:** Subscribe to the workspace SSE stream and switch on data.type==='ms_video_export' (matching exportId) to drive progress/done, per the SSE memo. Keep a single fallback poll only if SSE is unavailable.

**`booking-2` — Admin booking list never live-updates despite SSE being broadcast**  
MED · S effort · medium impact · performance · _Booking & Scheduling_  
- **Evidence:** booking.js:167 broadcasts broadcastToWorkspace(ws,'booking_created',...) on every create, and reschedule/cancel re-broadcast 'booking_created' (booking.js:195/208). app/bookings/page.js loads bookingAPI.list() once in a mount useEffect (line 22) and never subscribes to the SSE stream. MEMORY notes the app's SSE pattern is es.onmessage + switch on data.type.
